FILE: ORVA/AI_COLLABORATION_CONTEXT.md
================================================================================
# ORVA — authoritative handoff context for external coding models

## Product and owner/staff requirements

- Product name: **ORVA**. Publisher/parent brand: **Rindle Works**. Working tagline/category: Pharmacy Billing & Inventory.
- ORVA is intended to become a reusable commercial Windows product for multiple independent medical shops. Every installation is configured for one shop and has its own local database and shop data. Do not create a multi-tenant shared customer database.
- Current V1 is **Windows-only**. The client explicitly cancelled the Android application and device synchronisation. Do not add Android, Flutter, cloud business-data hosting, LAN sync, device pairing, or sync event queues.
- Target laptop is Windows 10. Current engineering baseline: C# 7.3, WPF, .NET Framework 4.8.1, System.Data.SQLite.Core 1.0.119, SQLite. This compatibility choice must be tested on the client's exact Windows 10 edition/build before release.
- Database and backups are deliberately **unencrypted**. Do not add SQLCipher, encryption keys, TLS/certificate/key-management work, or encrypted archives. Ordinary operating-system file permissions and normal backups are sufficient for the agreed threat model.
- The actual owner and staff must be able to use ORVA with little technical knowledge. This is a core requirement: minimise typing, choices, setup prompts and waiting; use professional but clear English and Bengali; icons must have text; readable controls; natural error messages; no technical jargon in staff-facing UI. Never add a workflow merely because it is technically possible.
- No routine login, no staff accounts, no roles and no permissions. The product subscription account is a separate licensing concern; do not add employee access control.

## Brand and visual direction

- Warm paper `#F3EADA`; deep charcoal teal `#172C30`; ink `#1A1712`; vermilion `#FF4B1F`.
- No warm amber and no dark mode.
- Print-inspired editorial style with architectural rules, restrained two-ink overprint, subtle paper grain only on decorative surfaces, and the scalloped/perforated vermilion ticket edge as a selective signature motif.
- Functional medicine grids, invoice tables and entry forms must remain clear, solid and readable. Animations must never delay a transaction.

## Functional decisions already made

- Medicine catalogue plus batch-wise inventory. Each batch has its own batch number, expiry month, MRP, purchase cost and quantity.
- Stock unit is defined per medicine. Do not invent unit conversion/pack-size conversion logic unless specifically requested.
- Automatic FEFO batch allocation is desired for sales. Expired, quarantined, blocked or otherwise non-sellable stock must not be sold.
- Every committed business operation has an automatic UTC timestamp and local-display timestamp. Staff never type timestamps.
- Every stock change must be represented by a stock movement record. The database is the source of truth.
- Medicine search and recognised existing barcodes are supported; Windows uses USB barcode scanners with manual search fallback. Do not add staff-facing barcode registration or barcode-generation screens.
- Payment methods: CASH, UPI, DEBIT_CARD, CREDIT_CARD. Split payment is supported. Do not add generic "Other". For a fully paid invoice, tender amounts must exactly equal the payable amount.
- Returns are linked to original invoices. Returned medicine goes into quarantine by default; it must not automatically become sellable. Damaged/expired items cannot return to sellable stock.
- Invoice paper formats: 58 mm, 80 mm, A4 and A5.
- Customer name, phone, address and GSTIN are optional for ordinary billing; request legally necessary data only in applicable cases.
- Purchase flow should be simple. Real-world supplier/document records must be retained accurately. Do not fabricate invoices or claim input tax credit eligibility for undocumented purchases. Tax/legal treatment requires CA/pharmacist review.
- ITR support means preserving and exporting accurate records; do not claim to calculate/file the whole ITR.
- No unnecessary sales/profit dashboard. Preserve the underlying invoice, payment, purchase, returns, stock movement and audit records.

## Portable data files

The application uses a shop-named folder under the owner's Documents/data location.

1. `StockData.json`: generated automatically from the authoritative database. It is a complete snapshot of current medicine catalogue and batch inventory, not a full backup. It may be imported to replace current inventory only after validation, preview, pre-import backup and explicit confirmation. Never delete invoices, payment, purchase, return, or audit history when importing stock. Keep historical medicine/batch rows when foreign-key history depends on them; represent quantity differences using stock adjustments/movements.
2. `ShopBackup.shopbackup`: separate one-file complete shop backup containing all business records and required metadata. Restore only after validation, preview, safety backup and confirmation.

Both files are unencrypted. Snapshot writing currently uses a temporary file and replacement. Do not make these files the live database or silently overwrite historical records.

## Subscription direction (not part of either coding assignment)

- Commercial product is intended to offer monthly, quarterly, annual and one-time licence options.
- Manual UPI payment review through WhatsApp is planned.
- A public GitHub repository containing only a minimal `licenses.csv` subscription registry was accepted for V1 exploration. It is only licensing data, never business data. No GitHub credentials/tokens are embedded in the distributed app.
- Do not implement subscription UI or GitHub licence lookup in the billing/backup modules unless explicitly asked.

## Current source tree status

This source package contains an early permanent WPF application foundation, not a completed product. Existing files include:
- WPF startup and main shell.
- Shop profile setup/repository.
- SQLite initial schema and migration runner.
- Inventory repository and stock-entry service.
- Add Stock and Inventory screens.
- `StockSnapshotWriter` that writes `StockData.json` format `ORVA_STOCK_DATA`, `FormatVersion = 1`.
- English/Bengali resource dictionaries and local language preference.

Not yet implemented: complete billing/invoice issuance, payments UI, sales return workflow, complete shop backup/restore, stock import, full reports, printer integration, USB barcode integration, subscription module and production installer validation.

The project has **not been compiled/tested on Windows in this environment**. Do not claim a successful Windows build or hardware test unless you actually perform it on a suitable Windows machine.

## Engineering constraints

- Preserve C# 7.3 and .NET Framework 4.8.1 compatibility. Do not use records, nullable reference types, file-scoped namespaces, `using var`, top-level statements, or APIs unavailable to .NET Framework 4.8.1.
- Use the existing pinned `System.Data.SQLite.Core` 1.0.119 package unless a clear compatibility issue is demonstrated.
- Money is stored as integer paise (`long`/SQLite INTEGER); quantities are stored as thousandths (`quantity_milli`, SQLite INTEGER). Never use floating-point for money or stock arithmetic.
- Use parameterised SQL. Use transactions for multi-record business operations. Foreign keys are enabled. SQLite WAL and `synchronous=FULL` are configured in `SqliteDatabase`.
- Keep UI, domain/business logic and database infrastructure separated.
- Do not refactor unrelated files. Do not change migration 001 destructively. Add a new migration only when necessary and explain it.
- Do not hardcode medicine tax rates or silently assume a default 0% means exempt. Do not make legal conclusions. Any tax calculation inputs/modes must be explicit and configurable; flag unresolved CA validation clearly.
- No fake data, no fabricated test results, no broad rewrite. Return actual changed source files and a precise change report.

## Current AI development ownership (billing implementation)

- `Domain/Billing/BillingContracts.cs` is the frozen public boundary between billing backend and WPF billing UI.
- Gemini owns implementation of `IBillingService`, billing calculators, FEFO allocation, SQLite transaction handling, billing migrations and backend tests.
- Qwen owns the native WPF BillingView, view model, dynamic invoice cart and on-screen 58mm/80mm/A4/A5 preview consuming the frozen contract.
- ChatGPT owns AppServices/MainWindow integration, project/resource wiring, merge review, build fixes and final regression verification.
- The separate Qwen portability assignment (StockData import and ShopBackup restore) is deferred until billing backend/UI are integrated.
- Do not replace WPF with a web UI. Do not let either external model edit the other model's owned files or the frozen billing contract.

================================================================================
FILE: ORVA/docs/BILLING_UI_BACKEND_CONTRACT_v1.md
================================================================================
# ORVA Billing UI ↔ Backend Contract v1.0

**Status:** Frozen interface boundary for the Gemini backend task and Qwen WPF UI task.

**Project:** ORVA by Rindle Works — native Windows WPF, C# 7.3, .NET Framework 4.8.1, SQLite.

## 1. Why this contract exists

Gemini implements the billing domain and persistence. Qwen implements the WPF billing experience and live invoice preview. Both must use the same public contract so the work can be integrated without rewriting either module.

The C# contract file included with the source is authoritative:

`src/Orva.Desktop/Domain/Billing/BillingContracts.cs`

Do not independently redefine DTOs, rename members, change enum values, or create a second competing billing API. If a genuine blocker requires a change, document the proposed change and why before implementing it.

## 2. Public interface

```csharp
public interface IBillingService
{
    IList<MedicineLookupResult> SearchMedicines(string query, int maximumResults);
    InvoicePreviewResult PreviewInvoice(InvoiceDraft draft);
    InvoiceResult FinalizeInvoice(InvoiceDraft draft);
}
```

### SearchMedicines

- Returns active catalogue items relevant to the entered medicine text or a recognised barcode.
- Search should be fast and limited to `maximumResults`.
- A medicine can be returned with `CanSell = false` when it currently has no eligible stock, so the UI can explain availability without hiding the medicine entirely.
- `AvailableQuantityMilli` is the sum of eligible sellable quantities across batches, not a batch quantity.
- FEFO batch selection is not exposed to the UI.
- The current schema has `barcode_mapping`; barcode lookup may be integrated into this method. Do not add a barcode registration workflow.

### PreviewInvoice

- Must not write to the database, allocate an invoice number, reserve stock, or deduct stock.
- Re-reads current database state and uses the exact same calculation/allocation rules as finalisation.
- Returns line totals, tax totals, grand total, tender total, remaining amount and any blocking issues.
- If a requested quantity must be fulfilled from multiple FEFO batches, return those allocations in `BatchAllocations` for the paper preview. The staff must not choose or edit those allocations.
- While the user is still entering payment, `AmountRemainingPaise` may be positive and `CanFinalize` false. This is a normal incomplete draft, not a crash.
- `DiscountPaise` remains zero for V1; discount is not exposed as a UI feature.
- Tax configuration is resolved from saved shop/CA-approved configuration. Never ask ordinary staff to choose intra-state/inter-state, tax-inclusive/exclusive, GST rates or HSN codes during routine billing. If required configuration is missing, return a clear blocking issue such as “Tax setup needs to be completed in Settings. Please contact support.”

### FinalizeInvoice

- Revalidates the draft using fresh database reads. A preview result is not a reservation and must not be trusted as current stock.
- Creates invoice, invoice lines, payment rows, stock movements, stock deductions, invoice sequence and audit entry in one SQLite transaction.
- Payment tender total must exactly equal the final payable amount in paise for the agreed fully-paid workflow.
- Returns `Succeeded = true` only after the transaction commits.
- A post-commit StockData.json refresh failure must be a warning, not a false invoice failure.
- Does not print. Printing is a separate integration concern.

## 3. Input ownership

`InvoiceDraft` contains only:
- Medicine IDs and requested `QuantityMilli` for each medicine line.
- Optional customer name, phone, address and GSTIN.
- Zero or more payment tenders with method, exact paise amount and optional reference text.

The UI must not send authoritative prices, batch IDs, expiry dates, GST rates, HSN codes, stock quantity, tax mode, or batch status. The backend reads those from the database/configuration and returns a preview.

Payment enum values are exactly:
- `Cash`
- `Upi`
- `DebitCard`
- `CreditCard`

There is no generic “Other” tender and no unapproved discount field.

## 4. Integer units

- Money: integer paise (`long`). Convert to decimal rupees only for display formatting.
- Quantity: integer thousandths (`QuantityMilli`). Convert to a readable quantity string only for display.
- Never calculate money or stock using `double` or `float` in either layer.
- The WPF UI displays backend-returned totals. It must not duplicate tax, FEFO or rounding calculations.

## 5. Staff-facing UI rules

- Search-first; keyboard and USB keyboard-wedge barcode scanner friendly.
- Staff never select batches.
- Medicine names, unit, available quantity and price/line total are clearly visible.
- Use clear messages (“Only 3 strips are available”), not exception text or database terms.
- Do not require customer details for ordinary sales.
- Do not expose discounts, GST configuration or HSN configuration as routine billing controls.
- Split payments supported using only the four approved methods.
- Keep “Generate Invoice” disabled until the service says `CanFinalize`.
- On finalisation success, clear the draft only after displaying the committed invoice number. Show immediate next actions; animation must never delay commit.

## 6. Paper preview

Qwen may implement an on-screen live paper preview for:
- 58 mm thermal receipt
- 80 mm thermal receipt
- A4
- A5

This is only an on-screen preview. Do not implement physical printer integration in this assignment. Use backend preview data for all monetary and batch-allocation values. Preview format changes must not alter invoice totals.

## 7. Integration ownership

- Gemini owns backend implementation of `IBillingService` and database transactions.
- Qwen owns new WPF billing view/view-model/preview controls using `IBillingService`.
- ChatGPT owns integration: `AppServices`, dependency wiring, MainWindow navigation, shared project references, build fixes and final regression verification.

To prevent conflicts:
- Gemini must not edit WPF XAML, MainWindow or AppServices.
- Qwen must not edit SQL schema, repositories, billing calculations or AppServices/MainWindow.
- Neither model may modify `BillingContracts.cs` without a written proposal.

## 8. Error handling

- Expected user-correctable conditions are returned as `BillingIssue` values, not raw exceptions.
- Unexpected infrastructure failures may throw; the UI catches them, logs technical details locally, and shows a short actionable message.
- Never show a stack trace, SQL query or exception type to staff.

FILE: ORVA/docs/INTEGRATION_STAGE_1_REVIEW.md
================================================================================
# ORVA Stage 1 Integration Patch — Review Notes

This is an integration overlay for the current ORVA baseline. It is not a complete release or a verified Windows build.

## Included

- Register Gemini's `IBillingService` implementation in `AppServices`.
- Construct `BillingService` with the existing local SQLite database and `StockSnapshotWriter`.
- Embed migration `002_BillingTaxConfig.sql` in the WPF project.
- Open the application directly on New Billing after first-run shop setup.
- Connect BillingView to the service through constructor injection.
- Map the existing ShopProfile to the paper preview shop header.
- Add WPF support classes referenced by Qwen's billing screen and paper preview.
- Preserve C# 7.3 / .NET Framework 4.8.1 compatibility.

## Important source files to bring in from the existing Dropbox batches

Copy the reviewed files from these workspace locations into the paths stated inside each batch:

- `02-GEMINI-BACKEND/ORVA.zip`: the Billing namespace, BillingRepository, BillingService, calculator, FEFO allocator, migration 002 and tests.
- `03-QWEN-WPF-UI/qwen batch 2r`: BillingViewModel.cs.
- `03-QWEN-WPF-UI/qwen batch 3`: BillingView.xaml and BillingView.xaml.cs.
- `03-QWEN-WPF-UI/qwen batch 4`: invoice preview control, preview window and English/Bengali resource additions.

Do not use the superseded Qwen Batch 2.

## Required follow-up fixes before the first Windows build

1. **Busy-state edit lock.** In BillingViewModel, NewBillCommand currently has no busy-state CanExecute condition, and ClearDraft currently only checks shutdown. Add `_isBusy` guards. Set `CanEdit=false` on cart/tender row view models while `IsBusy=true`, and restore it when `IsBusy=false`. Search, customer, cart quantity and tender inputs must not be writable during invoice finalisation. The invoice must be finalised from the exact draft snapshot created before the service call.
2. **Clear Bill click handler.** Add an immediate `if (ViewModel.IsBusy) return;` guard to `ClearBill_Click`.
3. **Search result keyboard routing.** Restrict Delete to the cart when focus is inside the cart, not when focus is in search results or other controls.
4. **PrintRequested event.** Subscribe only after a proper physical print service has been implemented. Do not show a successful print state until the host printer confirms the job was submitted.
5. **Mixed-batch page preview.** On A4/A5, show the unit price for every batch allocation. The current allocation template has batch, expiry and quantity but omits each allocation's rate.
6. **Signature scallop.** Qwen's current dashed/dotted line is not the agreed physical scallop-cutout edge. Replace it with a true repeated semicircle geometry and vermilion underlay when refining the preview.
7. **Typography.** Bundle and apply the agreed Noto Serif / Noto Sans / Noto Serif Bengali / Noto Sans Bengali / IBM Plex Mono font families. Do not rely only on system-installed fonts.
8. **Tax configuration UI.** Gemini's service correctly blocks billing where shop tax settings or per-medicine GST configuration are missing. This patch does not invent tax rates or tax treatment. Add a separate, explicit Settings workflow and validate it with the shop's CA before release.
9. **Snapshot completeness.** Extend StockData.json in a new compatible format version to include GST-configured state and existing barcode mappings if they are part of the portable medicine catalogue. Keep import history preserving.
10. **Schema migration safety.** Verify migration 002 can be applied once and rolled back/kept atomic with the existing migration runner. Do not modify migration 001.

## Verification status

The current environment does not contain Visual Studio/MSBuild or the .NET Framework 4.8.1 targeting pack. No Windows compilation, runtime, print, scanner, DPI, Bengali glyph or installer test has been claimed. The source files must be built and exercised on Windows 10 before this stage can be considered verified.


## Finalisation safety patch

Run `apply-billing-safety-fixes.ps1` after copying the Gemini and Qwen source files into the ORVA project. It makes guarded, exact text replacements and stops rather than guessing if the source differs. It blocks edits to the draft while `FinalizeInvoice` is running, prevents New Bill/Clear Bill from clearing a draft mid-save, and restricts the Delete shortcut to the cart.

The script is intentionally separate from the basic overlay so source differences cannot cause silent or broad replacements.


The current safety script uses line-ending-tolerant matches and stops on unexpected source differences. It is safe to run again; already-applied changes are detected.

FILE: ORVA/README.md
================================================================================
# ORVA — Pharmacy Billing & Inventory

**A product by Rindle Works** (working brand; legal/trademark clearance pending).

## Current scope

- Reusable commercial Windows desktop application for independent medical shops.
- One local SQLite database per shop installation.
- Windows 10 target; WPF, C# 7.3, .NET Framework 4.8.1 baseline.
- Offline daily billing and inventory; no Android app in current V1, no cloud business-data database and no device synchronisation.
- Ordinary unencrypted database and backups by explicit product decision.
- Invoice formats: 58 mm, 80 mm, A4 and A5.
- USB barcode scanner support and manual search fallback.
- Each shop configures its own profile, stock, print settings and invoice settings.

## Non-negotiable UX requirement

Design every workflow for the actual owner and staff described in the planning conversation: professional wording, minimum typing and decisions, no unnecessary prompts, comfortable readable controls, clear error messages, and no technical setup during routine billing. This must guide engineering and visual decisions equally.

## Current permanent foundation in source

- WPF application shell and theme resources.
- First-run shop profile setup.
- SQLite initial schema and migration runner.
- Shop and inventory repositories.
- Add Stock and Inventory screens.
- Transactional stock receipt logic.
- Automatic `StockData.json` snapshot writer (format `ORVA_STOCK_DATA`, version 1).
- English/Bengali resource dictionaries and persisted local language preference.
- Basic build/installer scaffolding.

## Not yet implemented

- Invoice/billing engine and complete billing screen.
- Payment capture UI.
- Sales return and invoice cancellation workflows.
- Complete `ShopBackup.shopbackup` creation/verification/restore.
- `StockData.json` import/preview/replacement.
- Reports/records exports.
- Printer integration for 58 mm, 80 mm, A4 and A5.
- USB barcode scanner integration.
- Subscription/activation UI and online GitHub licence lookup.
- Production installer validation and full regression suite.

## Portable files

- `StockData.json` is an automatically updated inventory snapshot. It is not the source of truth and is not a complete backup.
- `ShopBackup.shopbackup` is intended to contain the complete business database and metadata for migration/recovery.
- Both are unencrypted. Imports/restores must validate, preview, create a safety copy and require confirmation. Inventory imports must never delete historical invoices, payments, purchases, returns or audit history.

## Development status

The current source is an early permanent application foundation. It has **not yet been compiled and tested on the client's Windows 10 machine**. Do not treat source presence as a successful Windows build. Target verification requires Visual Studio/MSBuild and the .NET Framework 4.8.1 targeting pack on Windows.

See `AI_COLLABORATION_CONTEXT.md` for the authoritative product requirements and coding constraints.

## Billing implementation boundary

The shared backend/UI contract is in `docs/BILLING_UI_BACKEND_CONTRACT_v1.md` and the corresponding C# types are in `src/Orva.Desktop/Domain/Billing/BillingContracts.cs`.

Current AI task ownership:
- Gemini: billing domain/service, money and tax arithmetic, FEFO allocation, SQLite atomicity and backend tests.
- Qwen: native WPF billing interface, dynamic cart, payment UI and live on-screen paper preview.
- ChatGPT: integration, application service registration, main navigation, project wiring and final verification.

The contract is frozen for these parallel tasks. Do not add web technologies or allow UI code to calculate authoritative totals.

&lt;Application x:Class=&quot;Orva.App&quot;
             xmlns=&quot;http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2006/xaml/presentation&quot;

        &lt;SolidColorBrush x:Key=&quot;Paper&quot; Color=&quot;#F3EADA&quot; /&gt;

        &lt;SolidColorBrush x:Key=&quot;Success&quot; Color=&quot;#16845B&quot; /&gt;

        <Style TargetType="TextBlock">
            <Setter Property="Foreground" Value="{StaticResource Ink}" />
            <Setter Property="FontFamily" Value="Segoe UI" />
            <Setter Property="TextOptions.TextFormattingMode" Value="Display" />
        </Style>

        <Style x:Key="DisplayHeading" TargetType="TextBlock">
            <Setter Property="FontFamily" Value="Georgia" />
            <Setter Property="FontSize" Value="29" />
            <Setter Property="FontWeight" Value="Normal" />
            <Setter Property="Foreground" Value="{StaticResource Teal}" />
        </Style>

        <Style x:Key="SectionHeading" TargetType="TextBlock">
            <Setter Property="FontSize" Value="17" />

================================================================================
FILE: ORVA/src/Orva.Desktop/App.xaml.cs
================================================================================
using System;
using System.Windows;
using Orva.Infrastructure;
using Orva.Infrastructure.Database;
using Orva.Infrastructure.Repositories;
using Orva.Services;
using Orva.Services.Billing;
using Orva.Views;

namespace Orva
{
    public partial class App : Application
    {
        public static AppServices Services { get; private set; }

        private void Application_Startup(object sender, StartupEventArgs e)
        {
            try
            {
                AppPaths.EnsureDirectories();

                var database = new SqliteDatabase(AppPaths.DatabasePath);
                new DatabaseMigrator(database).Migrate();

                var shopRepository = new ShopRepository(database);
                var inventoryRepository = new InventoryRepository(database);
                var snapshotWriter = new StockSnapshotWriter(database, shopRepository);
                var stockEntryService = new StockEntryService(inventoryRepository, snapshotWriter);
                var appSettings = new AppSettingsRepository(database);
                var billingService = new BillingService(database, snapshotWriter);

                Services = new AppServices(
                    database,
                    shopRepository,
                    inventoryRepository,
                    stockEntryService,
                    snapshotWriter,
                    appSettings,
                    billingService);

                LocalizationManager.SetLanguage(appSettings.Get("display_language", "en"));

                if (shopRepository.GetProfile() == null)
                {
                    var setup = new ShopSetupWindow(shopRepository);
                    if (setup.ShowDialog() != true)
                    {
                        Shutdown();
                        return;
                    }
                }

                try
                {
                    Services.SnapshotWriter.WriteCurrentSnapshot();
                }
                catch
                {
                    // A snapshot failure must not prevent normal offline billing.
                }

                var main = new MainWindow();
                MainWindow = main;
                main.Show();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                MessageBox.Show(
                    "ORVA could not start because its local data could not be opened.\n\n" + ex.Message +
                    "\n\nYour existing business data has not been intentionally deleted. Please contact support if this continues.",
                    "ORVA — Startup problem",
                    MessageBoxButton.OK,
                    MessageBoxImage.Error);
                Shutdown(-1);
            }
        }
    }
}

================================================================================
FILE: ORVA/src/Orva.Desktop/AppServices.cs
================================================================================
using Orva.Domain.Billing;
using Orva.Infrastructure.Database;
using Orva.Infrastructure.Repositories;
using Orva.Services;

namespace Orva
{
    public sealed class AppServices
    {
        public SqliteDatabase Database { get; private set; }
        public ShopRepository ShopRepository { get; private set; }
        public InventoryRepository InventoryRepository { get; private set; }
        public StockEntryService StockEntryService { get; private set; }
        public StockSnapshotWriter SnapshotWriter { get; private set; }
        public AppSettingsRepository AppSettings { get; private set; }
        public IBillingService BillingService { get; private set; }

        public AppServices(
            SqliteDatabase database,
            ShopRepository shopRepository,
            InventoryRepository inventoryRepository,
            StockEntryService stockEntryService,
            StockSnapshotWriter snapshotWriter,
            AppSettingsRepository appSettings,
            IBillingService billingService)
        {
            Database = database;
            ShopRepository = shopRepository;
            InventoryRepository = inventoryRepository;
            StockEntryService = stockEntryService;
            SnapshotWriter = snapshotWriter;
            AppSettings = appSettings;
            BillingService = billingService;
        }
    }
}

================================================================================
FILE: ORVA/src/Orva.Desktop/Controls/BillingConverters.cs
================================================================================
using System;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Data;
using System.Windows.Media;
using Orva.Domain.Billing;
using Orva.ViewModels;

namespace Orva.Controls
{
    public sealed class BoolToVisibilityConverter : IValueConverter
    {
        public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
        {
            return value is bool && (bool)value ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed;
        }

        public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
        {
            return value is Visibility && (Visibility)value == Visibility.Visible;
        }
    }

    public sealed class InverseBoolConverter : IValueConverter
    {
        public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
        {
            return !(value is bool) || !(bool)value;
        }

        public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
        {
            return !(value is bool) || !(bool)value;
        }
    }

    public sealed class NullToVisibilityConverter : IValueConverter
    {
        public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
        {
            return value == null ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible;
        }

        public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
        {
            throw new NotSupportedException();
        }
    }

    public sealed class StringToVisibilityConverter : IValueConverter
    {
        public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
        {
            return string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value as string) ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible;
        }

        public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
        {
            throw new NotSupportedException();
        }
    }

    public sealed class BillingSeverityToBrushConverter : IValueConverter
    {
        public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
        {
            if (value is BillingIssueSeverity)
            {
                var issue = (BillingIssueSeverity)value;
                if (issue == BillingIssueSeverity.Blocking)
                    return new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(0xC9, 0x36, 0x2B));
                if (issue == BillingIssueSeverity.Warning)
                    return new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(0xFF, 0x4B, 0x1F));
            }
            return new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(0x17, 0x2C, 0x30));
        }

        public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
        {
            throw new NotSupportedException();
        }
    }

    public sealed class PaiseToRupeesConverter : IValueConverter
    {
        public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
        {
            if (value == null) return "—";
            try { return BillingDisplay.FormatRupees(System.Convert.ToInt64(value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)); }
            catch { return "—"; }
        }

        public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
        {
            throw new NotSupportedException();
        }
    }

    public sealed class QuantityMilliToTextConverter : IValueConverter
    {
        public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
        {
            if (value == null) return "0";
            try { return BillingDisplay.FormatQuantity(System.Convert.ToInt64(value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)); }
            catch { return "0"; }
        }

        public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
        {
            throw new NotSupportedException();
        }
    }

    public sealed class ExpiryYyyyMmToTextConverter : IValueConverter
    {
        public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
        {
            if (value == null) return string.Empty;
            int parsed;
            if (!int.TryParse(System.Convert.ToString(value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture), out parsed))
                return string.Empty;

            var year = parsed / 100;
            var month = parsed % 100;
            if (month < 1 || month > 12) return string.Empty;
            return new DateTime(year, month, 1).ToString("MMM yyyy", culture ?? CultureInfo.CurrentCulture);
        }

        public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
        {
            throw new NotSupportedException();
        }
    }
}

================================================================================
FILE: ORVA/src/Orva.Desktop/Controls/BillingPreviewWindow.cs
================================================================================
using System;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Controls.Primitives;
using System.Windows.Data;
using System.Windows.Input;
using System.Windows.Media;
using Orva.ViewModels;

namespace Orva.Controls
{
    /// &lt;summary&gt;
    /// Non-modal owner window that hosts the live paper preview, the paper-format selector
    /// and the zoom slider. It binds to the same BillingViewModel as the billing workspace,
    /// so it updates live and never triggers a separate preview request.
    ///
    /// The toolbar is built in code so that the whole preview feature stays inside the
    /// permitted Controls/ViewModels file areas. Closing this window never affects the draft.
    /// &lt;/summary&gt;
    public sealed class BillingPreviewWindow : Window
    {
        private readonly BillingViewModel _viewModel;

        public BillingPreviewWindow(BillingViewModel viewModel)
        {
            if (viewModel == null) throw new ArgumentNullException("viewModel");
            _viewModel = viewModel;

            Title = BillingText.Get("BillPreviewWindowTitle", "ORVA — Live Invoice Preview");
            Background = ResolveBrush("Paper", Color.FromRgb(0xF3, 0xEA, 0xDA));
            FontFamily = new FontFamily("Segoe UI, Nirmala UI");
            ShowInTaskbar = false;
            ResizeMode = ResizeMode.CanResize;
            WindowStartupLocation = WindowStartupLocation.CenterOwner;

            Width = Math.Min(900, SystemParameters.WorkArea.Width - 60);
            Height = Math.Min(820, SystemParameters.WorkArea.Height - 60);
            MinWidth = 460;
            MinHeight = 420;

            Content = BuildContent();
            PreviewKeyDown += Window_PreviewKeyDown;
        }

        private UIElement BuildContent()
        {
            var root = new DockPanel { LastChildFill = true };

            var toolbar = BuildToolbar();
            DockPanel.SetDock(toolbar, Dock.Top);
            root.Children.Add(toolbar);

            var preview = new InvoicePaperPreviewControl();
            preview.SetBinding(InvoicePaperPreviewControl.PreviewProperty, Bind("CurrentPreview"));
            preview.SetBinding(InvoicePaperPreviewControl.CommittedInvoiceProperty, Bind("CommittedInvoice"));
            preview.SetBinding(InvoicePaperPreviewControl.PaperFormatProperty, Bind("SelectedPaperFormat"));
            preview.SetBinding(InvoicePaperPreviewControl.ShopProperty, Bind("ShopHeader"));
            preview.SetBinding(InvoicePaperPreviewControl.CustomerNameProperty, Bind("CustomerName"));
            preview.SetBinding(InvoicePaperPreviewControl.CustomerPhoneProperty, Bind("CustomerPhone"));
            preview.SetBinding(InvoicePaperPreviewControl.CustomerAddressProperty, Bind("CustomerAddress"));
            preview.SetBinding(InvoicePaperPreviewControl.CustomerGstinProperty, Bind("CustomerGstin"));
            preview.SetBinding(InvoicePaperPreviewControl.TenderLinesProperty, Bind("Tenders"));
            preview.SetBinding(InvoicePaperPreviewControl.ZoomPercentProperty, Bind("PreviewZoomPercent"));

            var scroll = new ScrollViewer
            {
                HorizontalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Auto,
                VerticalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Auto,
                Padding = new Thickness(24),
                Background = ResolveBrush("PaperRecessed", Color.FromRgb(0xE7, 0xDC, 0xC6)),
                Content = preview
            };
            root.Children.Add(scroll);

            return root;
        }

        private UIElement BuildToolbar()
        {
            var grid = new Grid();
            grid.ColumnDefinitions.Add(new ColumnDefinition { Width = GridLength.Auto });
            grid.ColumnDefinitions.Add(new ColumnDefinition { Width = GridLength.Auto });
            grid.ColumnDefinitions.Add(new ColumnDefinition { Width = GridLength.Auto });
            grid.ColumnDefinitions.Add(new ColumnDefinition { Width = new GridLength(1, GridUnitType.Star) });
            grid.ColumnDefinitions.Add(new ColumnDefinition { Width = GridLength.Auto });
            grid.ColumnDefinitions.Add(new ColumnDefinition { Width = GridLength.Auto });
            grid.ColumnDefinitions.Add(new ColumnDefinition { Width = GridLength.Auto });

            var paperLabel = new TextBlock
            {
                Text = BillingText.Get("BillPaperFormatLabel", "Paper"),
                VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center,
                FontWeight = FontWeights.SemiBold,
                FontSize = 12.5
            };
            Grid.SetColumn(paperLabel, 0);
            grid.Children.Add(paperLabel);

            var formatBox = new ComboBox
            {
                MinWidth = 165,
                Margin = new Thickness(10, 0, 0, 0),
                DisplayMemberPath = "DisplayName",
                VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center
            };
            formatBox.SetBinding(ItemsControl.ItemsSourceProperty, Bind("PaperFormats"));
            formatBox.SetBinding(Selector.SelectedItemProperty, BindTwoWay("SelectedPaperFormatOption"));
            Grid.SetColumn(formatBox, 1);
            grid.Children.Add(formatBox);

            var updating = new TextBlock
            {
                Text = BillingText.Get("BillUpdatingTotals", "Updating totals…"),
                Foreground = ResolveBrush("Vermilion", Color.FromRgb(0xFF, 0x4B, 0x1F)),
                FontSize = 11.5,
                FontWeight = FontWeights.SemiBold,
                Margin = new Thickness(14, 0, 0, 0),
                VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center
            };
            updating.SetBinding(VisibilityProperty, new Binding("IsPreviewPending")
            {
                Source = _viewModel,
                Converter = new BoolToVisibilityConverter()
            });
            Grid.SetColumn(updating, 2);
            grid.Children.Add(updating);

            var zoomLabel = new TextBlock
            {
                Text = BillingText.Get("BillZoom", "Zoom"),
                VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center,
                FontWeight = FontWeights.SemiBold,
                FontSize = 12.5,
                Margin = new Thickness(16, 0, 0, 0)
            };
            Grid.SetColumn(zoomLabel, 4);
            grid.Children.Add(zoomLabel);

            var zoomSlider = new Slider
            {
                Minimum = 50,
                Maximum = 250,
                SmallChange = 5,
                LargeChange = 25,
                MinWidth = 170,
                Margin = new Thickness(10, 0, 0, 0),
                VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center,
                IsSnapToTickEnabled = false
            };
            zoomSlider.SetBinding(RangeBase.ValueProperty, BindTwoWay("PreviewZoomPercent"));
            Grid.SetColumn(zoomSlider, 5);
            grid.Children.Add(zoomSlider);

            var zoomText = new TextBlock
            {
                MinWidth = 48,
                Margin = new Thickness(10, 0, 0, 0),
                TextAlignment = TextAlignment.Right,
                VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center,
                FontSize = 12.5
            };
            zoomText.SetBinding(TextBlock.TextProperty, new Binding("PreviewZoomPercent")
            {
                Source = _viewModel,
                StringFormat = "{0:0}%"
            });
            Grid.SetColumn(zoomText, 6);
            grid.Children.Add(zoomText);

            return new Border
            {
                Background = ResolveBrush("PaperRaised", Color.FromRgb(0xFB, 0xF7, 0xEF)),
                BorderBrush = ResolveBrush("Rule", Color.FromRgb(0xD5, 0xC9, 0xB4)),
                BorderThickness = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 1),
                Padding = new Thickness(16, 12, 16, 12),
                Child = grid
            };
        }

        private Binding Bind(string path)
        {
            return new Binding(path) { Source = _viewModel, Mode = BindingMode.OneWay };
        }

        private Binding BindTwoWay(string path)
        {
            return new Binding(path) { Source = _viewModel, Mode = BindingMode.TwoWay };
        }

        private void Window_PreviewKeyDown(object sender, KeyEventArgs e)
        {
            if (e.Key != Key.Escape) return;
            e.Handled = true;
            Close();
        }

        private static Brush ResolveBrush(string key, Color fallback)
        {
            var app = Application.Current;
            var brush = app == null ? null : app.TryFindResource(key) as Brush;
            if (brush != null) return brush;

            var solid = new SolidColorBrush(fallback);
            solid.Freeze();
            return solid;
        }
    }
}

using System;
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.Specialized;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Media;
using Orva.Domain.Billing;
using Orva.ViewModels;

namespace Orva.Controls
{
    /// &lt;summary&gt;
    /// On-screen invoice paper preview for 58 mm, 80 mm, A4 and A5.
    ///
    /// All monetary values, quantities, batch numbers and expiries are bound directly from
    /// InvoicePreviewResult / BatchAllocationPreview. This control performs no pricing, tax,
    /// FEFO or rounding arithmetic. The only double arithmetic here is the visual zoom scale.
    ///
    /// Paper geometry is applied in code rather than through bindings on a Freezable, because
    /// RelativeSource bindings on a LayoutTransform Freezable are not reliable in WPF.
    /// &lt;/summary&gt;
    public partial class InvoicePaperPreviewControl : UserControl
    {
        private readonly List&lt;PaymentTenderViewModel&gt; _watchedTenders = new List&lt;PaymentTenderViewModel&gt;();
        private INotifyCollectionChanged _watchedCollection;

        public InvoicePaperPreviewControl()
        {
            InitializeComponent();

            Loaded += Control_Loaded;
            Unloaded += Control_Unloaded;

            ApplyFormat();
            ApplyZoom();
            UpdatePreviewFlags();
            UpdateShopFlags();
            UpdateCustomerFlags();
            UpdatePaymentFlags();
            UpdateInvoiceIdentity();
        }

        // ---- Input dependency properties ---------------------------------------------

        public static readonly DependencyProperty PreviewProperty = DependencyProperty.Register(
            nameof(Preview), typeof(InvoicePreviewResult), typeof(InvoicePaperPreviewControl),
            new PropertyMetadata(null, OnPreviewChanged));

        public InvoicePreviewResult Preview
        {
            get { return (InvoicePreviewResult)GetValue(PreviewProperty); }
            set { SetValue(PreviewProperty, value); }
        }

        public static readonly DependencyProperty CommittedInvoiceProperty = DependencyProperty.Register(
            nameof(CommittedInvoice), typeof(InvoiceResult), typeof(InvoicePaperPreviewControl),
            new PropertyMetadata(null, OnCommittedChanged));

        public InvoiceResult CommittedInvoice
        {
            get { return (InvoiceResult)GetValue(CommittedInvoiceProperty); }
            set { SetValue(CommittedInvoiceProperty, value); }
        }

        public static readonly DependencyProperty PaperFormatProperty = DependencyProperty.Register(
            nameof(PaperFormat), typeof(InvoicePaperFormat), typeof(InvoicePaperPreviewControl),
            new PropertyMetadata(InvoicePaperFormat.Thermal80, OnPaperFormatChanged));

        public InvoicePaperFormat PaperFormat
        {
            get { return (InvoicePaperFormat)GetValue(PaperFormatProperty); }
            set { SetValue(PaperFormatProperty, value); }
        }

        public static readonly DependencyProperty ShopProperty = DependencyProperty.Register(
            nameof(Shop), typeof(BillingShopHeader), typeof(InvoicePaperPreviewControl),
            new PropertyMetadata(null, OnShopChanged));

        public BillingShopHeader Shop
        {
            get { return (BillingShopHeader)GetValue(ShopProperty); }
            set { SetValue(ShopProperty, value); }
        }

        public static readonly DependencyProperty CustomerNameProperty = DependencyProperty.Register(
            nameof(CustomerName), typeof(string), typeof(InvoicePaperPreviewControl),
            new PropertyMetadata(null, OnCustomerChanged));

        public string CustomerName
        {
            get { return (string)GetValue(CustomerNameProperty); }
            set { SetValue(CustomerNameProperty, value); }
        }

        public static readonly DependencyProperty CustomerPhoneProperty = DependencyProperty.Register(
            nameof(CustomerPhone), typeof(string), typeof(InvoicePaperPreviewControl),
            new PropertyMetadata(null, OnCustomerChanged));

        public string CustomerPhone
        {
            get { return (string)GetValue(CustomerPhoneProperty); }
            set { SetValue(CustomerPhoneProperty, value); }
        }

        public static readonly DependencyProperty CustomerAddressProperty = DependencyProperty.Register(
            nameof(CustomerAddress), typeof(string), typeof(InvoicePaperPreviewControl),
            new PropertyMetadata(null, OnCustomerChanged));

        public string CustomerAddress
        {
            get { return (string)GetValue(CustomerAddressProperty); }
            set { SetValue(CustomerAddressProperty, value); }
        }

        public static readonly DependencyProperty CustomerGstinProperty = DependencyProperty.Register(
            nameof(CustomerGstin), typeof(string), typeof(InvoicePaperPreviewControl),
            new PropertyMetadata(null, OnCustomerChanged));

        public string CustomerGstin
        {
            get { return (string)GetValue(CustomerGstinProperty); }
            set { SetValue(CustomerGstinProperty, value); }
        }

        public static readonly DependencyProperty TenderLinesProperty = DependencyProperty.Register(
            nameof(TenderLines), typeof(IEnumerable), typeof(InvoicePaperPreviewControl),
            new PropertyMetadata(null, OnTenderLinesChanged));

        public IEnumerable TenderLines
        {
            get { return (IEnumerable)GetValue(TenderLinesProperty); }
            set { SetValue(TenderLinesProperty, value); }
        }

        public static readonly DependencyProperty ZoomPercentProperty = DependencyProperty.Register(
            nameof(ZoomPercent), typeof(double), typeof(InvoicePaperPreviewControl),
            new PropertyMetadata(100.0, OnZoomChanged));

        public double ZoomPercent
        {
            get { return (double)GetValue(ZoomPercentProperty); }
            set { SetValue(ZoomPercentProperty, value); }
        }

        // ---- Computed display properties ---------------------------------------------

        public static readonly DependencyProperty HasPreviewProperty = DependencyProperty.Register(
            nameof(HasPreview), typeof(bool), typeof(InvoicePaperPreviewControl), new PropertyMetadata(false));

        public bool HasPreview
        {
            get { return (bool)GetValue(HasPreviewProperty); }
            private set { SetValue(HasPreviewProperty, value); }
        }

        public static readonly DependencyProperty IsThermalProperty = DependencyProperty.Register(
            nameof(IsThermal), typeof(bool), typeof(InvoicePaperPreviewControl), new PropertyMetadata(true));

        public bool IsThermal
        {
            get { return (bool)GetValue(IsThermalProperty); }
            private set { SetValue(IsThermalProperty, value); }
        }

        public static readonly DependencyProperty IsPageProperty = DependencyProperty.Register(
            nameof(IsPage), typeof(bool), typeof(InvoicePaperPreviewControl), new PropertyMetadata(false));

        public bool IsPage
        {
            get { return (bool)GetValue(IsPageProperty); }
            private set { SetValue(IsPageProperty, value); }
        }

        public static readonly DependencyProperty ShowShopProperty = DependencyProperty.Register(
            nameof(ShowShop), typeof(bool), typeof(InvoicePaperPreviewControl), new PropertyMetadata(false));

        public bool ShowShop
        {
            get { return (bool)GetValue(ShowShopProperty); }
            private set { SetValue(ShowShopProperty, value); }
        }

        public static readonly DependencyProperty ShowCustomerProperty = DependencyProperty.Register(
            nameof(ShowCustomer), typeof(bool), typeof(InvoicePaperPreviewControl), new PropertyMetadata(false));

        public bool ShowCustomer
        {
            get { return (bool)GetValue(ShowCustomerProperty); }
            private set { SetValue(ShowCustomerProperty, value); }
        }

        public static readonly DependencyProperty ShowCgstSgstProperty = DependencyProperty.Register(

        {
            get { return (bool)GetValue(ShowCgstSgstProperty); }
            private set { SetValue(ShowCgstSgstProperty, value); }
        }

        public static readonly DependencyProperty ShowIgstProperty = DependencyProperty.Register(
            nameof(ShowIgst), typeof(bool), typeof(InvoicePaperPreviewControl), new PropertyMetadata(false));

        public bool ShowIgst
        {
            get { return (bool)GetValue(ShowIgstProperty); }
            private set { SetValue(ShowIgstProperty, value); }
        }

        public static readonly DependencyProperty ShowTaxableValueProperty = DependencyProperty.Register(
            nameof(ShowTaxableValue), typeof(bool), typeof(InvoicePaperPreviewControl), new PropertyMetadata(false));

        public bool ShowTaxableValue

            get { return (bool)GetValue(ShowTaxableValueProperty); }
            private set { SetValue(ShowTaxableValueProperty, value); }
        }

        public static readonly DependencyProperty ShowRoundOffProperty = DependencyProperty.Register(

        public bool ShowRoundOff

            get { return (bool)GetValue(ShowRoundOffProperty); }
            private set { SetValue(ShowRoundOffProperty, value); }

        public bool ShowRemaining

            get { return (bool)GetValue(ShowRemainingProperty); }
            private set { SetValue(ShowRemainingProperty, value); }

        public static readonly DependencyProperty ShowPaymentsProperty = DependencyProperty.Register(

        public bool ShowPayments
        {
            get { return (bool)GetValue(ShowPaymentsProperty); }
            private set { SetValue(ShowPaymentsProperty, value); }
        }

        public static readonly DependencyProperty InvoiceNumberTextProperty = DependencyProperty.Register(

        // ---- Callbacks ---------------------------------------------------------------

        private static void OnPreviewChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
        {
            var control = (InvoicePaperPreviewControl)d;
            control.UpdatePreviewFlags();
            control.UpdateInvoiceIdentity();
        }

        private static void OnCommittedChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
        {
            ((InvoicePaperPreviewControl)d).UpdateInvoiceIdentity();
        }

        private static void OnPaperFormatChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
        {
            ((InvoicePaperPreviewControl)d).ApplyFormat();
        }

        private static void OnShopChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
        {
            ((InvoicePaperPreviewControl)d).UpdateShopFlags();
        }

        private static void OnCustomerChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
        {
            ((InvoicePaperPreviewControl)d).UpdateCustomerFlags();
        }

        private static void OnZoomChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
        {
            ((InvoicePaperPreviewControl)d).ApplyZoom();
        }

        private static void OnTenderLinesChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
        {
            var control = (InvoicePaperPreviewControl)d;
            control.DetachTenders();
            control.AttachTenders(control.TenderLines);
            control.UpdatePaymentFlags();
        }

        private void Control_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            DetachTenders();
            AttachTenders(TenderLines);
            UpdatePaymentFlags();
        }

        private void Control_Unloaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            DetachTenders();
        }

        // ---- Layout ------------------------------------------------------------------

        private void ApplyFormat()
        {
            if (PaperBorder == null || PaperContent == null) return;

            var format = PaperFormat;
            var thermal = format == InvoicePaperFormat.Thermal58 || format == InvoicePaperFormat.Thermal80;
            IsThermal = thermal;
            IsPage = !thermal;

            // On-screen paper sizes at 96 device-independent pixels per inch.
            double width;
            double minHeight;
            double baseFont;
            double smallFont;
            double tinyFont;
            Thickness padding;

            switch (format)
            {
                case InvoicePaperFormat.Thermal58:
                    width = 220; minHeight = 380;
                    padding = new Thickness(10, 8, 10, 12);
                    baseFont = 10.5; smallFont = 9.5; tinyFont = 8.5;
                    break;
                case InvoicePaperFormat.Thermal80:
                    width = 304; minHeight = 420;
                    padding = new Thickness(12, 10, 12, 14);
                    baseFont = 11.5; smallFont = 10.5; tinyFont = 9.5;
                    break;
                case InvoicePaperFormat.A5:
                    width = 559; minHeight = 794;
                    padding = new Thickness(26, 22, 26, 26);
                    baseFont = 11.5; smallFont = 10.5; tinyFont = 9.5;
                    break;
                default:
                    width = 794; minHeight = 1123;
                    padding = new Thickness(38, 32, 38, 36);
                    baseFont = 12.5; smallFont = 11.0; tinyFont = 10.0;
                    break;
            }

            PaperBorder.Width = width;
            PaperBorder.MinHeight = minHeight;
            PaperContent.Padding = padding;
            ThermalPanel.FontSize = baseFont;
            PagePanel.FontSize = baseFont;
            SmallFontSize = smallFont;
            TinyFontSize = tinyFont;

            ApplyZoom();
        }

        private void ApplyZoom()
        {
            if (PaperBorder == null) return;

            var percent = ZoomPercent;
            if (percent <= 0) percent = 100.0;

            // Pure visual scale. This value never participates in money or stock arithmetic.
            var scale = percent / 100.0;
            var transform = new ScaleTransform(scale, scale);
            transform.Freeze();
            PaperBorder.LayoutTransform = transform;
        }

        // ---- Display flags -----------------------------------------------------------

        private void UpdatePreviewFlags()
        {
            var preview = Preview;
            HasPreview = preview != null;

            var cgstSgst = preview != null && (preview.CgstPaise != 0 || preview.SgstPaise != 0);
            var igst = preview != null && preview.IgstPaise != 0;

            ShowCgstSgst = cgstSgst;
            ShowIgst = igst;
            ShowTaxableValue = cgstSgst || igst;
            ShowRoundOff = preview != null && preview.RoundOffPaise != 0;
            ShowRemaining = preview != null && preview.AmountRemainingPaise != 0;
        }

        private void UpdateShopFlags()
        {
            var shop = Shop;
            ShowShop = shop != null && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(shop.ShopName);
        }

        private void UpdateCustomerFlags()
        {
            ShowCustomer = !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(CustomerName)
                || !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(CustomerPhone)
                || !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(CustomerAddress)
                || !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(CustomerGstin);
        }

        private void UpdateInvoiceIdentity()
        {
            var committed = CommittedInvoice;
            if (committed != null && committed.Succeeded)
            {
                var number = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(committed.InvoiceNumber) ? "—" : committed.InvoiceNumber.Trim();
                InvoiceNumberText = BillingText.Get("BillInvoiceNoLabel", "Invoice no.") + ": " + number;
                InvoiceDateText = BillingText.Get("BillIssuedAtLabel", "Date & time") + ": " + FormatIssuedLocal(committed);
            }
            else
            {
                InvoiceNumberText = BillingText.Get("BillPreviewPendingNumber",
                    "Invoice number will be assigned after generation");
                InvoiceDateText = BillingText.Get("BillPreviewPendingDate",
                    "Date & time will be assigned after generation");
            }
        }

        private static string FormatIssuedLocal(InvoiceResult result)
        {
            if (!result.IssuedAtUtc.HasValue) return string.Empty;
            try
            {
                // Timestamp display only: the backend supplies the UTC instant and the offset.
                var local = result.IssuedAtUtc.Value.AddMinutes(result.IssuedLocalOffsetMinutes);
                return local.ToString("dd MMM yyyy, hh:mm tt", CultureInfo.CurrentCulture);
            }
            catch (ArgumentOutOfRangeException)
            {
                return result.IssuedAtUtc.Value.ToString("dd MMM yyyy, HH:mm", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) + " UTC";
            }
        }

        // ---- Tender watching (display of the payments block) -------------------------

        private void AttachTenders(IEnumerable source)
        {
            if (source == null) return;

            var collection = source as INotifyCollectionChanged;
            if (collection != null)
            {
                collection.CollectionChanged += TenderCollection_Changed;
                _watchedCollection = collection;
            }

            foreach (object item in source)
            {
                var tender = item as PaymentTenderViewModel;
                if (tender == null) continue;
                tender.PropertyChanged += Tender_PropertyChanged;
                _watchedTenders.Add(tender);
            }
        }

        private void DetachTenders()
        {
            if (_watchedCollection != null)
            {
                _watchedCollection.CollectionChanged -= TenderCollection_Changed;
                _watchedCollection = null;
            }
            for (var i = 0; i < _watchedTenders.Count; i++)
            {
                _watchedTenders[i].PropertyChanged -= Tender_PropertyChanged;
            }
            _watchedTenders.Clear();
        }

        private void TenderCollection_Changed(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
        {
            DetachTenders();
            AttachTenders(TenderLines);
            UpdatePaymentFlags();
        }

        private void Tender_PropertyChanged(object sender, PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
        {
            var name = e.PropertyName;
            if (!string.Equals(name, "HasAmount", StringComparison.Ordinal)
                && !string.Equals(name, "AmountPaise", StringComparison.Ordinal)
                && !string.Equals(name, "HasError", StringComparison.Ordinal)
                && !string.Equals(name, "MethodName", StringComparison.Ordinal)) return;

            UpdatePaymentFlags();
        }

        private void UpdatePaymentFlags()
        {
            var any = false;
            var source = TenderLines;
            if (source != null)
            {
                foreach (object item in source)
                {
                    var tender = item as PaymentTenderViewModel;
                    if (tender == null) continue;
                    if (tender.HasAmount) { any = true; break; }
                }
            }
            ShowPayments = any;
        }
    }
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace Orva.Domain.Billing
{
    // Shared, versioned boundary between the billing engine and the WPF billing UI.
    // Keep this contract compatible with C# 7.3 / .NET Framework 4.8.1.

    public enum InvoicePaymentMethod
    {
        Cash = 1,
        Upi = 2,
        DebitCard = 3,
        CreditCard = 4
    }

    public enum BillingIssueSeverity
    {
        Information = 1,
        Warning = 2,
        Blocking = 3
    }

    public sealed class InvoiceDraft
    {
        public InvoiceDraft()
        {
            Items = new List<InvoiceItemDraft>();
            Tenders = new List<PaymentTenderDraft>();
            Customer = new InvoiceCustomerDraft();
        }

        public List<InvoiceItemDraft> Items { get; set; }
        public List<PaymentTenderDraft> Tenders { get; set; }
        public InvoiceCustomerDraft Customer { get; set; }
    }

    public sealed class InvoiceItemDraft
    {
        public string MedicineId { get; set; }
        public long QuantityMilli { get; set; }
    }

    public sealed class PaymentTenderDraft
    {
        public InvoicePaymentMethod Method { get; set; }
        public long AmountPaise { get; set; }
        public string ReferenceText { get; set; }
    }

    public sealed class InvoiceCustomerDraft
    {
        public string Name { get; set; }
        public string Phone { get; set; }
        public string Address { get; set; }
        public string Gstin { get; set; }
    }

    public sealed class MedicineLookupResult
    {
        public string MedicineId { get; set; }
        public string DisplayName { get; set; }
        public string GenericName { get; set; }
        public string BrandName { get; set; }
        public string Strength { get; set; }
        public string DosageForm { get; set; }
        public string StockUnitName { get; set; }
        public long AvailableQuantityMilli { get; set; }
        public long LowStockThresholdMilli { get; set; }
        public bool IsLowStock { get; set; }
        public bool CanSell { get; set; }
        public string AvailabilityMessage { get; set; }
    }

    public sealed class InvoicePreviewResult
    {
        public InvoicePreviewResult()
        {
            Lines = new List<InvoiceLinePreview>();
            Issues = new List<BillingIssue>();
        }

        public bool IsDraftValid { get; set; }
        public bool CanFinalize { get; set; }
        public List<InvoiceLinePreview> Lines { get; set; }
        public List<BillingIssue> Issues { get; set; }
        public long SubtotalPaise { get; set; }
        public long DiscountPaise { get; set; }
        public long TaxableValuePaise { get; set; }
        public long CgstPaise { get; set; }
        public long SgstPaise { get; set; }
        public long IgstPaise { get; set; }
        public long RoundOffPaise { get; set; }
        public long GrandTotalPaise { get; set; }
        public long TenderTotalPaise { get; set; }
        public long AmountRemainingPaise { get; set; }
        public bool IsFullyPaid { get; set; }
    }

    public sealed class InvoiceLinePreview
    {
        public InvoiceLinePreview()
        {
            BatchAllocations = new List<BatchAllocationPreview>();
        }

        public string MedicineId { get; set; }
        public string MedicineName { get; set; }
        public long QuantityMilli { get; set; }
        public string StockUnitName { get; set; }
        public List<BatchAllocationPreview> BatchAllocations { get; set; }
        public long SubtotalPaise { get; set; }
        public long TaxableValuePaise { get; set; }
        public long CgstPaise { get; set; }
        public long SgstPaise { get; set; }
        public long IgstPaise { get; set; }
        public long LineTotalPaise { get; set; }
    }

    public sealed class BatchAllocationPreview
    {
        public string BatchNumber { get; set; }
        public int ExpiryYyyyMm { get; set; }
        public long QuantityMilli { get; set; }
        public long UnitPricePaise { get; set; }
        public long SubtotalPaise { get; set; }
        public long TaxableValuePaise { get; set; }
        public long CgstPaise { get; set; }
        public long SgstPaise { get; set; }
        public long IgstPaise { get; set; }
        public long LineTotalPaise { get; set; }
    }

    public sealed class BillingIssue
    {
        public BillingIssueSeverity Severity { get; set; }
        public string Code { get; set; }
        public string Message { get; set; }
        public string MedicineId { get; set; }
    }

    public sealed class InvoiceResult
    {
        public InvoiceResult()
        {
            Issues = new List<BillingIssue>();
            Warnings = new List<string>();
        }

        public bool Succeeded { get; set; }
        public string InvoiceId { get; set; }
        public string InvoiceNumber { get; set; }
        public DateTime? IssuedAtUtc { get; set; }
        public int IssuedLocalOffsetMinutes { get; set; }
        public long GrandTotalPaise { get; set; }
        public List<BillingIssue> Issues { get; set; }
        public List<string> Warnings { get; set; }
    }

    public interface IBillingService
    {
        IList<MedicineLookupResult> SearchMedicines(string query, int maximumResults);
        InvoicePreviewResult PreviewInvoice(InvoiceDraft draft);
        InvoiceResult FinalizeInvoice(InvoiceDraft draft);
    }
}

using System;
using System.IO;

namespace Orva.Infrastructure
{
    public static class AppPaths
    {
        public static string ProductRoot
        {
            get { return Path.Combine(Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.CommonApplicationData), "Rindle Works", "ORVA"); }
        }

        public static string DataDirectory { get { return Path.Combine(ProductRoot, "Data"); } }
        public static string DatabasePath { get { return Path.Combine(DataDirectory, "orva.sqlite"); } }
        public static string LogsDirectory { get { return Path.Combine(ProductRoot, "Logs"); } }
        public static string BackupsDirectory { get { return Path.Combine(ProductRoot, "Backups"); } }

        public static void EnsureDirectories()
        {
            Directory.CreateDirectory(ProductRoot);
            Directory.CreateDirectory(DataDirectory);
            Directory.CreateDirectory(LogsDirectory);
            Directory.CreateDirectory(BackupsDirectory);
        }

        public static string GetShopFolder(string shopName)
        {
            var documents = Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.MyDocuments);
            return Path.Combine(documents, MakeSafeFolderName(shopName));
        }

        public static string MakeSafeFolderName(string value)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value)) return "ORVA Shop Data";
            var invalid = Path.GetInvalidFileNameChars();
            var chars = value.Trim().ToCharArray();
            for (var i = 0; i < chars.Length; i++)
            {
                if (Array.IndexOf(invalid, chars[i]) >= 0) chars[i] = '-';
            }
            var result = new string(chars).Trim().TrimEnd('.');
            return string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(result) ? "ORVA Shop Data" : result;
        }
    }
}
