FILE: ORVA/src/Orva.Desktop/Infrastructure/Database/001_InitialSchema.sql
================================================================================
-- ORVA permanent schema, migration 001.
-- Quantities are stored as milli-units (1.000 unit = 1000).
-- Monetary amounts are stored as integer paise. Never store money as REAL.

PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON;

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS app_meta (
    meta_key TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    meta_value TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS shop_profile (
    profile_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK (profile_id = 1),
    shop_id TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    shop_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    legal_name TEXT,
    address TEXT,
    phone TEXT,
    email TEXT,
    state_name TEXT,
    state_code TEXT,
    gstin TEXT,
    drug_licence_number TEXT,
    invoice_prefix TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'INV',
    financial_year_start_month INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 4 CHECK (financial_year_start_month BETWEEN 1 AND 12),
    created_at_utc TEXT NOT NULL,
    updated_at_utc TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS app_setting (
    setting_key TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    setting_value TEXT NOT NULL,
    updated_at_utc TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS medicine (
    medicine_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    display_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    normalized_name TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    generic_name TEXT,
    brand_name TEXT,
    strength TEXT,
    dosage_form TEXT,
    stock_unit_name TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'unit',
    hsn_code TEXT,
    gst_rate_basis_points INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (gst_rate_basis_points BETWEEN 0 AND 10000),
    low_stock_threshold_milli INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (low_stock_threshold_milli >= 0),
    is_active INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (is_active IN (0, 1)),
    created_at_utc TEXT NOT NULL,
    updated_at_utc TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS barcode_mapping (
    barcode TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    medicine_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES medicine(medicine_id),
    created_at_utc TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS stock_batch (
    batch_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    medicine_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES medicine(medicine_id),
    batch_number TEXT NOT NULL,
    expiry_yyyymm INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (expiry_yyyymm BETWEEN 200001 AND 219912),
    stock_unit_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    mrp_paise INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (mrp_paise >= 0),
    latest_purchase_price_paise INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (latest_purchase_price_paise >= 0),
    quantity_milli INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (quantity_milli >= 0),
    batch_status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE' CHECK (batch_status IN ('ACTIVE', 'QUARANTINED', 'EXPIRED', 'BLOCKED')),
    created_at_utc TEXT NOT NULL,
    updated_at_utc TEXT NOT NULL,
    UNIQUE (medicine_id, batch_number, expiry_yyyymm)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS purchase_receipt (
    purchase_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    received_at_utc TEXT NOT NULL,
    supplier_name_text TEXT,
    supplier_licence_text TEXT,
    document_type TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (document_type IN ('TAX_INVOICE', 'CASH_CREDIT_MEMO', 'NO_DOCUMENT', 'OTHER')),
    document_reference TEXT,
    notes TEXT,
    subtotal_paise INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (subtotal_paise >= 0),
    total_tax_paise INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (total_tax_paise >= 0),
    total_paise INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (total_paise >= 0),
    created_at_utc TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS purchase_line (
    purchase_line_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    purchase_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES purchase_receipt(purchase_id),
    batch_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES stock_batch(batch_id),
    medicine_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES medicine(medicine_id),
    quantity_milli INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (quantity_milli > 0),
    unit_name_snapshot TEXT NOT NULL,
    unit_purchase_price_paise INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (unit_purchase_price_paise >= 0),
    line_total_paise INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (line_total_paise >= 0),
    created_at_utc TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS invoice_sequence (
    financial_year TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    last_number INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (last_number >= 0)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS invoice (
    invoice_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    invoice_number TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    financial_year TEXT NOT NULL,
    issued_at_utc TEXT NOT NULL,
    issued_local_offset_minutes INTEGER NOT NULL,
    customer_name TEXT,
    customer_phone TEXT,
    customer_address TEXT,
    customer_gstin TEXT,
    place_of_supply_state_code TEXT,
    subtotal_paise INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (subtotal_paise >= 0),
    discount_paise INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (discount_paise >= 0),
    taxable_value_paise INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (taxable_value_paise >= 0),
    cgst_paise INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (cgst_paise >= 0),
    sgst_paise INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (sgst_paise >= 0),
    igst_paise INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (igst_paise >= 0),
    round_off_paise INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    grand_total_paise INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (grand_total_paise >= 0),
    status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ISSUED' CHECK (status IN ('ISSUED', 'CANCELLED', 'CREDIT_NOTE_ISSUED')),
    cancellation_reason TEXT,
    created_at_utc TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS invoice_line (
    invoice_line_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    invoice_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES invoice(invoice_id),
    line_group_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    medicine_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES medicine(medicine_id),
    batch_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES stock_batch(batch_id),
    medicine_name_snapshot TEXT NOT NULL,
    batch_number_snapshot TEXT NOT NULL,
    expiry_yyyymm_snapshot INTEGER NOT NULL,
    quantity_milli INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (quantity_milli > 0),
    unit_name_snapshot TEXT NOT NULL,
    unit_price_paise INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (unit_price_paise >= 0),
    discount_paise INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (discount_paise >= 0),
    taxable_value_paise INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (taxable_value_paise >= 0),
    gst_rate_basis_points INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (gst_rate_basis_points BETWEEN 0 AND 10000),
    cgst_paise INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (cgst_paise >= 0),
    sgst_paise INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (sgst_paise >= 0),
    igst_paise INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (igst_paise >= 0),
    line_total_paise INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (line_total_paise >= 0),
    created_at_utc TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS payment (
    payment_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    invoice_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES invoice(invoice_id),
    method TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (method IN ('CASH', 'UPI', 'DEBIT_CARD', 'CREDIT_CARD')),
    amount_paise INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (amount_paise > 0),
    reference_text TEXT,
    paid_at_utc TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS sales_return (
    return_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    return_number TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    invoice_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES invoice(invoice_id),
    returned_at_utc TEXT NOT NULL,
    reason TEXT NOT NULL,
    refund_method TEXT CHECK (refund_method IN ('CASH', 'UPI', 'DEBIT_CARD', 'CREDIT_CARD', 'ADJUSTMENT')),
    refund_amount_paise INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (refund_amount_paise >= 0),
    status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'COMPLETED' CHECK (status IN ('COMPLETED', 'CANCELLED')),
    created_at_utc TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS sales_return_line (
    return_line_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    return_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES sales_return(return_id),
    invoice_line_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES invoice_line(invoice_line_id),
    batch_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES stock_batch(batch_id),
    quantity_milli INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (quantity_milli > 0),
    resale_disposition TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (resale_disposition IN ('QUARANTINE', 'RESTOCK', 'DAMAGED', 'EXPIRED')),
    line_refund_paise INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (line_refund_paise >= 0)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS stock_movement (
    movement_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    batch_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES stock_batch(batch_id),
    medicine_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES medicine(medicine_id),
    movement_type TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (movement_type IN ('OPENING', 'PURCHASE', 'SALE', 'SALE_RETURN_RESTOCK', 'DAMAGE', 'EXPIRY_WRITE_OFF', 'ADJUSTMENT', 'IMPORT')),
    quantity_delta_milli INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (quantity_delta_milli <> 0),
    reference_type TEXT,
    reference_id TEXT,
    reason TEXT,
    created_at_utc TEXT NOT NULL,
    created_local_text TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS stock_discrepancy (
    discrepancy_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    batch_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES stock_batch(batch_id),
    expected_quantity_milli INTEGER NOT NULL,
    counted_quantity_milli INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (counted_quantity_milli >= 0),
    difference_milli INTEGER NOT NULL,
    reason TEXT NOT NULL,
    reconciled_at_utc TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS audit_log (
    audit_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    action_type TEXT NOT NULL,
    entity_type TEXT NOT NULL,
    entity_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    summary TEXT NOT NULL,
    before_json TEXT,
    after_json TEXT,
    created_at_utc TEXT NOT NULL,
    created_local_text TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS expense_record (
    expense_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    expense_date_utc TEXT NOT NULL,
    category TEXT NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    amount_paise INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (amount_paise > 0),
    payment_method TEXT CHECK (payment_method IN ('CASH', 'UPI', 'DEBIT_CARD', 'CREDIT_CARD')),
    document_reference TEXT,
    created_at_utc TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_medicine_name ON medicine(normalized_name);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ux_batch_normalized_identity ON stock_batch(medicine_id, UPPER(TRIM(batch_number)), expiry_yyyymm);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_batch_medicine_expiry ON stock_batch(medicine_id, expiry_yyyymm);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_purchase_date ON purchase_receipt(received_at_utc);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_invoice_date ON invoice(issued_at_utc);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_invoice_financial_year ON invoice(financial_year, invoice_number);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_return_date ON sales_return(returned_at_utc);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_stock_movement_batch_date ON stock_movement(batch_id, created_at_utc);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_audit_entity_date ON audit_log(entity_type, entity_id, created_at_utc);

INSERT OR IGNORE INTO app_meta(meta_key, meta_value) VALUES ('schema_version', '1');

-- ORVA permanent schema, migration 002.
-- Adds explicit medicine GST configuration tracking and immutable HSN snapshot on invoice lines.
-- Does not destructively modify migration 001.

PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON;

ALTER TABLE medicine
    ADD COLUMN gst_rate_configured INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (gst_rate_configured IN (0, 1));

ALTER TABLE invoice_line
    ADD COLUMN hsn_code_snapshot TEXT;

UPDATE app_meta
SET meta_value = '2'
WHERE meta_key = 'schema_version';

INSERT OR IGNORE INTO app_meta(meta_key, meta_value)
VALUES ('schema_version', '2');

================================================================================
FILE: ORVA/src/Orva.Desktop/Infrastructure/Database/DatabaseMigrator.cs
================================================================================
using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Reflection;
using System.Data.SQLite;

namespace Orva.Infrastructure.Database
{
    public sealed class DatabaseMigrator
    {
        private const int CurrentSchemaVersion = 2;
        private readonly SqliteDatabase _database;

        public DatabaseMigrator(SqliteDatabase database)
        {
            _database = database;
        }

        public void Migrate()
        {
            using (var connection = _database.OpenConnection())
            {
                var version = Convert.ToInt32(Scalar(connection, "PRAGMA user_version;"));
                if (version > CurrentSchemaVersion)
                    throw new InvalidOperationException("This ORVA data file was created by a newer version of the application. Update ORVA before opening it.");

                if (version < 1)
                {
                    ApplyMigration(connection, "Orva.Infrastructure.Database.001_InitialSchema.sql", 1);
                    version = 1;
                }

                if (version < 2)
                {
                    ApplyMigration(connection, "Orva.Infrastructure.Database.002_BillingTaxConfig.sql", 2);
                }
            }
        }

        private static void ApplyMigration(SQLiteConnection connection, string resourceName, int targetVersion)
        {
            var sql = ReadResource(resourceName);
            using (var transaction = connection.BeginTransaction())
            using (var command = new SQLiteCommand(sql, connection, transaction))
            {
                command.ExecuteNonQuery();
                using (var versionCommand = new SQLiteCommand("PRAGMA user_version = " + targetVersion + ";", connection, transaction))
                    versionCommand.ExecuteNonQuery();
                transaction.Commit();
            }
        }

        private static object Scalar(SQLiteConnection connection, string sql)
        {
            using (var command = new SQLiteCommand(sql, connection)) return command.ExecuteScalar();
        }

        private static string ReadResource(string resourceName)
        {
            var assembly = Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly();
            using (var stream = assembly.GetManifestResourceStream(resourceName))
            {
                if (stream == null) throw new FileNotFoundException("Database migration resource was not embedded: " + resourceName);
                using (var reader = new StreamReader(stream)) return reader.ReadToEnd();
            }
        }
    }
}

================================================================================
FILE: ORVA/src/Orva.Desktop/Infrastructure/Database/SqliteDatabase.cs
================================================================================
using System;
using System.Data;
using System.IO;
using System.Data.SQLite;

namespace Orva.Infrastructure.Database
{
    public sealed class SqliteDatabase
    {
        private readonly string _databasePath;

        public SqliteDatabase(string databasePath)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(databasePath)) throw new ArgumentException("A database path is required.", "databasePath");
            _databasePath = databasePath;
        }

        public SQLiteConnection OpenConnection()
        {
            var directory = Path.GetDirectoryName(_databasePath);
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(directory)) Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);

            var builder = new SQLiteConnectionStringBuilder
            {
                DataSource = _databasePath,
                Version = 3,
                Pooling = true,
                DefaultTimeout = 5
            };

            var connection = new SQLiteConnection(builder.ConnectionString);
            connection.Open();
            ExecutePragma(connection, "PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON;");
            ExecutePragma(connection, "PRAGMA busy_timeout = 5000;");
            ExecutePragma(connection, "PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL;");
            ExecutePragma(connection, "PRAGMA synchronous = FULL;");
            return connection;
        }

        public string DatabasePath { get { return _databasePath; } }

        private static void ExecutePragma(SQLiteConnection connection, string sql)
        {
            using (var command = new SQLiteCommand(sql, connection)) command.ExecuteNonQuery();
        }
    }
}

================================================================================
FILE: ORVA/src/Orva.Desktop/Infrastructure/Repositories/AppSettingsRepository.cs
================================================================================
using System;
using System.Data.SQLite;
using Orva.Infrastructure.Database;

namespace Orva.Infrastructure.Repositories
{
    public sealed class AppSettingsRepository
    {
        private readonly SqliteDatabase _database;
        public AppSettingsRepository(SqliteDatabase database) { _database = database; }

        public string Get(string key, string defaultValue)
        {
            using (var connection = _database.OpenConnection())
            using (var command = new SQLiteCommand("SELECT setting_value FROM app_setting WHERE setting_key=@key;", connection))
            {
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@key", key);
                var value = command.ExecuteScalar();
                return value == null || value == DBNull.Value ? defaultValue : Convert.ToString(value);
            }
        }

        public void Set(string key, string value)
        {
            var now = DateTime.UtcNow.ToString("o");
            using (var connection = _database.OpenConnection())
            using (var command = new SQLiteCommand(@"
INSERT INTO app_setting(setting_key, setting_value, updated_at_utc) VALUES(@key, @value, @updated)
ON CONFLICT(setting_key) DO UPDATE SET setting_value=excluded.setting_value, updated_at_utc=excluded.updated_at_utc;", connection))
            {
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@key", key);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@value", value);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@updated", now);
                command.ExecuteNonQuery();
            }
        }
    }
}

================================================================================
FILE: ORVA/src/Orva.Desktop/Infrastructure/Repositories/BillingRepository.cs
================================================================================
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Data;
using System.Data.SQLite;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Web.Script.Serialization;
using Orva.Domain.Billing;
using Orva.Infrastructure.Database;
using Orva.Services.Billing;

namespace Orva.Infrastructure.Repositories
{
    public sealed class BillingRepository
    {
        private readonly SqliteDatabase _database;

        public BillingRepository(SqliteDatabase database)
        {
            if (database == null) throw new ArgumentNullException("database");
            _database = database;
        }

        public IList<MedicineLookupResult> SearchMedicines(string query, int maximumResults, int currentExpiryYearMonth)
        {
            var results = new List<MedicineLookupResult>();
            if (maximumResults <= 0) return results;

            string trimmed = (query ?? string.Empty).Trim();
            string normalized = NormalizeSearchText(trimmed);

            const string sql = @"
SELECT m.medicine_id,
       m.display_name,
       m.generic_name,
       m.brand_name,
       m.strength,
       m.dosage_form,
       m.stock_unit_name,
       m.low_stock_threshold_milli,
       COALESCE((
           SELECT SUM(b.quantity_milli)
           FROM stock_batch b
           WHERE b.medicine_id = m.medicine_id
             AND b.batch_status = 'ACTIVE'
             AND b.quantity_milli > 0
             AND b.expiry_yyyymm >= @current_month
       ), 0) AS available_qty_milli,
       CASE
           WHEN @raw <> '' AND EXISTS (
               SELECT 1 FROM barcode_mapping bm
               WHERE bm.medicine_id = m.medicine_id AND bm.barcode = @raw
           ) THEN 0
           WHEN @norm <> '' AND m.normalized_name LIKE @prefix THEN 1
           ELSE 2
       END AS match_rank
FROM medicine m
WHERE m.is_active = 1
  AND (
      @norm = ''
      OR m.normalized_name LIKE @like
      OR UPPER(COALESCE(m.generic_name, '')) LIKE @like
      OR UPPER(COALESCE(m.brand_name, '')) LIKE @like
      OR EXISTS (
          SELECT 1 FROM barcode_mapping bm
          WHERE bm.medicine_id = m.medicine_id
            AND bm.barcode = @raw
      )
  )
ORDER BY match_rank ASC, m.display_name COLLATE NOCASE ASC
LIMIT @limit;";

            using (var connection = _database.OpenConnection())
            using (var command = new SQLiteCommand(sql, connection))
            {
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@current_month", currentExpiryYearMonth);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@raw", trimmed);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@norm", normalized);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@prefix", normalized + "%");
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@like", "%" + normalized + "%");
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@limit", maximumResults);

                using (var reader = command.ExecuteReader())
                {
                    while (reader.Read())
                    {
                        long available = reader.GetInt64(8);
                        long threshold = reader.GetInt64(7);
                        string unit = reader.GetString(6);
                        bool canSell = available > 0;
                        bool isLowStock = canSell && threshold > 0 && available <= threshold;

                        results.Add(new MedicineLookupResult
                        {
                            MedicineId = reader.GetString(0),
                            DisplayName = reader.GetString(1),
                            GenericName = ReadNullableString(reader, 2),
                            BrandName = ReadNullableString(reader, 3),
                            Strength = ReadNullableString(reader, 4),
                            DosageForm = ReadNullableString(reader, 5),
                            StockUnitName = unit,
                            AvailableQuantityMilli = available,
                            LowStockThresholdMilli = threshold,
                            IsLowStock = isLowStock,
                            CanSell = canSell,
                            AvailabilityMessage = BuildAvailabilityMessage(available, unit, isLowStock)
                        });
                    }
                }
            }

            return results;
        }

        public BillingDataSnapshot LoadReadOnlySnapshot(IList<string> medicineIds, int currentExpiryYearMonth)
        {
            using (var connection = _database.OpenConnection())
            {
                return LoadSnapshot(connection, null, medicineIds, currentExpiryYearMonth);
            }
        }

        public BillingDataSnapshot LoadSnapshot(
            SQLiteConnection connection,
            SQLiteTransaction transaction,
            IList<string> medicineIds,
            int currentExpiryYearMonth)
        {
            var snapshot = new BillingDataSnapshot
            {
                Shop = ReadShopContext(connection, transaction),
                TaxSettings = ReadTaxSettings(connection, transaction)
            };

            if (medicineIds == null || medicineIds.Count == 0)
            {
                return snapshot;
            }

            for (int i = 0; i < medicineIds.Count; i++)
            {
                string medicineId = medicineIds[i];
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(medicineId) || snapshot.Medicines.ContainsKey(medicineId))
                {
                    continue;
                }

                var medicine = ReadMedicine(connection, transaction, medicineId);
                if (medicine != null)
                {
                    snapshot.Medicines.Add(medicineId, medicine);
                    snapshot.EligibleBatchesByMedicine.Add(
                        medicineId,
                        ReadEligibleBatches(connection, transaction, medicineId, currentExpiryYearMonth));
                }
            }

            return snapshot;
        }

        public SQLiteConnection OpenWriteConnection()
        {
            return _database.OpenConnection();
        }

        public CommittedInvoiceRecord PersistInvoiceInTransaction(
            SQLiteConnection connection,
            SQLiteTransaction transaction,
            BillingShopContext shop,
            ResolvedTaxPolicy taxPolicy,
            CalculatedInvoice calculated,
            InvoiceCustomerDraft customer,
            IList<ValidatedTender> tenders,
            DateTime issuedAtUtc,
            int localOffsetMinutes,
            DateTime localNow,
            int currentExpiryYearMonth,
            BillingWriteCheckpoint checkpoint)
        {
            string utcText = issuedAtUtc.ToString("o", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
            string localText = localNow.ToString("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
            string financialYear = InvoiceCalculator.GetFinancialYearLabel(localNow, shop.FinancialYearStartMonth);

            long nextSequence = IncrementInvoiceSequence(connection, transaction, financialYear);
            InvokeCheckpoint(checkpoint, "after_sequence_increment");

            string invoiceId = Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N");
            string invoiceNumber = InvoiceCalculator.FormatInvoiceNumber(shop.InvoicePrefix, financialYear, nextSequence);

            InsertInvoiceHeader(
                connection,
                transaction,
                invoiceId,
                invoiceNumber,
                financialYear,
                utcText,
                localOffsetMinutes,
                customer,
                taxPolicy.PlaceOfSupplyStateCode,
                calculated);
            InvokeCheckpoint(checkpoint, "after_invoice_insert");

            var beforeBatches = new List<Dictionary<string, object>>();
            var afterBatches = new List<Dictionary<string, object>>();

            for (int i = 0; i < calculated.Lines.Count; i++)
            {
                var line = calculated.Lines[i];
                for (int j = 0; j < line.BatchLines.Count; j++)
                {
                    var batchLine = line.BatchLines[j];

                    InsertInvoiceLine(
                        connection,
                        transaction,
                        invoiceId,
                        line.LineGroupId,
                        line.MedicineId,
                        line.MedicineName,
                        batchLine,
                        utcText);
                    InvokeCheckpoint(checkpoint, "after_invoice_line_insert");

                    DeductBatchStock(
                        connection,
                        transaction,
                        batchLine.BatchId,
                        batchLine.QuantityMilli,
                        currentExpiryYearMonth,
                        utcText);
                    InvokeCheckpoint(checkpoint, "after_batch_deduction");

                    InsertSaleStockMovement(
                        connection,
                        transaction,
                        batchLine.BatchId,
                        line.MedicineId,
                        batchLine.QuantityMilli,
                        invoiceId,
                        invoiceNumber,
                        utcText,
                        localText);
                    InvokeCheckpoint(checkpoint, "after_stock_movement_insert");

                    long postQty = checked(batchLine.PreviousQuantityMilli - batchLine.QuantityMilli);
                    beforeBatches.Add(new Dictionary<string, object>
                    {
                        { "batchId", batchLine.BatchId },
                        { "batchNumber", batchLine.BatchNumber },
                        { "quantityMilli", batchLine.PreviousQuantityMilli }
                    });
                    afterBatches.Add(new Dictionary<string, object>
                    {
                        { "batchId", batchLine.BatchId },
                        { "batchNumber", batchLine.BatchNumber },
                        { "quantityMilli", postQty }
                    });
                }
            }

            var paymentSummaries = new List<Dictionary<string, object>>();
            for (int i = 0; i < tenders.Count; i++)
            {
                var tender = tenders[i];
                InsertPayment(connection, transaction, invoiceId, tender, utcText);
                paymentSummaries.Add(new Dictionary<string, object>
                {
                    { "method", tender.DatabaseMethod },
                    { "amountPaise", tender.AmountPaise }
                });
            }
            InvokeCheckpoint(checkpoint, "after_payment_insert");

            InsertAuditLog(
                connection,
                transaction,
                invoiceId,
                invoiceNumber,
                calculated,
                beforeBatches,
                afterBatches,
                paymentSummaries,
                utcText,
                localText);
            InvokeCheckpoint(checkpoint, "after_audit_log_insert");

            MarkStockSnapshotDirty(connection, transaction, utcText);
            InvokeCheckpoint(checkpoint, "after_snapshot_dirty_update");

            return new CommittedInvoiceRecord
            {
                InvoiceId = invoiceId,
                InvoiceNumber = invoiceNumber,
                FinancialYear = financialYear,
                IssuedAtUtc = issuedAtUtc,
                IssuedLocalOffsetMinutes = localOffsetMinutes,
                GrandTotalPaise = calculated.GrandTotalPaise
            };
        }

        private static BillingShopContext ReadShopContext(SQLiteConnection connection, SQLiteTransaction transaction)
        {
            const string sql = @"
SELECT shop_id, shop_name, state_code, gstin, invoice_prefix, financial_year_start_month
FROM shop_profile
WHERE profile_id = 1;";

            using (var command = new SQLiteCommand(sql, connection, transaction))
            using (var reader = command.ExecuteReader())
            {
                if (!reader.Read()) return null;
                return new BillingShopContext
                {
                    ShopId = reader.GetString(0),
                    ShopName = reader.GetString(1),
                    StateCode = ReadNullableString(reader, 2),
                    Gstin = ReadNullableString(reader, 3),
                    InvoicePrefix = reader.GetString(4),
                    FinancialYearStartMonth = reader.GetInt32(5)
                };
            }
        }

        private static BillingTaxSettings ReadTaxSettings(SQLiteConnection connection, SQLiteTransaction transaction)
        {
            var settings = new BillingTaxSettings();
            const string sql = @"
SELECT setting_key, setting_value
FROM app_setting
WHERE setting_key IN (@k1, @k2, @k3, @k4);";

            using (var command = new SQLiteCommand(sql, connection, transaction))
            {
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@k1", BillingSettingKeys.TaxConfigured);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@k2", BillingSettingKeys.TaxPricingMode);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@k3", BillingSettingKeys.DefaultSupplyType);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@k4", BillingSettingKeys.RoundOffMode);

                using (var reader = command.ExecuteReader())
                {
                    while (reader.Read())
                    {
                        string key = reader.GetString(0);
                        string value = reader.GetString(1);

                        if (string.Equals(key, BillingSettingKeys.TaxConfigured, StringComparison.Ordinal))
                            settings.IsConfigured = string.Equals(value, "1", StringComparison.Ordinal);
                        else if (string.Equals(key, BillingSettingKeys.TaxPricingMode, StringComparison.Ordinal))
                            settings.PricingModeRaw = value;
                        else if (string.Equals(key, BillingSettingKeys.DefaultSupplyType, StringComparison.Ordinal))
                            settings.DefaultSupplyTypeRaw = value;
                        else if (string.Equals(key, BillingSettingKeys.RoundOffMode, StringComparison.Ordinal))
                            settings.RoundOffModeRaw = value;
                    }
                }
            }

            return settings;
        }

        private static BillingMedicineRecord ReadMedicine(SQLiteConnection connection, SQLiteTransaction transaction, string medicineId)
        {
            const string sql = @"
SELECT medicine_id, display_name, generic_name, brand_name, strength, dosage_form,
       stock_unit_name, hsn_code, gst_rate_basis_points, gst_rate_configured,
       low_stock_threshold_milli, is_active
FROM medicine
WHERE medicine_id = @id;";

            using (var command = new SQLiteCommand(sql, connection, transaction))
            {
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@id", medicineId);
                using (var reader = command.ExecuteReader())
                {
                    if (!reader.Read()) return null;
                    return new BillingMedicineRecord
                    {
                        MedicineId = reader.GetString(0),
                        DisplayName = reader.GetString(1),
                        GenericName = ReadNullableString(reader, 2),
                        BrandName = ReadNullableString(reader, 3),
                        Strength = ReadNullableString(reader, 4),
                        DosageForm = ReadNullableString(reader, 5),
                        StockUnitName = reader.GetString(6),
                        HsnCode = ReadNullableString(reader, 7),
                        GstRateBasisPoints = reader.GetInt32(8),
                        GstRateConfigured = reader.GetInt32(9) == 1,
                        LowStockThresholdMilli = reader.GetInt64(10),
                        IsActive = reader.GetInt32(11) == 1
                    };
                }
            }
        }

        private static List<BillingBatchRecord> ReadEligibleBatches(
            SQLiteConnection connection,
            SQLiteTransaction transaction,
            string medicineId,
            int currentExpiryYearMonth)
        {
            var batches = new List<BillingBatchRecord>();
            const string sql = @"
SELECT batch_id, medicine_id, batch_number, expiry_yyyymm, stock_unit_name,
       mrp_paise, quantity_milli, batch_status
FROM stock_batch
WHERE medicine_id = @medicine_id
  AND batch_status = 'ACTIVE'
  AND quantity_milli > 0
  AND expiry_yyyymm >= @current_month
ORDER BY expiry_yyyymm ASC, UPPER(TRIM(batch_number)) ASC, batch_id ASC;";

            using (var command = new SQLiteCommand(sql, connection, transaction))
            {
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@medicine_id", medicineId);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@current_month", currentExpiryYearMonth);

                using (var reader = command.ExecuteReader())
                {
                    while (reader.Read())
                    {
                        batches.Add(new BillingBatchRecord
                        {
                            BatchId = reader.GetString(0),
                            MedicineId = reader.GetString(1),
                            BatchNumber = reader.GetString(2),
                            ExpiryYyyyMm = reader.GetInt32(3),
                            StockUnitName = reader.GetString(4),
                            MrpPaise = reader.GetInt64(5),
                            QuantityMilli = reader.GetInt64(6),
                            BatchStatus = reader.GetString(7)
                        });
                    }
                }
            }

            return batches;
        }

        private static long IncrementInvoiceSequence(SQLiteConnection connection, SQLiteTransaction transaction, string financialYear)
        {
            const string upsertSql = @"
INSERT INTO invoice_sequence(financial_year, last_number)
VALUES(@fy, 1)
ON CONFLICT(financial_year) DO UPDATE SET last_number = last_number + 1;";

            using (var upsert = new SQLiteCommand(upsertSql, connection, transaction))
            {
                upsert.Parameters.AddWithValue("@fy", financialYear);
                upsert.ExecuteNonQuery();
            }

            using (var select = new SQLiteCommand("SELECT last_number FROM invoice_sequence WHERE financial_year = @fy;", connection, transaction))
            {
                select.Parameters.AddWithValue("@fy", financialYear);
                return Convert.ToInt64(select.ExecuteScalar(), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
            }
        }

        private static void InsertInvoiceHeader(
            SQLiteConnection connection,
            SQLiteTransaction transaction,
            string invoiceId,
            string invoiceNumber,
            string financialYear,
            string utcText,
            int localOffsetMinutes,
            InvoiceCustomerDraft customer,
            string placeOfSupplyStateCode,
            CalculatedInvoice calculated)
        {
            const string sql = @"
INSERT INTO invoice(
    invoice_id, invoice_number, financial_year, issued_at_utc, issued_local_offset_minutes,
    customer_name, customer_phone, customer_address, customer_gstin, place_of_supply_state_code,
    subtotal_paise, discount_paise, taxable_value_paise, cgst_paise, sgst_paise, igst_paise,
    round_off_paise, grand_total_paise, status, cancellation_reason, created_at_utc)
VALUES(
    @id, @number, @fy, @issued_utc, @offset_min,
    @cust_name, @cust_phone, @cust_addr, @cust_gstin, @pos_state,
    @subtotal, @discount, @taxable, @cgst, @sgst, @igst,
    @round_off, @grand_total, 'ISSUED', NULL, @created_utc);";

            using (var command = new SQLiteCommand(sql, connection, transaction))
            {
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@id", invoiceId);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@number", invoiceNumber);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@fy", financialYear);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@issued_utc", utcText);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@offset_min", localOffsetMinutes);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@cust_name", DbNullable(customer != null ? customer.Name : null));
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@cust_phone", DbNullable(customer != null ? customer.Phone : null));
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@cust_addr", DbNullable(customer != null ? customer.Address : null));
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@cust_gstin", DbNullable(customer != null ? NormalizeGstin(customer.Gstin) : null));
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@pos_state", DbNullable(placeOfSupplyStateCode));
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@subtotal", calculated.SubtotalPaise);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@discount", calculated.DiscountPaise);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@taxable", calculated.TaxableValuePaise);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@cgst", calculated.CgstPaise);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@sgst", calculated.SgstPaise);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@igst", calculated.IgstPaise);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@round_off", calculated.RoundOffPaise);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@grand_total", calculated.GrandTotalPaise);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@created_utc", utcText);
                command.ExecuteNonQuery();
            }
        }

        private static void InsertInvoiceLine(
            SQLiteConnection connection,
            SQLiteTransaction transaction,
            string invoiceId,
            string lineGroupId,
            string medicineId,
            string medicineNameSnapshot,
            CalculatedBatchLine batchLine,
            string utcText)
        {
            const string sql = @"
INSERT INTO invoice_line(
    invoice_line_id, invoice_id, line_group_id, medicine_id, batch_id,
    medicine_name_snapshot, batch_number_snapshot, expiry_yyyymm_snapshot,
    quantity_milli, unit_name_snapshot, unit_price_paise, discount_paise,
    taxable_value_paise, gst_rate_basis_points, hsn_code_snapshot,
    cgst_paise, sgst_paise, igst_paise, line_total_paise, created_at_utc)
VALUES(
    @id, @invoice_id, @group_id, @medicine_id, @batch_id,
    @med_name, @batch_num, @expiry,
    @qty, @unit_name, @unit_price, @discount,
    @taxable, @gst_bps, @hsn,
    @cgst, @sgst, @igst, @line_total, @created_utc);";

            using (var command = new SQLiteCommand(sql, connection, transaction))
            {
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@id", Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N"));
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@invoice_id", invoiceId);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@group_id", lineGroupId);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@medicine_id", medicineId);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@batch_id", batchLine.BatchId);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@med_name", medicineNameSnapshot);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@batch_num", batchLine.BatchNumber);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@expiry", batchLine.ExpiryYyyyMm);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@qty", batchLine.QuantityMilli);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@unit_name", batchLine.StockUnitName);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@unit_price", batchLine.UnitPricePaise);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@discount", batchLine.DiscountPaise);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@taxable", batchLine.TaxableValuePaise);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@gst_bps", batchLine.GstRateBasisPoints);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@hsn", DbNullable(batchLine.HsnCode));
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@cgst", batchLine.CgstPaise);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@sgst", batchLine.SgstPaise);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@igst", batchLine.IgstPaise);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@line_total", batchLine.LineTotalPaise);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@created_utc", utcText);
                command.ExecuteNonQuery();
            }
        }

        private static void DeductBatchStock(
            SQLiteConnection connection,
            SQLiteTransaction transaction,
            string batchId,
            long deductQuantityMilli,
            int currentExpiryYearMonth,
            string utcText)
        {
            const string sql = @"
UPDATE stock_batch
SET quantity_milli = quantity_milli - @deduct,
    updated_at_utc = @updated
WHERE batch_id = @batch_id
  AND batch_status = 'ACTIVE'
  AND expiry_yyyymm >= @current_month
  AND quantity_milli >= @deduct;";

            using (var command = new SQLiteCommand(sql, connection, transaction))
            {
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@deduct", deductQuantityMilli);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@updated", utcText);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@batch_id", batchId);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@current_month", currentExpiryYearMonth);

                int affected = command.ExecuteNonQuery();
                if (affected != 1)
                {
                    throw new InvalidOperationException("Batch stock changed or became ineligible during invoice finalisation.");
                }
            }
        }

        private static void InsertSaleStockMovement(
            SQLiteConnection connection,
            SQLiteTransaction transaction,
            string batchId,
            string medicineId,
            long deductedQuantityMilli,
            string invoiceId,
            string invoiceNumber,
            string utcText,
            string localText)
        {
            const string sql = @"
INSERT INTO stock_movement(
    movement_id, batch_id, medicine_id, movement_type, quantity_delta_milli,
    reference_type, reference_id, reason, created_at_utc, created_local_text)
VALUES(
    @id, @batch_id, @medicine_id, 'SALE', @delta,
    'INVOICE', @ref_id, @reason, @utc, @local);";

            using (var command = new SQLiteCommand(sql, connection, transaction))
            {
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@id", Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N"));
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@batch_id", batchId);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@medicine_id", medicineId);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@delta", checked(-deductedQuantityMilli));
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@ref_id", invoiceId);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@reason", "Sale invoice " + invoiceNumber);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@utc", utcText);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@local", localText);
                command.ExecuteNonQuery();
            }
        }

        private static void InsertPayment(
            SQLiteConnection connection,
            SQLiteTransaction transaction,
            string invoiceId,
            ValidatedTender tender,
            string utcText)
        {
            const string sql = @"
INSERT INTO payment(payment_id, invoice_id, method, amount_paise, reference_text, paid_at_utc)
VALUES(@id, @invoice_id, @method, @amount, @reference, @paid_utc);";

            using (var command = new SQLiteCommand(sql, connection, transaction))
            {
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@id", Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N"));
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@invoice_id", invoiceId);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@method", tender.DatabaseMethod);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@amount", tender.AmountPaise);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@reference", DbNullable(tender.ReferenceText));
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@paid_utc", utcText);
                command.ExecuteNonQuery();
            }
        }

        private static void InsertAuditLog(
            SQLiteConnection connection,
            SQLiteTransaction transaction,
            string invoiceId,
            string invoiceNumber,
            CalculatedInvoice calculated,
            List<Dictionary<string, object>> beforeBatches,
            List<Dictionary<string, object>> afterBatches,
            List<Dictionary<string, object>> payments,
            string utcText,
            string localText)
        {
            var serializer = new JavaScriptSerializer();
            string beforeJson = serializer.Serialize(new Dictionary<string, object>
            {
                { "batches", beforeBatches }
            });
            string afterJson = serializer.Serialize(new Dictionary<string, object>
            {
                { "invoiceId", invoiceId },
                { "invoiceNumber", invoiceNumber },
                { "grandTotalPaise", calculated.GrandTotalPaise },
                { "taxableValuePaise", calculated.TaxableValuePaise },
                { "cgstPaise", calculated.CgstPaise },
                { "sgstPaise", calculated.SgstPaise },
                { "igstPaise", calculated.IgstPaise },
                { "roundOffPaise", calculated.RoundOffPaise },
                { "payments", payments },
                { "batches", afterBatches }
            });

            string summary = string.Format(
                CultureInfo.InvariantCulture,
                "Issued invoice {0} for Rs. {1} ({2} medicine line(s)).",
                invoiceNumber,
                InvoiceCalculator.FormatRupees(calculated.GrandTotalPaise),
                calculated.Lines.Count);

            const string sql = @"
INSERT INTO audit_log(
    audit_id, action_type, entity_type, entity_id, summary,
    before_json, after_json, created_at_utc, created_local_text)
VALUES(
    @id, 'INVOICE_ISSUED', 'INVOICE', @entity_id, @summary,
    @before_json, @after_json, @utc, @local);";

            using (var command = new SQLiteCommand(sql, connection, transaction))
            {
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@id", Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N"));
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@entity_id", invoiceId);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@summary", summary);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@before_json", beforeJson);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@after_json", afterJson);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@utc", utcText);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@local", localText);
                command.ExecuteNonQuery();
            }
        }

        private static void MarkStockSnapshotDirty(SQLiteConnection connection, SQLiteTransaction transaction, string utcText)
        {
            const string sql = @"
INSERT INTO app_setting(setting_key, setting_value, updated_at_utc)
VALUES('stock_snapshot_dirty', '1', @updated)
ON CONFLICT(setting_key) DO UPDATE SET setting_value = '1', updated_at_utc = excluded.updated_at_utc;";

            using (var command = new SQLiteCommand(sql, connection, transaction))
            {
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@updated", utcText);
                command.ExecuteNonQuery();
            }
        }

        private static void InvokeCheckpoint(BillingWriteCheckpoint checkpoint, string name)
        {
            if (checkpoint != null)
            {
                checkpoint(name);
            }
        }

        private static string BuildAvailabilityMessage(long availableMilli, string unitName, bool isLowStock)
        {
            if (availableMilli <= 0)
            {
                return "Out of stock";
            }

            string qtyText = InvoiceCalculator.FormatQuantity(availableMilli);
            string unitText = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(unitName) ? "units" : unitName.Trim();
            if (isLowStock)
            {
                return string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "Low stock: {0} {1} available", qtyText, unitText);
            }

            return string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "{0} {1} available", qtyText, unitText);
        }

        private static string NormalizeSearchText(string value)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value)) return string.Empty;
            var parts = value.Trim().Split(new[] { ' ', '\t', '\r', '\n' }, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
            return string.Join(" ", parts).ToUpperInvariant();
        }

        private static string NormalizeGstin(string gstin)
        {
            return string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(gstin) ? null : gstin.Trim().ToUpperInvariant();
        }

        private static object DbNullable(string value)
        {
            return string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value) ? (object)DBNull.Value : value.Trim();
        }

        private static string ReadNullableString(SQLiteDataReader reader, int ordinal)
        {
            return reader.IsDBNull(ordinal) ? null : reader.GetString(ordinal);
        }
    }
}

================================================================================
FILE: ORVA/src/Orva.Desktop/Infrastructure/Repositories/InventoryRepository.cs
================================================================================
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Data.SQLite;
using System.Globalization;
using Orva.Domain;
using Orva.Infrastructure.Database;

namespace Orva.Infrastructure.Repositories
{
    public sealed class InventoryRepository
    {
        private readonly SqliteDatabase _database;
        public InventoryRepository(SqliteDatabase database) { _database = database; }

        public string SaveStockEntry(StockEntryInput input)
        {
            Validate(input);
            var nowUtc = DateTime.UtcNow;
            var now = nowUtc.ToString("o", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
            var medicineId = Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N");
            var batchId = Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N");
            var purchaseId = Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N");
            var movementId = Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N");
            var auditId = Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N");
            var normalizedName = NormalizeName(input.MedicineName);

            using (var connection = _database.OpenConnection())
            using (var transaction = connection.BeginTransaction())
            {
                medicineId = FindOrCreateMedicine(connection, transaction, input, normalizedName, now);
                batchId = FindOrCreateBatch(connection, transaction, medicineId, input, now);

                using (var purchase = new SQLiteCommand(@"
INSERT INTO purchase_receipt(purchase_id, received_at_utc, supplier_name_text, supplier_licence_text, document_type, document_reference, subtotal_paise, total_tax_paise, total_paise, created_at_utc)
VALUES(@id, @received, @supplier, @supplier_licence, @document_type, @document_reference, @subtotal, 0, @total, @created);", connection, transaction))
                {
                    purchase.Parameters.AddWithValue("@id", purchaseId);
                    purchase.Parameters.AddWithValue("@received", now);
                    purchase.Parameters.AddWithValue("@supplier", Db(input.SupplierName));
                    purchase.Parameters.AddWithValue("@supplier_licence", Db(input.SupplierLicence));
                    purchase.Parameters.AddWithValue("@document_type", input.DocumentType);
                    purchase.Parameters.AddWithValue("@document_reference", Db(input.DocumentReference));
                    purchase.Parameters.AddWithValue("@subtotal", MultiplyMoney(input.QuantityMilli, input.PurchasePricePaise));
                    purchase.Parameters.AddWithValue("@total", MultiplyMoney(input.QuantityMilli, input.PurchasePricePaise));
                    purchase.Parameters.AddWithValue("@created", now);
                    purchase.ExecuteNonQuery();
                }

                using (var line = new SQLiteCommand(@"
INSERT INTO purchase_line(purchase_line_id, purchase_id, batch_id, medicine_id, quantity_milli, unit_name_snapshot, unit_purchase_price_paise, line_total_paise, created_at_utc)
VALUES(@id, @purchase, @batch, @medicine, @quantity, @unit, @price, @total, @created);", connection, transaction))
                {
                    line.Parameters.AddWithValue("@id", Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N"));
                    line.Parameters.AddWithValue("@purchase", purchaseId);
                    line.Parameters.AddWithValue("@batch", batchId);
                    line.Parameters.AddWithValue("@medicine", medicineId);
                    line.Parameters.AddWithValue("@quantity", input.QuantityMilli);
                    line.Parameters.AddWithValue("@unit", input.StockUnitName.Trim());
                    line.Parameters.AddWithValue("@price", input.PurchasePricePaise);
                    line.Parameters.AddWithValue("@total", MultiplyMoney(input.QuantityMilli, input.PurchasePricePaise));
                    line.Parameters.AddWithValue("@created", now);
                    line.ExecuteNonQuery();
                }

                using (var movement = new SQLiteCommand(@"
INSERT INTO stock_movement(movement_id, batch_id, medicine_id, movement_type, quantity_delta_milli, reference_type, reference_id, reason, created_at_utc, created_local_text)
VALUES(@id, @batch, @medicine, 'PURCHASE', @quantity, 'PURCHASE_RECEIPT', @reference, 'Stock received', @utc, @local);", connection, transaction))
                {
                    movement.Parameters.AddWithValue("@id", movementId);
                    movement.Parameters.AddWithValue("@batch", batchId);
                    movement.Parameters.AddWithValue("@medicine", medicineId);
                    movement.Parameters.AddWithValue("@quantity", input.QuantityMilli);
                    movement.Parameters.AddWithValue("@reference", purchaseId);
                    movement.Parameters.AddWithValue("@utc", now);
                    movement.Parameters.AddWithValue("@local", nowUtc.ToLocalTime().ToString("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
                    movement.ExecuteNonQuery();
                }

                using (var audit = new SQLiteCommand(@"
INSERT INTO audit_log(audit_id, action_type, entity_type, entity_id, summary, before_json, after_json, created_at_utc, created_local_text)
VALUES(@id, 'STOCK_RECEIVED', 'STOCK_BATCH', @entity, @summary, NULL, @after, @utc, @local);", connection, transaction))
                {
                    audit.Parameters.AddWithValue("@id", auditId);
                    audit.Parameters.AddWithValue("@entity", batchId);
                    audit.Parameters.AddWithValue("@summary", "Received " + FormatQuantity(input.QuantityMilli) + " " + input.StockUnitName + " of " + input.MedicineName.Trim());
                    audit.Parameters.AddWithValue("@after", "{\"purchaseId\":\"" + purchaseId + "\",\"movementId\":\"" + movementId + "\"}");
                    audit.Parameters.AddWithValue("@utc", now);
                    audit.Parameters.AddWithValue("@local", nowUtc.ToLocalTime().ToString("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
                    audit.ExecuteNonQuery();
                }

                // Persist the export-dirty marker in the same transaction as the stock receipt.
                // If the process exits immediately after commit, the next startup knows to rebuild StockData.json.
                using (var dirty = new SQLiteCommand(@"
INSERT INTO app_setting(setting_key, setting_value, updated_at_utc) VALUES('stock_snapshot_dirty', '1', @updated)
ON CONFLICT(setting_key) DO UPDATE SET setting_value='1', updated_at_utc=excluded.updated_at_utc;", connection, transaction))
                {
                    dirty.Parameters.AddWithValue("@updated", now);
                    dirty.ExecuteNonQuery();
                }

                transaction.Commit();
            }
            return batchId;
        }

        public IList<InventoryRow> GetInventoryRows(string searchText)
        {
            var rows = new List<InventoryRow>();
            var sql = @"
SELECT m.medicine_id, b.batch_id, m.display_name, b.batch_number, b.expiry_yyyymm,
       b.quantity_milli, b.stock_unit_name, b.mrp_paise, b.batch_status,
       m.low_stock_threshold_milli,
       COALESCE((SELECT SUM(b2.quantity_milli) FROM stock_batch b2
                 WHERE b2.medicine_id=m.medicine_id AND b2.batch_status='ACTIVE'
                   AND b2.expiry_yyyymm >= @current_month), 0) AS usable_total
FROM stock_batch b
JOIN medicine m ON m.medicine_id = b.medicine_id
WHERE m.is_active = 1
  AND (@search = '' OR m.normalized_name LIKE @like OR UPPER(b.batch_number) LIKE @like)
ORDER BY m.display_name COLLATE NOCASE, b.expiry_yyyymm, b.batch_number;";
            using (var connection = _database.OpenConnection())
            using (var command = new SQLiteCommand(sql, connection))
            {
                var search = (searchText ?? string.Empty).Trim().ToUpperInvariant();
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@search", search);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@like", "%" + search + "%");
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@current_month", DateTime.Now.Year * 100 + DateTime.Now.Month);
                using (var reader = command.ExecuteReader())
                {
                    while (reader.Read())
                    {
                        var expiry = reader.GetInt32(4);
                        var quantity = reader.GetInt64(5);
                        var status = GetDisplayStatus(reader.GetString(8), expiry, quantity, reader.GetInt64(9), reader.GetInt64(10));
                        rows.Add(new InventoryRow
                        {
                            MedicineId = reader.GetString(0),
                            BatchId = reader.GetString(1),
                            MedicineName = reader.GetString(2),
                            BatchNumber = reader.GetString(3),
                            Expiry = expiry.ToString("0000-00", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture),
                            Quantity = FormatQuantity(quantity),
                            Unit = reader.GetString(6),
                            Mrp = FormatMoney(reader.GetInt64(7)),
                            Status = status,
                            StatusColour = status == "Expired" ? "#C9362B" : (status == "Low stock" ? "#B56A13" : "#16845B")
                        });
                    }
                }
            }
            return rows;
        }

        public string GetStockUnitForMedicine(string medicineName)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(medicineName)) return null;
            var normalized = NormalizeName(medicineName);
            using (var connection = _database.OpenConnection())
            using (var command = new SQLiteCommand("SELECT stock_unit_name FROM medicine WHERE normalized_name=@name AND is_active=1;", connection))
            {
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@name", normalized);
                return command.ExecuteScalar() as string;
            }
        }

        public int GetMedicineCount()
        {
            using (var connection = _database.OpenConnection())
            using (var command = new SQLiteCommand("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM medicine WHERE is_active = 1;", connection))
                return Convert.ToInt32(command.ExecuteScalar());
        }

        public int GetBatchCount()
        {
            using (var connection = _database.OpenConnection())
            using (var command = new SQLiteCommand("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM stock_batch;", connection))
                return Convert.ToInt32(command.ExecuteScalar());
        }

        private static string FindOrCreateMedicine(SQLiteConnection connection, SQLiteTransaction transaction, StockEntryInput input, string normalizedName, string now)
        {
            using (var find = new SQLiteCommand("SELECT medicine_id, stock_unit_name FROM medicine WHERE normalized_name = @name;", connection, transaction))
            {
                find.Parameters.AddWithValue("@name", normalizedName);
                using (var reader = find.ExecuteReader())
                {
                    if (reader.Read())
                    {
                        var existingId = reader.GetString(0);
                        var existingUnit = reader.GetString(1);
                        if (!string.Equals(existingUnit, input.StockUnitName.Trim(), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                            throw new InvalidOperationException("This medicine is already recorded using the unit '" + existingUnit + "'. Use that unit for this stock entry.");
                        return existingId;
                    }
                }
            }

            var id = Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N");
            using (var insert = new SQLiteCommand(@"
INSERT INTO medicine(medicine_id, display_name, normalized_name, stock_unit_name, created_at_utc, updated_at_utc)
VALUES(@id, @name, @normalized, @unit, @created, @updated);", connection, transaction))
            {
                insert.Parameters.AddWithValue("@id", id);
                insert.Parameters.AddWithValue("@name", input.MedicineName.Trim());
                insert.Parameters.AddWithValue("@normalized", normalizedName);
                insert.Parameters.AddWithValue("@unit", input.StockUnitName.Trim());
                insert.Parameters.AddWithValue("@created", now);
                insert.Parameters.AddWithValue("@updated", now);
                insert.ExecuteNonQuery();
            }
            return id;
        }

        private static string FindOrCreateBatch(SQLiteConnection connection, SQLiteTransaction transaction, string medicineId, StockEntryInput input, string now)
        {
            string id = null;
            using (var find = new SQLiteCommand(@"SELECT batch_id, batch_status FROM stock_batch WHERE medicine_id=@medicine AND UPPER(TRIM(batch_number))=UPPER(TRIM(@batch)) AND expiry_yyyymm=@expiry;", connection, transaction))
            {
                find.Parameters.AddWithValue("@medicine", medicineId);
                find.Parameters.AddWithValue("@batch", input.BatchNumber.Trim());
                find.Parameters.AddWithValue("@expiry", input.ExpiryYearMonth);
                using (var reader = find.ExecuteReader())
                {
                    if (reader.Read())
                    {
                        id = reader.GetString(0);
                        var status = reader.GetString(1);
                        if (!string.Equals(status, "ACTIVE", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                            throw new InvalidOperationException("This batch is marked as " + status.ToLowerInvariant() + ". It must be checked before more stock is added.");
                    }
                }
            }

            if (id == null)
            {
                id = Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N");
                using (var insert = new SQLiteCommand(@"
INSERT INTO stock_batch(batch_id, medicine_id, batch_number, expiry_yyyymm, stock_unit_name, mrp_paise, latest_purchase_price_paise, quantity_milli, batch_status, created_at_utc, updated_at_utc)
VALUES(@id, @medicine, @batch, @expiry, @unit, @mrp, @purchase, @quantity, 'ACTIVE', @created, @updated);", connection, transaction))
                {
                    insert.Parameters.AddWithValue("@id", id);
                    insert.Parameters.AddWithValue("@medicine", medicineId);
                    insert.Parameters.AddWithValue("@batch", input.BatchNumber.Trim());
                    insert.Parameters.AddWithValue("@expiry", input.ExpiryYearMonth);
                    insert.Parameters.AddWithValue("@unit", input.StockUnitName.Trim());
                    insert.Parameters.AddWithValue("@mrp", input.MrpPaise);
                    insert.Parameters.AddWithValue("@purchase", input.PurchasePricePaise);
                    insert.Parameters.AddWithValue("@quantity", input.QuantityMilli);
                    insert.Parameters.AddWithValue("@created", now);
                    insert.Parameters.AddWithValue("@updated", now);
                    insert.ExecuteNonQuery();
                }
            }
            else
            {
                using (var update = new SQLiteCommand(@"
UPDATE stock_batch SET quantity_milli = quantity_milli + @quantity,
mrp_paise=@mrp, latest_purchase_price_paise=@purchase, updated_at_utc=@updated
WHERE batch_id=@id;", connection, transaction))
                {
                    update.Parameters.AddWithValue("@quantity", input.QuantityMilli);
                    update.Parameters.AddWithValue("@mrp", input.MrpPaise);
                    update.Parameters.AddWithValue("@purchase", input.PurchasePricePaise);
                    update.Parameters.AddWithValue("@updated", now);
                    update.Parameters.AddWithValue("@id", id);
                    update.ExecuteNonQuery();
                }
            }
            return id;
        }

        private static void Validate(StockEntryInput input)
        {
            if (input == null) throw new ArgumentNullException("input");
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(input.MedicineName)) throw new ArgumentException("Enter the medicine name.");
            if (input.MedicineName.Trim().Length > 180) throw new ArgumentException("Medicine name is too long.");
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(input.StockUnitName)) throw new ArgumentException("Choose the stock unit.");
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(input.BatchNumber)) throw new ArgumentException("Enter the batch number.");
            if (input.BatchNumber.Trim().Length > 80) throw new ArgumentException("Batch number is too long.");
            if (input.ExpiryYearMonth < 200001 || input.ExpiryYearMonth > 219912) throw new ArgumentException("Choose a valid expiry month.");
            var currentMonth = DateTime.Now.Year * 100 + DateTime.Now.Month;
            if (input.ExpiryYearMonth < currentMonth) throw new ArgumentException("This batch has already passed its expiry month. Check the date before receiving it.");
            if (input.QuantityMilli <= 0) throw new ArgumentException("Enter a quantity greater than zero.");
            if (input.PurchasePricePaise < 0 || input.MrpPaise < 0) throw new ArgumentException("Prices cannot be negative.");
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(input.DocumentType)) input.DocumentType = "NO_DOCUMENT";
            var allowed = new[] { "TAX_INVOICE", "CASH_CREDIT_MEMO", "NO_DOCUMENT", "OTHER" };
            if (Array.IndexOf(allowed, input.DocumentType) < 0) throw new ArgumentException("Choose a valid purchase document type.");
        }

        private static string GetDisplayStatus(string batchStatus, int expiryYyyyMm, long quantityMilli, long thresholdMilli, long usableTotalMilli)
        {
            var now = DateTime.Now;
            var expiryDate = new DateTime(expiryYyyyMm / 100, expiryYyyyMm % 100, 1).AddMonths(1).AddDays(-1);
            if (batchStatus == "QUARANTINED") return "Quarantined";
            if (batchStatus == "BLOCKED") return "Blocked";
            if (expiryDate < now.Date || batchStatus == "EXPIRED") return "Expired";
            if (quantityMilli <= 0) return "Out of stock";
            if (thresholdMilli > 0 && usableTotalMilli <= thresholdMilli) return "Low stock";
            return "Available";
        }

        private static string NormalizeName(string value)
        {
            return string.Join(" ", value.Trim().Split(new[] { ' ', '\t', '\r', '\n' }, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries)).ToUpperInvariant();
        }

        private static object Db(string value) { return string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value) ? (object)DBNull.Value : value.Trim(); }
        private static long MultiplyMoney(long quantityMilli, long pricePaise)
        {
            return checked((long)Math.Round((decimal)quantityMilli * pricePaise / 1000m, 0, MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero));
        }
        private static string FormatMoney(long paise) { return (paise / 100m).ToString("N2", CultureInfo.GetCultureInfo("en-IN")); }
        private static string FormatQuantity(long quantityMilli) { return (quantityMilli / 1000m).ToString("0.###", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture); }
    }
}

FILE: ORVA/src/Orva.Desktop/Infrastructure/Repositories/ShopRepository.cs
================================================================================
using System;
using System.Data.SQLite;
using Orva.Domain;
using Orva.Infrastructure.Database;

namespace Orva.Infrastructure.Repositories
{
    public sealed class ShopRepository
    {
        private readonly SqliteDatabase _database;

        public ShopRepository(SqliteDatabase database) { _database = database; }

        public ShopProfile GetProfile()
        {
            using (var connection = _database.OpenConnection())
            using (var command = new SQLiteCommand("SELECT shop_id, shop_name, legal_name, address, phone, email, state_name, state_code, gstin, drug_licence_number, invoice_prefix FROM shop_profile WHERE profile_id = 1;", connection))
            using (var reader = command.ExecuteReader())
            {
                if (!reader.Read()) return null;
                return new ShopProfile
                {
                    ShopId = reader.GetString(0),
                    ShopName = reader.GetString(1),
                    LegalName = ReadNullable(reader, 2),
                    Address = ReadNullable(reader, 3),
                    Phone = ReadNullable(reader, 4),
                    Email = ReadNullable(reader, 5),
                    StateName = ReadNullable(reader, 6),
                    StateCode = ReadNullable(reader, 7),
                    Gstin = ReadNullable(reader, 8),
                    DrugLicenceNumber = ReadNullable(reader, 9),
                    InvoicePrefix = reader.GetString(10)
                };
            }
        }

        public void SaveProfile(ShopProfile profile)
        {
            if (profile == null) throw new ArgumentNullException("profile");
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(profile.ShopName)) throw new ArgumentException("Enter the shop name.");
            var now = DateTime.UtcNow.ToString("o");
            using (var connection = _database.OpenConnection())
            using (var transaction = connection.BeginTransaction())
            using (var command = new SQLiteCommand(@"
INSERT INTO shop_profile(profile_id, shop_id, shop_name, legal_name, address, phone, email, state_name, state_code, gstin, drug_licence_number, invoice_prefix, created_at_utc, updated_at_utc)
VALUES(1, @shop_id, @shop_name, @legal_name, @address, @phone, @email, @state_name, @state_code, @gstin, @drug_licence, @invoice_prefix, @created, @updated)
ON CONFLICT(profile_id) DO UPDATE SET shop_name=excluded.shop_name, legal_name=excluded.legal_name, address=excluded.address, phone=excluded.phone, email=excluded.email, state_name=excluded.state_name, state_code=excluded.state_code, gstin=excluded.gstin, drug_licence_number=excluded.drug_licence_number, invoice_prefix=excluded.invoice_prefix, updated_at_utc=excluded.updated_at_utc;", connection, transaction))
            {
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@shop_id", string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(profile.ShopId) ? Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N") : profile.ShopId);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@shop_name", profile.ShopName.Trim());
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@legal_name", Db(profile.LegalName));
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@address", Db(profile.Address));
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@phone", Db(profile.Phone));
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@email", Db(profile.Email));
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@state_name", Db(profile.StateName));
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@state_code", Db(profile.StateCode));
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@gstin", Db(profile.Gstin));
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@drug_licence", Db(profile.DrugLicenceNumber));
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@invoice_prefix", string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(profile.InvoicePrefix) ? "INV" : profile.InvoicePrefix.Trim());
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@created", now);
                command.Parameters.AddWithValue("@updated", now);
                command.ExecuteNonQuery();
                transaction.Commit();
            }
        }

        private static object Db(string value) { return string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value) ? (object)DBNull.Value : value.Trim(); }
        private static string ReadNullable(SQLiteDataReader reader, int ordinal) { return reader.IsDBNull(ordinal) ? null : reader.GetString(ordinal); }
    }
}

using System;
using System.Windows;
using Orva.Services;
using Orva.ViewModels;
using Orva.Views;

namespace Orva
{
    public partial class MainWindow : Window
    {
        public MainWindow()
        {
            InitializeComponent();

            var profile = App.Services.ShopRepository.GetProfile();
            ShopNameText.Text = profile == null ? "Medical store" : profile.ShopName;
            CurrentDateText.Text = DateTime.Now.ToString("dd MMM yyyy").ToUpperInvariant();

            ApplyLanguageSelection();
            ShowBilling();
        }

        private void ApplyLanguageSelection()
        {
            EnglishButton.Background = LocalizationManager.CurrentLanguage == "en"
                ? (System.Windows.Media.Brush)FindResource("Paper")
                : System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent;

            BengaliButton.Background = LocalizationManager.CurrentLanguage == "bn"
                ? (System.Windows.Media.Brush)FindResource("Paper")
                : System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent;
        }

        private void SetPage(string title, string eyebrow, FrameworkElement page)
        {
            PageTitle.Text = title;
            PageEyebrow.Text = eyebrow;
            PageHost.Content = page;
        }

        private void ShowBilling()
        {
            var profile = App.Services.ShopRepository.GetProfile();
            var header = BillingShopHeader.FromProfile(profile);

            SetPage(
                LocalizationManager.Get("PageBillingTitle"),
                LocalizationManager.Get("PageBillingEyebrow"),
                new BillingView(App.Services.BillingService, header));
        }

        private void ShowInventory()
        {
            SetPage(
                LocalizationManager.Get("PageInventoryTitle"),
                LocalizationManager.Get("PageInventoryEyebrow"),
                new InventoryView());
        }

        private void Inventory_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e) { ShowInventory(); }
        private void Billing_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e) { ShowBilling(); }

        private void Invoices_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            SetPage("Invoices", "ORVA  /  SALES RECORDS",
                new ModuleNoticeView("The invoice archive will be connected to the permanent billing records."));
        }

        private void Returns_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            SetPage("Sales Returns", "ORVA  /  RETURNS",
                new ModuleNoticeView("Returns will be linked to original invoices. Returned stock will remain quarantined until assessed."));
        }

        private void Records_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            SetPage("Records & Tax", "ORVA  /  BUSINESS RECORDS",
                new ModuleNoticeView("Sales, purchase, GST and export registers will be connected to permanent transaction records."));
        }

        private void Settings_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            SetPage("Settings", "ORVA  /  SHOP CONFIGURATION",
                new ModuleNoticeView("Shop details, CA-reviewed tax setup, printer settings and backup tools will be managed here."));
        }

        private void Subscription_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            SetPage("Subscription & Support", "ORVA  /  LICENCE",
                new ModuleNoticeView("Subscription plans, UPI payment and support remain separate from billing."));
        }

        private void English_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            LocalizationManager.SetLanguage("en");
            App.Services.AppSettings.Set("display_language", "en");
            ApplyLanguageSelection();
            ShowBilling();
        }

        private void Bengali_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            LocalizationManager.SetLanguage("bn");
            App.Services.AppSettings.Set("display_language", "bn");
            ApplyLanguageSelection();
            ShowBilling();
        }
    }
}

FILE: ORVA/src/Orva.Desktop/Services/Billing/BillingClock.cs

using System;

namespace Orva.Services.Billing
{
    public interface IBillingClock
    {
        DateTime UtcNow { get; }
        int LocalOffsetMinutes { get; }
        DateTime LocalNow { get; }
        int CurrentExpiryYearMonth { get; }
    }

    public sealed class SystemBillingClock : IBillingClock
    {
        public DateTime UtcNow
        {
            get { return DateTime.UtcNow; }
        }

        public int LocalOffsetMinutes
        {
            get
            {
                var offset = TimeZoneInfo.Local.GetUtcOffset(DateTime.UtcNow);
                return (int)offset.TotalMinutes;
            }
        }

        public DateTime LocalNow
        {
            get { return DateTime.Now; }
        }

        public int CurrentExpiryYearMonth
        {
            get
            {
                var local = DateTime.Now;
                return local.Year * 100 + local.Month;
            }
        }
    }
}
