    public enum TaxPricingMode
    {
        TaxInclusive = 1,
        TaxExclusive = 2
    }

    public enum SupplyType
    {
        IntraState = 1,
        InterState = 2
    }

    public enum RoundOffMode
    {
        None = 0,
        NearestRupee = 1
    }

    public static class BillingSettingKeys
    {
        public const string TaxConfigured = "billing.tax_configured";
        public const string TaxPricingMode = "billing.tax_pricing_mode";
        public const string DefaultSupplyType = "billing.default_supply_type";
        public const string RoundOffMode = "billing.round_off_mode";
    }

    public static class BillingIssueCodes

        public const string InvalidQuantity = "INVALID_QUANTITY";

        public const string MedicineInactive = "MEDICINE_INACTIVE";
        public const string InsufficientStock = "INSUFFICIENT_STOCK";

        public const string InvalidPaymentAmount = "INVALID_PAYMENT_AMOUNT";
        public const string PaymentShort = "PAYMENT_SHORT";
        public const string PaymentOverpaid = "PAYMENT_OVERPAID";
        public const string CalculationOverflow = "CALCULATION_OVERFLOW";
    }

================================================================================
FILE: ORVA/src/Orva.Desktop/Services/Billing/BillingService.cs
================================================================================
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Data;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Globalization;
using Orva.Domain.Billing;
using Orva.Infrastructure.Database;
using Orva.Infrastructure.Repositories;

namespace Orva.Services.Billing
{
    /// &lt;summary&gt;
    /// Production implementation of &lt;see cref=&quot;IBillingService&quot;/&gt;.
    /// Enforces FEFO allocation, explicit tax configuration safeguards, exact integer paise
    /// accounting, payment reconciliation, and atomic SQLite invoice persistence.
    /// &lt;/summary&gt;
    public sealed class BillingService : IBillingService
    {
        private readonly BillingRepository _repository;
        private readonly FefoAllocator _allocator;
        private readonly InvoiceCalculator _calculator;
        private readonly StockSnapshotWriter _snapshotWriter;
        private readonly IBillingClock _clock;
        private readonly BillingWriteCheckpoint _writeCheckpoint;

        public BillingService(
            SqliteDatabase database,
            StockSnapshotWriter snapshotWriter = null,
            IBillingClock clock = null)
            : this(new BillingRepository(database), snapshotWriter, clock, null)
        {
        }

        internal BillingService(
            BillingRepository repository,
            StockSnapshotWriter snapshotWriter,
            IBillingClock clock,
            BillingWriteCheckpoint writeCheckpoint)
        {
            if (repository == null) throw new ArgumentNullException("repository");
            _repository = repository;
            _allocator = new FefoAllocator();
            _calculator = new InvoiceCalculator();
            _snapshotWriter = snapshotWriter;
            _clock = clock ?? new SystemBillingClock();
            _writeCheckpoint = writeCheckpoint;
        }

        public IList<MedicineLookupResult> SearchMedicines(string query, int maximumResults)
        {
            if (maximumResults <= 0)
            {
                return new List<MedicineLookupResult>();
            }

            return _repository.SearchMedicines(query, maximumResults, _clock.CurrentExpiryYearMonth);
        }

        public InvoicePreviewResult PreviewInvoice(InvoiceDraft draft)
        {
            var mergedItems = new List<MergedDraftItem>();
            var issues = new List<BillingIssue>();
            var validatedTenders = new List<ValidatedTender>();
            long tenderTotalPaise = 0;

            ValidateAndMergeDraft(draft, mergedItems, validatedTenders, out tenderTotalPaise, issues);

            var medicineIds = CollectMedicineIds(mergedItems);
            int currentMonth = _clock.CurrentExpiryYearMonth;
            var snapshot = _repository.LoadReadOnlySnapshot(medicineIds, currentMonth);

            var evaluation = EvaluateAgainstSnapshot(
                draft,
                mergedItems,
                validatedTenders,
                tenderTotalPaise,
                snapshot,
                currentMonth,
                issues,
                requireExactPaymentForIssues: false);

            return BuildPreviewResult(evaluation);
        }

        public InvoiceResult FinalizeInvoice(InvoiceDraft draft)
        {
            var mergedItems = new List<MergedDraftItem>();
            var preIssues = new List<BillingIssue>();
            var validatedTenders = new List<ValidatedTender>();
            long tenderTotalPaise = 0;

            ValidateAndMergeDraft(draft, mergedItems, validatedTenders, out tenderTotalPaise, preIssues);

            if (mergedItems.Count == 0 || HasBlockingIssues(preIssues))
            {
                return FailedInvoiceResult(preIssues);
            }

            var medicineIds = CollectMedicineIds(mergedItems);
            CommittedInvoiceRecord committed = null;

            using (var connection = _repository.OpenWriteConnection())
            using (var transaction = connection.BeginTransaction(IsolationLevel.Serializable))
            {
                DateTime utcNow = _clock.UtcNow;
                int localOffsetMinutes = _clock.LocalOffsetMinutes;
                DateTime localNow = _clock.LocalNow;
                int currentMonth = localNow.Year * 100 + localNow.Month;

                var freshIssues = new List<BillingIssue>();
                var snapshot = _repository.LoadSnapshot(connection, transaction, medicineIds, currentMonth);

                var evaluation = EvaluateAgainstSnapshot(
                    draft,
                    mergedItems,
                    validatedTenders,
                    tenderTotalPaise,
                    snapshot,
                    currentMonth,
                    freshIssues,
                    requireExactPaymentForIssues: true);

                if (!evaluation.CanFinalize || evaluation.Calculated == null || evaluation.TaxPolicy == null)
                {
                    transaction.Rollback();
                    return FailedInvoiceResult(evaluation.Issues);
                }

                committed = _repository.PersistInvoiceInTransaction(
                    connection,
                    transaction,
                    snapshot.Shop,
                    evaluation.TaxPolicy,
                    evaluation.Calculated,
                    draft != null ? draft.Customer : null,
                    validatedTenders,
                    utcNow,
                    localOffsetMinutes,
                    localNow,
                    currentMonth,
                    _writeCheckpoint);

                transaction.Commit();
            }

            var result = new InvoiceResult
            {
                Succeeded = true,
                InvoiceId = committed.InvoiceId,
                InvoiceNumber = committed.InvoiceNumber,
                IssuedAtUtc = committed.IssuedAtUtc,
                IssuedLocalOffsetMinutes = committed.IssuedLocalOffsetMinutes,
                GrandTotalPaise = committed.GrandTotalPaise
            };

            TryRefreshStockSnapshot(result);
            return result;
        }

        private void TryRefreshStockSnapshot(InvoiceResult result)
        {
            if (_snapshotWriter == null) return;

            try
            {
                _snapshotWriter.WriteCurrentSnapshot();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                // The invoice transaction is already committed and stock_snapshot_dirty is already 1.
                // Never turn a committed invoice into a reported failure.
                Trace.TraceError("Stock snapshot refresh failed after committed invoice " + result.InvoiceNumber + ": " + ex);
                result.Warnings.Add("Invoice was saved, but the portable stock file could not be refreshed. Use Refresh stock file in Inventory & Stock.");
            }
        }

        private DraftEvaluation EvaluateAgainstSnapshot(
            InvoiceDraft draft,
            IList<MergedDraftItem> mergedItems,
            IList<ValidatedTender> validatedTenders,
            long tenderTotalPaise,
            BillingDataSnapshot snapshot,
            int currentExpiryYearMonth,
            List<BillingIssue> issues,
            bool requireExactPaymentForIssues)
        {
            var evaluation = new DraftEvaluation
            {
                Issues = issues,
                TenderTotalPaise = tenderTotalPaise
            };

            if (snapshot.Shop == null)
            {
                AddBlockingIssue(issues, BillingIssueCodes.ShopProfileMissing, "Shop profile setup must be completed before billing.", null);
            }

            ResolvedTaxPolicy taxPolicy = ResolveTaxPolicy(snapshot.Shop, snapshot.TaxSettings, draft != null ? draft.Customer : null, issues);
            evaluation.TaxPolicy = taxPolicy;

            var lineInputs = new List<InvoiceLineCalculationInput>();
            for (int i = 0; i < mergedItems.Count; i++)
            {
                var item = mergedItems[i];
                BillingMedicineRecord medicine;
                if (!snapshot.Medicines.TryGetValue(item.MedicineId, out medicine) || medicine == null)
                {
                    AddBlockingIssue(issues, BillingIssueCodes.MedicineNotFound, "One of the selected medicines could not be found.", item.MedicineId);
                    continue;
                }

                if (!medicine.IsActive)
                {
                    AddBlockingIssue(issues, BillingIssueCodes.MedicineInactive, medicine.DisplayName + " is no longer active for sale.", medicine.MedicineId);
                    continue;
                }

                if (!medicine.GstRateConfigured)
                {
                    AddBlockingIssue(
                        issues,
                        BillingIssueCodes.MedicineTaxNotConfigured,
                        "Tax setup needs to be completed for " + medicine.DisplayName + " in Settings. Please contact support.",
                        medicine.MedicineId);
                }

                List<BillingBatchRecord> batches;
                snapshot.EligibleBatchesByMedicine.TryGetValue(item.MedicineId, out batches);

                FefoAllocationOutcome allocationOutcome;
                try
                {
                    allocationOutcome = _allocator.Allocate(batches, item.QuantityMilli, currentExpiryYearMonth);
                }
                catch (OverflowException)
                {
                    AddBlockingIssue(issues, BillingIssueCodes.QuantityOverflow, "Requested quantity for " + medicine.DisplayName + " is too large.", medicine.MedicineId);
                    continue;
                }

                if (!allocationOutcome.Succeeded)
                {
                    string availabilityMessage = allocationOutcome.AvailableQuantityMilli <= 0
                        ? medicine.DisplayName + " is currently out of stock."
                        : string.Format(
                            CultureInfo.InvariantCulture,
                            "Only {0} {1} of {2} available.",
                            InvoiceCalculator.FormatQuantity(allocationOutcome.AvailableQuantityMilli),
                            medicine.StockUnitName,
                            medicine.DisplayName);

                    AddBlockingIssue(issues, BillingIssueCodes.InsufficientStock, availabilityMessage, medicine.MedicineId);
                    continue;
                }

                lineInputs.Add(new InvoiceLineCalculationInput
                {
                    Medicine = medicine,
                    RequestedQuantityMilli = item.QuantityMilli,
                    Allocations = allocationOutcome.Allocations
                });
            }

            if (lineInputs.Count > 0 && taxPolicy != null)
            {
                try
                {
                    evaluation.Calculated = _calculator.Calculate(lineInputs, taxPolicy);
                }
                catch (OverflowException)
                {
                    AddBlockingIssue(issues, BillingIssueCodes.CalculationOverflow, "Invoice amount exceeds supported numeric limits.", null);
                }
            }

            long grandTotal = evaluation.Calculated != null ? evaluation.Calculated.GrandTotalPaise : 0L;
            long remaining = checked(grandTotal - tenderTotalPaise);
            evaluation.AmountRemainingPaise = remaining;

            if (evaluation.Calculated != null)
            {
                if (tenderTotalPaise > grandTotal)
                {
                    long overpaid = checked(tenderTotalPaise - grandTotal);
                    AddBlockingIssue(
                        issues,
                        BillingIssueCodes.PaymentOverpaid,
                        "Payment exceeds the invoice total by Rs. " + InvoiceCalculator.FormatRupees(overpaid) + ".",
                        null);
                }
                else if (requireExactPaymentForIssues && tenderTotalPaise < grandTotal)
                {
                    long shortBy = checked(grandTotal - tenderTotalPaise);
                    AddBlockingIssue(
                        issues,
                        BillingIssueCodes.PaymentShort,
                        "Payment is short by Rs. " + InvoiceCalculator.FormatRupees(shortBy) + ".",
                        null);
                }
            }

            bool hasBlocking = HasBlockingIssues(issues);
            bool hasLines = mergedItems.Count > 0 && evaluation.Calculated != null && evaluation.Calculated.Lines.Count == mergedItems.Count;
            bool isFullyPaid = hasLines && remaining == 0 && (grandTotal == 0 || validatedTenders.Count > 0);

            evaluation.IsDraftValid = hasLines && !hasBlocking;
            evaluation.IsFullyPaid = isFullyPaid;
            evaluation.CanFinalize = evaluation.IsDraftValid && isFullyPaid;

            return evaluation;
        }

        private static ResolvedTaxPolicy ResolveTaxPolicy(
            BillingShopContext shop,
            BillingTaxSettings settings,
            InvoiceCustomerDraft customer,
            List<BillingIssue> issues)
        {
            const string taxSetupMessage = "Tax setup needs to be completed in Settings. Please contact support.";

            if (settings == null || !settings.IsConfigured)
            {
                AddBlockingIssue(issues, BillingIssueCodes.TaxConfigMissing, taxSetupMessage, null);
                return null;
            }

            TaxPricingMode pricingMode;
            if (!TryParsePricingMode(settings.PricingModeRaw, out pricingMode))
            {
                AddBlockingIssue(issues, BillingIssueCodes.TaxConfigMissing, taxSetupMessage, null);
                return null;
            }

            RoundOffMode roundOffMode;
            if (!TryParseRoundOffMode(settings.RoundOffModeRaw, out roundOffMode))
            {
                AddBlockingIssue(issues, BillingIssueCodes.TaxConfigMissing, taxSetupMessage, null);
                return null;
            }

            string customerGstin = customer != null && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(customer.Gstin)
                ? customer.Gstin.Trim().ToUpperInvariant()
                : null;

            string shopStateCode = shop != null && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(shop.StateCode)
                ? shop.StateCode.Trim()
                : null;

            SupplyType supplyType;
            string placeOfSupplyStateCode;

            if (customerGstin != null)
            {
                if (!IsValidGstinFormat(customerGstin))
                {
                    AddBlockingIssue(issues, BillingIssueCodes.CustomerGstinInvalid, "Enter a valid 15-character customer GSTIN or leave it blank.", null);
                    return null;
                }

                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(shopStateCode) || shopStateCode.Length != 2)
                {
                    AddBlockingIssue(issues, BillingIssueCodes.TaxConfigMissing, taxSetupMessage, null);
                    return null;
                }

                string customerStateCode = customerGstin.Substring(0, 2);
                placeOfSupplyStateCode = customerStateCode;
                supplyType = string.Equals(customerStateCode, shopStateCode, StringComparison.Ordinal)
                    ? SupplyType.IntraState
                    : SupplyType.InterState;
            }
            else
            {
                if (!TryParseSupplyType(settings.DefaultSupplyTypeRaw, out supplyType))
                {
                    AddBlockingIssue(issues, BillingIssueCodes.TaxConfigMissing, taxSetupMessage, null);
                    return null;
                }
                placeOfSupplyStateCode = shopStateCode;
            }

            return new ResolvedTaxPolicy
            {
                PricingMode = pricingMode,
                SupplyType = supplyType,
                RoundOffMode = roundOffMode,
                PlaceOfSupplyStateCode = placeOfSupplyStateCode
            };
        }

        private static void ValidateAndMergeDraft(
            InvoiceDraft draft,
            List<MergedDraftItem> mergedItems,
            List<ValidatedTender> validatedTenders,
            out long tenderTotalPaise,
            List<BillingIssue> issues)
        {
            tenderTotalPaise = 0;
            if (draft == null || draft.Items == null || draft.Items.Count == 0)
            {
                AddBlockingIssue(issues, BillingIssueCodes.DraftEmpty, "Add at least one medicine to create an invoice.", null);
            }
            else
            {
                var indexByMedicineId = new Dictionary<string, int>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
                for (int i = 0; i < draft.Items.Count; i++)
                {
                    var item = draft.Items[i];
                    if (item == null || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(item.MedicineId))
                    {
                        AddBlockingIssue(issues, BillingIssueCodes.InvalidItem, "Each invoice line must reference a valid medicine.", null);
                        continue;
                    }

                    string medicineId = item.MedicineId.Trim();
                    if (item.QuantityMilli <= 0)
                    {
                        AddBlockingIssue(issues, BillingIssueCodes.InvalidQuantity, "Enter a quantity greater than zero.", medicineId);
                        continue;
                    }

                    int existingIndex;
                    if (indexByMedicineId.TryGetValue(medicineId, out existingIndex))
                    {
                        try
                        {
                            mergedItems[existingIndex].QuantityMilli = checked(mergedItems[existingIndex].QuantityMilli + item.QuantityMilli);
                        }
                        catch (OverflowException)
                        {
                            AddBlockingIssue(issues, BillingIssueCodes.QuantityOverflow, "Combined quantity for this medicine is too large.", medicineId);
                        }
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        indexByMedicineId.Add(medicineId, mergedItems.Count);
                        mergedItems.Add(new MergedDraftItem
                        {
                            MedicineId = medicineId,
                            QuantityMilli = item.QuantityMilli
                        });
                    }
                }
            }

            if (draft != null && draft.Tenders != null)
            {
                for (int i = 0; i < draft.Tenders.Count; i++)
                {
                    var tender = draft.Tenders[i];
                    if (tender == null)
                    {
                        AddBlockingIssue(issues, BillingIssueCodes.InvalidPaymentAmount, "Remove empty payment entries before finalising.", null);
                        continue;
                    }

                    string dbMethod;
                    if (!TryMapPaymentMethod(tender.Method, out dbMethod))
                    {
                        AddBlockingIssue(issues, BillingIssueCodes.InvalidPaymentMethod, "Choose an approved payment method (Cash, UPI, Debit Card, or Credit Card).", null);
                        continue;
                    }

                    if (tender.AmountPaise <= 0)
                    {
                        AddBlockingIssue(issues, BillingIssueCodes.InvalidPaymentAmount, "Enter a payment amount greater than zero.", null);
                        continue;
                    }

                    try
                    {
                        tenderTotalPaise = checked(tenderTotalPaise + tender.AmountPaise);
                    }
                    catch (OverflowException)
                    {
                        AddBlockingIssue(issues, BillingIssueCodes.CalculationOverflow, "Payment total exceeds supported numeric limits.", null);
                        continue;
                    }

                    validatedTenders.Add(new ValidatedTender
                    {
                        Method = tender.Method,
                        DatabaseMethod = dbMethod,
                        AmountPaise = tender.AmountPaise,
                        ReferenceText = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(tender.ReferenceText) ? null : tender.ReferenceText.Trim()
                    });
                }
            }
        }

        private static InvoicePreviewResult BuildPreviewResult(DraftEvaluation evaluation)
        {
            var preview = new InvoicePreviewResult
            {
                IsDraftValid = evaluation.IsDraftValid,
                CanFinalize = evaluation.CanFinalize,
                TenderTotalPaise = evaluation.TenderTotalPaise,
                AmountRemainingPaise = evaluation.AmountRemainingPaise,
                IsFullyPaid = evaluation.IsFullyPaid
            };

            preview.Issues.AddRange(evaluation.Issues);

            if (evaluation.Calculated != null)
            {
                preview.SubtotalPaise = evaluation.Calculated.SubtotalPaise;
                preview.DiscountPaise = evaluation.Calculated.DiscountPaise;
                preview.TaxableValuePaise = evaluation.Calculated.TaxableValuePaise;
                preview.CgstPaise = evaluation.Calculated.CgstPaise;
                preview.SgstPaise = evaluation.Calculated.SgstPaise;
                preview.IgstPaise = evaluation.Calculated.IgstPaise;
                preview.RoundOffPaise = evaluation.Calculated.RoundOffPaise;
                preview.GrandTotalPaise = evaluation.Calculated.GrandTotalPaise;

                for (int i = 0; i < evaluation.Calculated.Lines.Count; i++)
                {
                    var srcLine = evaluation.Calculated.Lines[i];
                    var dstLine = new InvoiceLinePreview
                    {
                        MedicineId = srcLine.MedicineId,
                        MedicineName = srcLine.MedicineName,
                        QuantityMilli = srcLine.QuantityMilli,
                        StockUnitName = srcLine.StockUnitName,
                        SubtotalPaise = srcLine.SubtotalPaise,
                        TaxableValuePaise = srcLine.TaxableValuePaise,
                        CgstPaise = srcLine.CgstPaise,
                        SgstPaise = srcLine.SgstPaise,
                        IgstPaise = srcLine.IgstPaise,
                        LineTotalPaise = srcLine.LineTotalPaise
                    };

                    for (int j = 0; j < srcLine.BatchLines.Count; j++)
                    {
                        var srcBatch = srcLine.BatchLines[j];
                        dstLine.BatchAllocations.Add(new BatchAllocationPreview
                        {
                            BatchNumber = srcBatch.BatchNumber,
                            ExpiryYyyyMm = srcBatch.ExpiryYyyyMm,
                            QuantityMilli = srcBatch.QuantityMilli,
                            UnitPricePaise = srcBatch.UnitPricePaise,
                            SubtotalPaise = srcBatch.SubtotalPaise,
                            TaxableValuePaise = srcBatch.TaxableValuePaise,
                            CgstPaise = srcBatch.CgstPaise,
                            SgstPaise = srcBatch.SgstPaise,
                            IgstPaise = srcBatch.IgstPaise,
                            LineTotalPaise = srcBatch.LineTotalPaise
                        });
                    }

                    preview.Lines.Add(dstLine);
                }
            }

            return preview;
        }

        private static InvoiceResult FailedInvoiceResult(IList<BillingIssue> issues)
        {
            var result = new InvoiceResult { Succeeded = false };
            if (issues != null)
            {
                for (int i = 0; i < issues.Count; i++)
                {
                    result.Issues.Add(issues[i]);
                }
            }
            return result;
        }

        private static List<string> CollectMedicineIds(IList<MergedDraftItem> mergedItems)
        {
            var ids = new List<string>(mergedItems.Count);
            for (int i = 0; i < mergedItems.Count; i++)
            {
                ids.Add(mergedItems[i].MedicineId);
            }
            return ids;
        }

        private static bool TryMapPaymentMethod(InvoicePaymentMethod method, out string databaseValue)
        {
            switch (method)
            {
                case InvoicePaymentMethod.Cash:
                    databaseValue = "CASH";
                    return true;
                case InvoicePaymentMethod.Upi:
                    databaseValue = "UPI";
                    return true;
                case InvoicePaymentMethod.DebitCard:
                    databaseValue = "DEBIT_CARD";
                    return true;
                case InvoicePaymentMethod.CreditCard:
                    databaseValue = "CREDIT_CARD";
                    return true;
                default:
                    databaseValue = null;
                    return false;
            }
        }

        private static bool TryParsePricingMode(string raw, out TaxPricingMode mode)
        {
            if (string.Equals(raw, "INCLUSIVE", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            {
                mode = TaxPricingMode.TaxInclusive;
                return true;
            }
            if (string.Equals(raw, "EXCLUSIVE", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            {
                mode = TaxPricingMode.TaxExclusive;
                return true;
            }
            mode = TaxPricingMode.TaxInclusive;
            return false;
        }

        private static bool TryParseSupplyType(string raw, out SupplyType supplyType)
        {
            if (string.Equals(raw, "INTRA_STATE", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            {
                supplyType = SupplyType.IntraState;
                return true;
            }
            if (string.Equals(raw, "INTER_STATE", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            {
                supplyType = SupplyType.InterState;
                return true;
            }
            supplyType = SupplyType.IntraState;
            return false;
        }

        private static bool TryParseRoundOffMode(string raw, out RoundOffMode mode)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(raw) || string.Equals(raw, "NONE", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            {
                mode = RoundOffMode.None;
                return true;
            }
            if (string.Equals(raw, "NEAREST_RUPEE", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            {
                mode = RoundOffMode.NearestRupee;
                return true;
            }
            mode = RoundOffMode.None;
            return false;
        }

        private static bool IsValidGstinFormat(string gstin)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(gstin) || gstin.Length != 15) return false;
            if (!char.IsDigit(gstin[0]) || !char.IsDigit(gstin[1])) return false;
            for (int i = 2; i < gstin.Length; i++)
            {
                char c = gstin[i];
                if (!char.IsLetterOrDigit(c)) return false;
            }
            return true;
        }

        private static bool HasBlockingIssues(IList<BillingIssue> issues)
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < issues.Count; i++)
            {
                if (issues[i].Severity == BillingIssueSeverity.Blocking) return true;
            }
            return false;
        }

        private static void AddBlockingIssue(List<BillingIssue> issues, string code, string message, string medicineId)
        {
            issues.Add(new BillingIssue
            {
                Severity = BillingIssueSeverity.Blocking,
                Code = code,
                Message = message,
                MedicineId = medicineId
            });
        }

        private sealed class MergedDraftItem
        {
            public string MedicineId { get; set; }
            public long QuantityMilli { get; set; }
        }

        private sealed class DraftEvaluation
        {
            public List<BillingIssue> Issues { get; set; }
            public ResolvedTaxPolicy TaxPolicy { get; set; }
            public CalculatedInvoice Calculated { get; set; }
            public long TenderTotalPaise { get; set; }
            public long AmountRemainingPaise { get; set; }
            public bool IsDraftValid { get; set; }
            public bool IsFullyPaid { get; set; }
            public bool CanFinalize { get; set; }
        }
    }
}

FILE: ORVA/src/Orva.Desktop/Services/Billing/FefoAllocator.cs
================================================================================
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace Orva.Services.Billing
{
    /// &lt;summary&gt;
    /// Deterministic First-Expired-First-Out (FEFO) batch allocator.
    /// Excludes expired, quarantined, blocked, and non-positive batches.
    /// &lt;/summary&gt;
    public sealed class FefoAllocator
    {
        public static bool IsBatchEligible(BillingBatchRecord batch, int currentExpiryYearMonth)
        {
            if (batch == null) return false;
            if (!string.Equals(batch.BatchStatus, "ACTIVE", StringComparison.Ordinal)) return false;
            if (batch.QuantityMilli <= 0) return false;
            if (batch.ExpiryYyyyMm < currentExpiryYearMonth) return false;
            return true;
        }

        public FefoAllocationOutcome Allocate(
            IList<BillingBatchRecord> candidateBatches,
            long requestedQuantityMilli,
            int currentExpiryYearMonth)
        {
            if (requestedQuantityMilli <= 0)
                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("requestedQuantityMilli", "Requested quantity must be greater than zero.");

            var eligible = new List<BillingBatchRecord>();
            if (candidateBatches != null)
            {
                for (int i = 0; i < candidateBatches.Count; i++)
                {
                    var batch = candidateBatches[i];
                    if (IsBatchEligible(batch, currentExpiryYearMonth))
                    {
                        eligible.Add(batch);
                    }
                }
            }

            eligible.Sort(CompareFefoOrder);

            long totalAvailable = 0;
            checked
            {
                for (int i = 0; i < eligible.Count; i++)
                {
                    totalAvailable += eligible[i].QuantityMilli;
                }
            }

            var outcome = new FefoAllocationOutcome
            {
                RequestedQuantityMilli = requestedQuantityMilli,
                AvailableQuantityMilli = totalAvailable,
                Succeeded = false
            };

            if (totalAvailable < requestedQuantityMilli)
            {
                return outcome;
            }

            long remaining = requestedQuantityMilli;
            checked
            {
                for (int i = 0; i < eligible.Count && remaining > 0; i++)
                {
                    var batch = eligible[i];
                    long take = batch.QuantityMilli < remaining ? batch.QuantityMilli : remaining;
                    if (take <= 0) continue;

                    outcome.Allocations.Add(new FefoBatchAllocation
                    {
                        BatchId = batch.BatchId,
                        BatchNumber = batch.BatchNumber,
                        ExpiryYyyyMm = batch.ExpiryYyyyMm,
                        QuantityMilli = take,
                        UnitPricePaise = batch.MrpPaise,
                        StockUnitName = batch.StockUnitName,
                        PreviousQuantityMilli = batch.QuantityMilli
                    });

                    remaining -= take;
                }
            }

            outcome.Succeeded = (remaining == 0);
            return outcome;
        }

        private static int CompareFefoOrder(BillingBatchRecord left, BillingBatchRecord right)
        {
            int expiryCompare = left.ExpiryYyyyMm.CompareTo(right.ExpiryYyyyMm);
            if (expiryCompare != 0) return expiryCompare;

            string leftNumber = (left.BatchNumber ?? string.Empty).Trim();
            string rightNumber = (right.BatchNumber ?? string.Empty).Trim();
            int numberCompare = StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase.Compare(leftNumber, rightNumber);
            if (numberCompare != 0) return numberCompare;

            return StringComparer.Ordinal.Compare(left.BatchId ?? string.Empty, right.BatchId ?? string.Empty);
        }
    }
}

================================================================================
FILE: ORVA/src/Orva.Desktop/Services/Billing/InvoiceCalculator.cs
================================================================================
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;

namespace Orva.Services.Billing
{
    /// &lt;summary&gt;
    /// Pure integer money, tax, round-off, and financial-year calculator.
    /// Uses checked integer arithmetic and exact base-10 rounding (zero binary floating-point).
    /// &lt;/summary&gt;
    public sealed class InvoiceCalculator
    {
        public const long QuantityScale = 1000L;
        public const long BasisPointsScale = 10000L;
        public const long PaisePerRupee = 100L;

        public CalculatedInvoice Calculate(
            IList<InvoiceLineCalculationInput> lineInputs,
            ResolvedTaxPolicy taxPolicy)
        {
            if (lineInputs == null) throw new ArgumentNullException("lineInputs");
            if (taxPolicy == null) throw new ArgumentNullException("taxPolicy");

            var invoice = new CalculatedInvoice();
            // Tracks odd-paise parity across intra-state batch allocations so CGST and SGST
            // remain balanced within +/- 1 paise both per line and across the entire invoice.
            bool nextOddPaiseToCgst = true;

            checked
            {
                for (int i = 0; i < lineInputs.Count; i++)
                {
                    var input = lineInputs[i];
                    var medicineLine = new CalculatedMedicineLine
                    {
                        LineGroupId = Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N"),
                        MedicineId = input.Medicine.MedicineId,
                        MedicineName = input.Medicine.DisplayName,
                        StockUnitName = input.Medicine.StockUnitName,
                        QuantityMilli = input.RequestedQuantityMilli
                    };

                    for (int j = 0; j < input.Allocations.Count; j++)
                    {
                        var alloc = input.Allocations[j];
                        var batchLine = CalculateBatchLine(
                            alloc,
                            input.Medicine.GstRateBasisPoints,
                            input.Medicine.HsnCode,
                            taxPolicy.PricingMode,
                            taxPolicy.SupplyType,
                            ref nextOddPaiseToCgst);

                        medicineLine.BatchLines.Add(batchLine);
                        medicineLine.SubtotalPaise += batchLine.SubtotalPaise;
                        medicineLine.DiscountPaise += batchLine.DiscountPaise;
                        medicineLine.TaxableValuePaise += batchLine.TaxableValuePaise;
                        medicineLine.CgstPaise += batchLine.CgstPaise;
                        medicineLine.SgstPaise += batchLine.SgstPaise;
                        medicineLine.IgstPaise += batchLine.IgstPaise;
                        medicineLine.LineTotalPaise += batchLine.LineTotalPaise;
                    }

                    invoice.Lines.Add(medicineLine);
                    invoice.SubtotalPaise += medicineLine.SubtotalPaise;
                    invoice.DiscountPaise += medicineLine.DiscountPaise;
                    invoice.TaxableValuePaise += medicineLine.TaxableValuePaise;
                    invoice.CgstPaise += medicineLine.CgstPaise;
                    invoice.SgstPaise += medicineLine.SgstPaise;
                    invoice.IgstPaise += medicineLine.IgstPaise;
                }

                long rawTotal = invoice.TaxableValuePaise + invoice.CgstPaise + invoice.SgstPaise + invoice.IgstPaise;
                long grandTotal = ApplyRoundOff(rawTotal, taxPolicy.RoundOffMode);
                invoice.RoundOffPaise = grandTotal - rawTotal;
                invoice.GrandTotalPaise = grandTotal;
            }

            return invoice;
        }

        public static long CalculateGrossPaise(long quantityMilli, long unitPricePaise)
        {
            if (quantityMilli < 0) throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("quantityMilli", "Quantity cannot be negative.");
            if (unitPricePaise < 0) throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("unitPricePaise", "Unit price cannot be negative.");

            decimal numerator = (decimal)quantityMilli * (decimal)unitPricePaise;
            return DivideRoundHalfAwayFromZero(numerator, QuantityScale);
        }

        public static long ApplyRoundOff(long rawTotalPaise, RoundOffMode mode)
        {
            if (mode == RoundOffMode.None) return rawTotalPaise;
            if (mode == RoundOffMode.NearestRupee)
            {
                long rupees = DivideRoundHalfAwayFromZero((decimal)rawTotalPaise, PaisePerRupee);
                return checked(rupees * PaisePerRupee);
            }
            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("mode", "Unsupported round-off mode.");
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Computes the financial year label (e.g. "26-27" for FY April 2026 - March 2027,
        /// or "2026" when start month is January) from the local invoice date.
        /// </summary>
        public static string GetFinancialYearLabel(DateTime localDate, int financialYearStartMonth)
        {
            if (financialYearStartMonth < 1 || financialYearStartMonth > 12)
                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("financialYearStartMonth", "Financial year start month must be between 1 and 12.");

            if (financialYearStartMonth == 1)
            {
                return localDate.Year.ToString("0000", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
            }

            int startYear = localDate.Month >= financialYearStartMonth ? localDate.Year : localDate.Year - 1;
            int endYear = startYear + 1;
            return string.Format(
                CultureInfo.InvariantCulture,
                "{0:00}-{1:00}",
                startYear % 100,
                endYear % 100);
        }

        public static string FormatInvoiceNumber(string prefix, string financialYear, long sequenceNumber)
        {
            if (sequenceNumber <= 0)
                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("sequenceNumber", "Invoice sequence number must be positive.");

            string cleanPrefix = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(prefix) ? "INV" : prefix.Trim();
            return string.Format(
                CultureInfo.InvariantCulture,
                "{0}/{1}/{2:D6}",
                cleanPrefix,
                financialYear,
                sequenceNumber);
        }

        public static string FormatQuantity(long quantityMilli)
        {
            decimal units = (decimal)quantityMilli / (decimal)QuantityScale;
            return units.ToString("0.###", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
        }

        public static string FormatRupees(long paise)
        {
            decimal rupees = (decimal)paise / (decimal)PaisePerRupee;
            return rupees.ToString("N2", CultureInfo.GetCultureInfo("en-IN"));
        }

        private static CalculatedBatchLine CalculateBatchLine(
            FefoBatchAllocation allocation,
            int gstRateBasisPoints,
            string hsnCode,
            TaxPricingMode pricingMode,
            SupplyType supplyType,
            ref bool nextOddPaiseToCgst)
        {
            if (gstRateBasisPoints < 0 || gstRateBasisPoints > 10000)
                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("gstRateBasisPoints", "GST rate basis points must be between 0 and 10000.");

            checked
            {
                long grossPaise = CalculateGrossPaise(allocation.QuantityMilli, allocation.UnitPricePaise);
                long discountPaise = 0; // Discounts are disabled in V1.
                long taxableValuePaise;
                long totalTaxPaise;
                long lineTotalPaise;

                if (pricingMode == TaxPricingMode.TaxInclusive)
                {
                    lineTotalPaise = grossPaise;
                    if (gstRateBasisPoints == 0)
                    {
                        taxableValuePaise = grossPaise;
                        totalTaxPaise = 0;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        decimal numerator = (decimal)grossPaise * (decimal)BasisPointsScale;
                        decimal denominator = (decimal)(BasisPointsScale + gstRateBasisPoints);
                        taxableValuePaise = DivideRoundHalfAwayFromZero(numerator, denominator);
                        totalTaxPaise = grossPaise - taxableValuePaise;
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    taxableValuePaise = grossPaise;
                    if (gstRateBasisPoints == 0)
                    {
                        totalTaxPaise = 0;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        decimal numerator = (decimal)taxableValuePaise * (decimal)gstRateBasisPoints;
                        totalTaxPaise = DivideRoundHalfAwayFromZero(numerator, BasisPointsScale);
                    }
                    lineTotalPaise = taxableValuePaise + totalTaxPaise;
                }

                long cgstPaise = 0;
                long sgstPaise = 0;
                long igstPaise = 0;

                if (supplyType == SupplyType.InterState)
                {
                    igstPaise = totalTaxPaise;
                }
                else
                {
                    long half = totalTaxPaise / 2L;
                    long remainder = totalTaxPaise % 2L;
                    if (remainder == 0)
                    {
                        cgstPaise = half;
                        sgstPaise = half;
                    }
                    else if (nextOddPaiseToCgst)
                    {
                        cgstPaise = half + 1L;
                        sgstPaise = half;
                        nextOddPaiseToCgst = false;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        cgstPaise = half;
                        sgstPaise = half + 1L;
                        nextOddPaiseToCgst = true;
                    }
                }

                return new CalculatedBatchLine
                {
                    BatchId = allocation.BatchId,
                    BatchNumber = allocation.BatchNumber,
                    ExpiryYyyyMm = allocation.ExpiryYyyyMm,
                    QuantityMilli = allocation.QuantityMilli,
                    StockUnitName = allocation.StockUnitName,
                    UnitPricePaise = allocation.UnitPricePaise,
                    GstRateBasisPoints = gstRateBasisPoints,
                    HsnCode = hsnCode,
                    PreviousQuantityMilli = allocation.PreviousQuantityMilli,
                    SubtotalPaise = grossPaise,
                    DiscountPaise = discountPaise,
                    TaxableValuePaise = taxableValuePaise,
                    CgstPaise = cgstPaise,
                    SgstPaise = sgstPaise,
                    IgstPaise = igstPaise,
                    LineTotalPaise = lineTotalPaise
                };
            }
        }

        private static long DivideRoundHalfAwayFromZero(decimal numerator, decimal denominator)
        {
            if (denominator <= 0m) throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("denominator");
            decimal quotient = numerator / denominator;
            decimal rounded = Math.Round(quotient, 0, MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero);
            return checked((long)rounded);
        }
    }

    public sealed class InvoiceLineCalculationInput
    {
        public BillingMedicineRecord Medicine { get; set; }
        public long RequestedQuantityMilli { get; set; }
        public IList<FefoBatchAllocation> Allocations { get; set; }
    }
}

FILE: ORVA/src/Orva.Desktop/Services/LocalizationManager.cs

using System;
using System.Linq;
using System.Windows;

namespace Orva.Services
{
    public static class LocalizationManager
    {
        private static ResourceDictionary _activeDictionary;
        public static string CurrentLanguage { get; private set; } = "en";

        public static void SetLanguage(string languageCode)
        {
            var language = string.Equals(languageCode, "bn", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ? "bn" : "en";
            var uri = new Uri("pack://application:,,,/ORVA;component/Resources/Strings." + language + ".xaml", UriKind.Absolute);
            var dictionary = new ResourceDictionary { Source = uri };

            var merged = Application.Current.Resources.MergedDictionaries;
            if (_activeDictionary != null) merged.Remove(_activeDictionary);
            else
            {
                var old = merged.FirstOrDefault(d => d.Contains("LanguageMarker"));
                if (old != null) merged.Remove(old);
            }
            merged.Add(dictionary);
            _activeDictionary = dictionary;
            CurrentLanguage = language;
        }

        public static string Get(string key)
        {
            var value = Application.Current.TryFindResource(key);
            return value == null ? key : Convert.ToString(value);
        }
    }
}

FILE: ORVA/src/Orva.Desktop/Services/StockEntryService.cs
================================================================================
using System;
using Orva.Domain;
using Orva.Infrastructure.Repositories;

namespace Orva.Services
{
    public sealed class StockEntryService
    {
        private readonly InventoryRepository _inventory;
        private readonly StockSnapshotWriter _snapshotWriter;

        public StockEntryService(InventoryRepository inventory, StockSnapshotWriter snapshotWriter)
        {
            _inventory = inventory;
            _snapshotWriter = snapshotWriter;
        }

        public StockEntryResult ReceiveStock(StockEntryInput input)
        {
            var batchId = _inventory.SaveStockEntry(input);
            var result = new StockEntryResult
            {
                MedicineName = input.MedicineName.Trim(),
                BatchNumber = input.BatchNumber.Trim(),
                SnapshotUpdated = false
            };

            try
            {
                _snapshotWriter.MarkDirty();
                _snapshotWriter.WriteCurrentSnapshot();
                result.SnapshotUpdated = true;
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                // The database transaction is already committed. Never report a committed stock entry as failed.
                try { _snapshotWriter.MarkDirty(); } catch { }
                System.Diagnostics.Trace.TraceError("Stock snapshot update failed after a committed stock receipt: " + ex);
                result.SnapshotWarning = "Stock was saved, but the portable stock file could not be refreshed. Use Refresh stock file in Inventory & Stock.";
            }
            return result;
        }
    }
}

================================================================================
FILE: ORVA/src/Orva.Desktop/Services/StockSnapshotWriter.cs
================================================================================
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Data.SQLite;
using System.Globalization;
using System.IO;
using System.Text;
using System.Web.Script.Serialization;
using Orva.Domain;
using Orva.Infrastructure;
using Orva.Infrastructure.Database;
using Orva.Infrastructure.Repositories;

namespace Orva.Services
{
    public sealed class StockSnapshotWriter
    {
        private readonly SqliteDatabase _database;
        private readonly ShopRepository _shopRepository;

        public StockSnapshotWriter(SqliteDatabase database, ShopRepository shopRepository)
        {
            _database = database;
            _shopRepository = shopRepository;
        }

        public string GetSnapshotPath()
        {
            var shop = _shopRepository.GetProfile();
            if (shop == null) throw new InvalidOperationException("Shop setup must be completed before creating the stock file.");
            return Path.Combine(AppPaths.GetShopFolder(shop.ShopName), "StockData.json");
        }

        public void WriteCurrentSnapshot()
        {
            var shop = _shopRepository.GetProfile();
            if (shop == null) throw new InvalidOperationException("Shop setup must be completed before creating the stock file.");

            var folder = AppPaths.GetShopFolder(shop.ShopName);
            Directory.CreateDirectory(folder);
            var targetPath = Path.Combine(folder, "StockData.json");
            var tempPath = targetPath + ".tmp";
            var previousPath = targetPath + ".previous";

            var snapshot = ReadSnapshot(shop);
            var serializer = new JavaScriptSerializer { MaxJsonLength = int.MaxValue, RecursionLimit = 100 };
            var json = serializer.Serialize(snapshot);
            var bytes = new UTF8Encoding(false).GetBytes(json);

            try
            {
                using (var stream = new FileStream(tempPath, FileMode.Create, FileAccess.Write, FileShare.None))
                {
                    stream.Write(bytes, 0, bytes.Length);
                    stream.Flush(true);
                }

                // Parse the exact file written before replacing the previous good snapshot.
                var check = File.ReadAllText(tempPath, Encoding.UTF8);
                var validated = serializer.Deserialize<StockSnapshot>(check);
                if (validated == null || validated.Format != "ORVA_STOCK_DATA" || validated.FormatVersion != 1 || validated.ShopId != shop.ShopId)
                    throw new InvalidDataException("The generated stock file did not pass validation.");

                if (File.Exists(targetPath))
                {
                    if (File.Exists(previousPath)) File.Delete(previousPath);
                    File.Replace(tempPath, targetPath, previousPath, true);
                }
                else
                {
                    File.Move(tempPath, targetPath);
                }
                SetSnapshotStatus(false);
            }
            finally
            {
                if (File.Exists(tempPath))
                {
                    try { File.Delete(tempPath); } catch { /* Preserve the original exception/result. */ }
                }
            }
        }

        public void MarkDirty()
        {
            SetSnapshotStatus(true);
        }

        public bool IsDirty()
        {
            using (var connection = _database.OpenConnection())
            using (var command = new SQLiteCommand("SELECT setting_value FROM app_setting WHERE setting_key='stock_snapshot_dirty';", connection))
            {
                var value = command.ExecuteScalar() as string;
                return string.Equals(value, "1", StringComparison.Ordinal);
            }
        }

        private void SetSnapshotStatus(bool dirty)
        {
            var now = DateTime.UtcNow.ToString("o", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
            using (var connection = _database.OpenConnection())
            using (var transaction = connection.BeginTransaction())
            {
                using (var dirtyCommand = new SQLiteCommand(@"
INSERT INTO app_setting(setting_key, setting_value, updated_at_utc) VALUES('stock_snapshot_dirty', @value, @updated)
ON CONFLICT(setting_key) DO UPDATE SET setting_value=excluded.setting_value, updated_at_utc=excluded.updated_at_utc;", connection, transaction))
                {
                    dirtyCommand.Parameters.AddWithValue("@value", dirty ? "1" : "0");
                    dirtyCommand.Parameters.AddWithValue("@updated", now);
                    dirtyCommand.ExecuteNonQuery();
                }
                if (!dirty)
                {
                    using (var timeCommand = new SQLiteCommand(@"
INSERT INTO app_setting(setting_key, setting_value, updated_at_utc) VALUES('stock_snapshot_last_written_at_utc', @value, @updated)
ON CONFLICT(setting_key) DO UPDATE SET setting_value=excluded.setting_value, updated_at_utc=excluded.updated_at_utc;", connection, transaction))
                    {
                        timeCommand.Parameters.AddWithValue("@value", now);
                        timeCommand.Parameters.AddWithValue("@updated", now);
                        timeCommand.ExecuteNonQuery();
                    }
                }
                transaction.Commit();
            }
        }

        private StockSnapshot ReadSnapshot(ShopProfile shop)
        {
            var snapshot = new StockSnapshot
            {
                Format = "ORVA_STOCK_DATA",
                FormatVersion = 1,
                ShopId = shop.ShopId,
                ShopName = shop.ShopName,
                GeneratedAtUtc = DateTime.UtcNow.ToString("o", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture),
                ApplicationVersion = "0.1.0",
                Medicines = new List<StockSnapshotMedicine>()
            };

            var medicines = new Dictionary<string, StockSnapshotMedicine>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
            using (var connection = _database.OpenConnection())
            using (var transaction = connection.BeginTransaction())
            using (var command = new SQLiteCommand(@"
SELECT m.medicine_id, m.display_name, m.generic_name, m.brand_name, m.strength, m.dosage_form,
       m.stock_unit_name, m.hsn_code, m.gst_rate_basis_points, m.low_stock_threshold_milli,
       b.batch_id, b.batch_number, b.expiry_yyyymm, b.mrp_paise, b.latest_purchase_price_paise,
       b.quantity_milli, b.batch_status, b.updated_at_utc
FROM medicine m
LEFT JOIN stock_batch b ON b.medicine_id=m.medicine_id
WHERE m.is_active=1
ORDER BY m.display_name COLLATE NOCASE, b.expiry_yyyymm, b.batch_number;", connection, transaction))
            using (var reader = command.ExecuteReader())
            {
                while (reader.Read())
                {
                    var medicineId = reader.GetString(0);
                    StockSnapshotMedicine medicine;
                    if (!medicines.TryGetValue(medicineId, out medicine))
                    {
                        medicine = new StockSnapshotMedicine
                        {
                            MedicineId = medicineId,
                            Name = reader.GetString(1),
                            GenericName = NullableString(reader, 2),
                            BrandName = NullableString(reader, 3),
                            Strength = NullableString(reader, 4),
                            DosageForm = NullableString(reader, 5),
                            StockUnit = reader.GetString(6),
                            HsnCode = NullableString(reader, 7),
                            GstRateBasisPoints = reader.GetInt32(8),
                            LowStockThresholdMilli = reader.GetInt64(9),
                            Batches = new List&lt;StockSnapshotBatch&gt;()
                        };
                        medicines.Add(medicineId, medicine);
                        snapshot.Medicines.Add(medicine);
                    }

                    if (!reader.IsDBNull(10))
                    {
                        medicine.Batches.Add(new StockSnapshotBatch
                        {
                            BatchId = reader.GetString(10),
                            BatchNumber = reader.GetString(11),
                            ExpiryYearMonth = reader.GetInt32(12),
                            MrpPaise = reader.GetInt64(13),
                            LatestPurchasePricePaise = reader.GetInt64(14),
                            QuantityMilli = reader.GetInt64(15),
                            Status = reader.GetString(16),
                            UpdatedAtUtc = reader.GetString(17)
                        });
                    }
                }
                transaction.Commit();
            }
            return snapshot;
        }

        private static string NullableString(SQLiteDataReader reader, int ordinal)
        {
            return reader.IsDBNull(ordinal) ? null : reader.GetString(ordinal);
        }
    }

    public sealed class StockSnapshot
    {
        public string Format { get; set; }
        public int FormatVersion { get; set; }
        public string ShopId { get; set; }
        public string ShopName { get; set; }
        public string GeneratedAtUtc { get; set; }
        public string ApplicationVersion { get; set; }
        public List<StockSnapshotMedicine> Medicines { get; set; }
    }

    public sealed class StockSnapshotMedicine
    {
        public string MedicineId { get; set; }
        public string Name { get; set; }
        public string GenericName { get; set; }
        public string BrandName { get; set; }
        public string Strength { get; set; }
        public string DosageForm { get; set; }
        public string StockUnit { get; set; }
        public string HsnCode { get; set; }
        public int GstRateBasisPoints { get; set; }
        public long LowStockThresholdMilli { get; set; }
        public List<StockSnapshotBatch> Batches { get; set; }
    }

    public sealed class StockSnapshotBatch
    {
        public string BatchId { get; set; }
        public string BatchNumber { get; set; }
        public int ExpiryYearMonth { get; set; }
        public long MrpPaise { get; set; }
        public long LatestPurchasePricePaise { get; set; }
        public long QuantityMilli { get; set; }
        public string Status { get; set; }
        public string UpdatedAtUtc { get; set; }
    }
}

using System;
using System.Diagnostics;

namespace Orva.ViewModels
{
    public static class BillingDiagnostics
    {
        public static void Log(string operation, Exception exception)
        {
            if (exception == null) return;

            // Local diagnostic trace only. Exception details are never shown to shop staff.
            Trace.TraceError(
                "ORVA billing operation failed. Operation={0}; ErrorType={1}; Message={2}",
                operation ?? "Unknown",
                exception.GetType().FullName,
                exception.Message);
        }
    }
}

================================================================================
FILE: ORVA/src/Orva.Desktop/ViewModels/BillingDisplay.cs

using System;
using System.Globalization;

namespace Orva.ViewModels
{
    public enum QuantityParseStatus
    {
        Valid = 0,
        Invalid = 1,
        NotPositive = 2,
        TooLarge = 3,
        TooPrecise = 4
    }

    public enum MoneyParseStatus
    {
        Valid = 0,
        Invalid = 1,
        NotPositive = 2,
        TooLarge = 3,
        TooPrecise = 4
    }

    public static class BillingDisplay
    {
        public static string FormatPaise(long paise)
        {
            return (paise / 100m).ToString("N2", CultureInfo.CurrentCulture);
        }

        public static string FormatRupees(long paise)
        {
            return "\u20B9 " + FormatPaise(paise);
        }

        public static string FormatQuantity(long quantityMilli)
        {
            return (quantityMilli / 1000m).ToString("0.###", CultureInfo.CurrentCulture);
        }

        public static bool TryParseQuantity(string text, out long quantityMilli, out QuantityParseStatus status)
        {
            quantityMilli = 0;
            decimal value;

            if (!decimal.TryParse(text, NumberStyles.Number, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, out value))
            {
                status = QuantityParseStatus.Invalid;
                return false;
            }

            if (value <= 0)
            {
                status = QuantityParseStatus.NotPositive;
                return false;
            }

            if (value > 999999999m)
            {
                status = QuantityParseStatus.TooLarge;
                return false;
            }

            var scaled = value * 1000m;
            if (scaled != decimal.Truncate(scaled))
            {
                status = QuantityParseStatus.TooPrecise;
                return false;
            }

            quantityMilli = checked((long)scaled);
            status = QuantityParseStatus.Valid;
            return true;
        }

        public static bool TryParseMoney(string text, out long amountPaise, out MoneyParseStatus status)
        {
            amountPaise = 0;
            decimal value;

            if (!decimal.TryParse(text, NumberStyles.Number, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, out value))
            {
                status = MoneyParseStatus.Invalid;
                return false;
            }

            if (value <= 0)
            {
                status = MoneyParseStatus.NotPositive;
                return false;
            }

            if (value > 999999999m)
            {
                status = MoneyParseStatus.TooLarge;
                return false;
            }

            var scaled = value * 100m;
            if (scaled != decimal.Truncate(scaled))
            {
                status = MoneyParseStatus.TooPrecise;
                return false;
            }

            amountPaise = checked((long)scaled);
            status = MoneyParseStatus.Valid;
            return true;
        }

        public static string DescribeQuantityError(QuantityParseStatus status)
        {
            switch (status)
            {
                case QuantityParseStatus.NotPositive:
                    return BillingText.Get("BillQuantityPositive", "Enter a quantity greater than zero.");
                case QuantityParseStatus.TooLarge:
                    return BillingText.Get("BillQuantityTooLarge", "That quantity is too large.");
                case QuantityParseStatus.TooPrecise:
                    return BillingText.Get("BillQuantityPrecision", "Use no more than three decimal places.");
                default:
                    return BillingText.Get("BillQuantityInvalid", "Enter a valid quantity.");
            }
        }

        public static string DescribeMoneyError(MoneyParseStatus status)
        {
            switch (status)
            {
                case MoneyParseStatus.NotPositive:
                    return BillingText.Get("BillAmountPositive", "Enter an amount greater than zero.");
                case MoneyParseStatus.TooLarge:
                    return BillingText.Get("BillAmountTooLarge", "That amount is too large.");
                case MoneyParseStatus.TooPrecise:
                    return BillingText.Get("BillAmountPrecision", "Use no more than two decimal places.");
                default:
                    return BillingText.Get("BillAmountInvalid", "Enter a valid amount.");
            }
        }
    }
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Linq;
using Orva.Domain.Billing;

namespace Orva.ViewModels
{
    public sealed class MedicineSearchHitViewModel
    {
        public MedicineSearchHitViewModel(MedicineLookupResult source)
        {
            Source = source ?? throw new ArgumentNullException("source");
            MedicineId = source.MedicineId;
            DisplayName = source.DisplayName ?? string.Empty;

            var detail = new List<string>();
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(source.Strength)) detail.Add(source.Strength.Trim());
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(source.DosageForm)) detail.Add(source.DosageForm.Trim());
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(source.GenericName)) detail.Add(source.GenericName.Trim());
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(source.BrandName)) detail.Add(source.BrandName.Trim());
            DetailText = string.Join(" · ", detail.Distinct(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase));

            AvailableText = BillingDisplay.FormatQuantity(source.AvailableQuantityMilli) +
                " " + (source.StockUnitName ?? string.Empty) + " available";

            CanSell = source.CanSell;
            StatusText = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(source.AvailabilityMessage)
                ? (CanSell ? string.Empty : "No eligible stock available")
                : source.AvailabilityMessage;
            ShowStatus = !CanSell || !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(StatusText);
            HasDetail = !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(DetailText);
        }

        public MedicineLookupResult Source { get; private set; }
        public string MedicineId { get; private set; }
        public string DisplayName { get; private set; }
        public string DetailText { get; private set; }
        public bool HasDetail { get; private set; }
        public string AvailableText { get; private set; }
        public bool CanSell { get; private set; }
        public string StatusText { get; private set; }
        public bool ShowStatus { get; private set; }
    }

    public sealed class CartLineViewModel : INotifyPropertyChanged
    {
        private long _quantityMilli;
        private string _quantityText;
        private string _quantityError;
        private string _lineTotalText = "—";
        private string _allocationNote = string.Empty;
        private bool _hasAllocationNote;
        private bool _hasError;
        private bool _canEdit = true;

        public CartLineViewModel(MedicineLookupResult source, long quantityMilli)
        {
            if (source == null) throw new ArgumentNullException("source");
            Source = source;
            MedicineId = source.MedicineId;
            MedicineName = source.DisplayName ?? string.Empty;
            StockUnitName = source.StockUnitName ?? string.Empty;
            SetQuantitySilently(quantityMilli);
        }

        public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

        public MedicineLookupResult Source { get; private set; }
        public string MedicineId { get; private set; }
        public string MedicineName { get; private set; }
        public string StockUnitName { get; private set; }

        public long QuantityMilli
        {
            get { return _quantityMilli; }
            private set
            {
                if (_quantityMilli == value) return;
                _quantityMilli = value;
                Raise("QuantityMilli");
            }
        }

        public string QuantityText
        {
            get { return _quantityText; }
            set
            {
                if (!_canEdit) return;
                var text = value ?? string.Empty;
                if (string.Equals(_quantityText, text, StringComparison.Ordinal)) return;
                _quantityText = text;
                Raise("QuantityText");

                long parsed;
                QuantityParseStatus status;
                if (BillingDisplay.TryParseQuantity(text, out parsed, out status))
                {
                    Quantity = parsed;
                    QuantityError = string.Empty;
                    HasError = false;
                }
                else
                {
                    QuantityError = BillingDisplay.DescribeQuantityError(status);
                    HasError = true;
                }
            }
        }

        public string QuantityError
        {
            get { return _quantityError; }
            private set
            {
                if (string.Equals(_quantityError, value, StringComparison.Ordinal)) return;
                _quantityError = value;
                Raise("QuantityError");
            }
        }

        public bool HasError
        {
            get { return _hasError; }
            private set
            {
                if (_hasError == value) return;
                _hasError = value;
                Raise("HasError");
            }
        }

        public bool CanEdit
        {
            get { return _canEdit; }
            set
            {
                if (_canEdit == value) return;
                _canEdit = value;
                Raise("CanEdit");
            }
        }

        public string LineTotalText
        {
            get { return _lineTotalText; }
            private set
            {
                if (string.Equals(_lineTotalText, value, StringComparison.Ordinal)) return;
                _lineTotalText = value;
                Raise("LineTotalText");
            }
        }

        public string AllocationNote
        {
            get { return _allocationNote; }
            private set
            {
                if (string.Equals(_allocationNote, value, StringComparison.Ordinal)) return;
                _allocationNote = value;
                Raise("AllocationNote");
            }
        }

        public bool HasAllocationNote
        {
            get { return _hasAllocationNote; }
            private set
            {
                if (_hasAllocationNote == value) return;
                _hasAllocationNote = value;
                Raise("HasAllocationNote");
            }
        }

        public void SetQuantitySilently(long quantityMilli)
        {
            _quantityMilli = quantityMilli;
            _quantityText = BillingDisplay.FormatQuantity(quantityMilli);
            _quantityError = string.Empty;
            _hasError = false;
            Raise("QuantityMilli");
            Raise("QuantityText");
            Raise("QuantityError");
            Raise("HasError");
        }

        public void CommitQuantityText()
        {
            long parsed;
            QuantityParseStatus status;
            if (BillingDisplay.TryParseQuantity(_quantityText, out parsed, out status))
            {
                Quantity = parsed;
                QuantityError = string.Empty;
                HasError = false;
                _quantityText = BillingDisplay.FormatQuantity(parsed);
                Raise("QuantityText");
            }
            else
            {
                QuantityError = BillingDisplay.DescribeQuantityError(status);
                HasError = true;
            }
        }

        public void ApplyPreviewLine(InvoiceLinePreview line)
        {
            if (line == null)
            {
                ClearPreviewTotals();
                return;
            }

            LineTotalText = BillingDisplay.FormatRupees(line.LineTotalPaise);
            var count = line.BatchAllocations == null ? 0 : line.BatchAllocations.Count;
            AllocationNote = count > 1 ? "Split across " + count.ToString() + " batches" : string.Empty;
            HasAllocationNote = count > 1;
        }

        public void ClearPreviewTotals()
        {
            LineTotalText = "—";
            AllocationNote = string.Empty;
            HasAllocationNote = false;
        }

        private long Quantity
        {
            set
            {
                if (_quantityMilli == value) return;
                _quantityMilli = value;
                Raise("QuantityMilli");
            }
        }

        private void Raise(string name)
        {
            var handler = PropertyChanged;
            if (handler != null) handler(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(name));
        }
    }

    public sealed class PaymentTenderViewModel : INotifyPropertyChanged
    {
        private PaymentMethodOption _selectedMethod;
        private long _amountPaise;
        private string _amountText;
        private string _referenceText = string.Empty;
        private string _amountError = string.Empty;
        private bool _hasError;
        private bool _canEdit = true;

        public PaymentTenderViewModel(
            IList<PaymentMethodOption> methods,
            PaymentMethodOption selectedMethod,
            long initialAmountPaise)
        {
            MethodOptions = methods ?? new List<PaymentMethodOption>();
            _selectedMethod = selectedMethod;
            SetAmountSilently(initialAmountPaise);
        }

        public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

        public IList<PaymentMethodOption> MethodOptions { get; private set; }

        public PaymentMethodOption SelectedMethod
        {
            get { return _selectedMethod; }
            set
            {
                if (!_canEdit || ReferenceEquals(_selectedMethod, value)) return;
                _selectedMethod = value;
                Raise("SelectedMethod");
                Raise("IsNonCash");
                Raise("Method");
            }
        }

        public InvoicePaymentMethod Method
        {
            get { return _selectedMethod == null ? InvoicePaymentMethod.Cash : _selectedMethod.Method; }
        }

        public bool IsNonCash
        {
            get { return Method != InvoicePaymentMethod.Cash; }
        }

        public long AmountPaise { get { return _amountPaise; } }

        public string AmountText
        {
            get { return _amountText; }
            set
            {
                if (!_canEdit) return;
                var text = value ?? string.Empty;
                if (string.Equals(_amountText, text, StringComparison.Ordinal)) return;
                _amountText = text;
                Raise("AmountText");

                long parsed;
                MoneyParseStatus status;
                if (BillingDisplay.TryParseMoney(text, out parsed, out status))
                {
                    _amountPaise = parsed;
                    AmountError = string.Empty;
                    HasError = false;
                    Raise("AmountPaise");
                }
                else
                {
                    AmountError = BillingDisplay.DescribeMoneyError(status);
                    HasError = true;
                }
            }
        }

        public string ReferenceText
        {
            get { return _referenceText; }
            set
            {
                if (!_canEdit) return;
                var text = value ?? string.Empty;
                if (string.Equals(_referenceText, text, StringComparison.Ordinal)) return;
                _referenceText = text;
                Raise("ReferenceText");
            }
        }

        public string AmountError
        {
            get { return _amountError; }
            private set
            {
                if (string.Equals(_amountError, value, StringComparison.Ordinal)) return;
                _amountError = value;
                Raise("AmountError");
            }
        }

        public bool HasError
        {
            get { return _hasError; }
            private set
            {
                if (_hasError == value) return;
                _hasError = value;
                Raise("HasError");
            }
        }

        public bool CanEdit
        {
            get { return _canEdit; }
            set
            {
                if (_canEdit == value) return;
                _canEdit = value;
                Raise("CanEdit");
            }
        }

        public void CommitAmountText()
        {
            long parsed;
            MoneyParseStatus status;
            if (BillingDisplay.TryParseMoney(_amountText, out parsed, out status))
            {
                SetAmountSilently(parsed);
                AmountError = string.Empty;
                HasError = false;
            }
            else
            {
                AmountError = BillingDisplay.DescribeMoneyError(status);
                HasError = true;
            }
        }

        public void SetAmountSilently(long amountPaise)
        {
            _amountPaise = amountPaise;
            _amountText = BillingDisplay.FormatPaise(amountPaise);
            _amountError = string.Empty;
            _hasError = false;
            Raise("AmountPaise");
            Raise("AmountText");
            Raise("AmountError");
            Raise("HasError");
        }

        private void Raise(string name)
        {
            var handler = PropertyChanged;
            if (handler != null) handler(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(name));
        }
    }
}

            };

FILE: ORVA/src/Orva.Desktop/ViewModels/BillingText.cs
