================================================================================
FILE: ORVA/src/Orva.Desktop/Views/BillingView.xaml.cs
================================================================================
using System;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Input;
using System.Windows.Threading;
using Orva.Controls;
using Orva.Domain.Billing;
using Orva.ViewModels;

namespace Orva.Views
{
    /// &lt;summary&gt;
    /// New Billing workspace. The billing service arrives through constructor injection;
    /// this view never touches App.Services or any static service locator.
    ///
    /// Focus policy: the medicine search box is the normal focus target, so a keyboard-wedge
    /// USB barcode scanner can send the next barcode immediately after an add.
    ///
    /// Lifetime: one instance per navigation. Unloaded stops the view model's debounce timers,
    /// invalidates in-flight requests and closes the owned preview window.
    /// &lt;/summary&gt;
    public partial class BillingView : UserControl
    {
        private BillingPreviewWindow _previewWindow;
        private bool _isUnloaded;

        public BillingView(IBillingService billingService)
            : this(billingService, null)
        {
        }

        public BillingView(IBillingService billingService, BillingShopHeader shopHeader)
        {
            if (billingService == null) throw new ArgumentNullException("billingService");

            ViewModel = new BillingViewModel(billingService, shopHeader);
            DataContext = ViewModel;
            InitializeComponent();
            ViewModel.PropertyChanged += ViewModel_PropertyChanged;
        }

        /// <summary>Exposed so the integrator can subscribe to PrintRequested later.</summary>
        public BillingViewModel ViewModel { get; private set; }

        private void BillingView_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            FocusSearch();
        }

        private void BillingView_Unloaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            _isUnloaded = true;
            ViewModel.PropertyChanged -= ViewModel_PropertyChanged;
            ViewModel.Shutdown();
            ClosePreviewWindow();
        }

        private void ViewModel_PropertyChanged(object sender, PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
        {
            if (_isUnloaded) return;
            if (!string.Equals(e.PropertyName, "IsCommitted", StringComparison.Ordinal)) return;
            if (!ViewModel.IsCommitted) return;

            // Show the committed invoice number on the paper preview as an immediate next
            // action. The draft stays on screen until staff start a new bill.
            OpenPreviewWindow();
            Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action(delegate
            {
                if (!_isUnloaded) NewBillButton.Focus();
            }), DispatcherPriority.Input);
        }

        // ---- Global shortcuts -------------------------------------------------------

        private void BillingView_PreviewKeyDown(object sender, KeyEventArgs e)
        {
            if (e.Key == Key.F2)
            {
                FocusSearch();
                e.Handled = true;
                return;
            }

            if (e.Key == Key.Enter && (Keyboard.Modifiers & ModifierKeys.Control) == ModifierKeys.Control)
            {
                if (ViewModel.FinalizeCommand.CanExecute(null))
                {
                    ViewModel.FinalizeCommand.Execute(null);
                    e.Handled = true;
                }
                return;
            }

            if (e.Key == Key.Delete)
            {
                // Never steal Delete from normal text entry.
                if (Keyboard.FocusedElement is TextBox) return;
                if (Keyboard.FocusedElement is ComboBox) return;
                if (ViewModel.IsCommitted) return;

                var line = ViewModel.SelectedCartLine;
                if (line == null) return;
                if (ViewModel.RemoveLineCommand.CanExecute(line))
                {
                    ViewModel.RemoveLineCommand.Execute(line);
                    e.Handled = true;
                }
            }
        }

        // ---- Search -----------------------------------------------------------------

        private void SearchBox_PreviewKeyDown(object sender, KeyEventArgs e)
        {
            if (e.Key == Key.Enter)
            {
                // The view model decides whether the current selection is valid for the
                // currently typed query; a stale selection can never be accepted here.
                ViewModel.AcceptSearchInput();
                e.Handled = true;
            }
            else if (e.Key == Key.Down)
            {
                MoveFocusToResults();
                e.Handled = true;
            }
            else if (e.Key == Key.Escape)
            {
                ViewModel.SearchText = string.Empty;
                e.Handled = true;
            }
        }

        private void MoveFocusToResults()
        {
            if (ResultsList.Items.Count == 0) return;
            if (ResultsList.SelectedIndex < 0) ResultsList.SelectedIndex = 0;

            var container = ResultsList.ItemContainerGenerator.ContainerFromIndex(ResultsList.SelectedIndex) as ListBoxItem;
            if (container != null)
            {
                container.Focus();
            }
            else
            {
                ResultsList.Focus();
            }
        }

        private void ResultsList_PreviewKeyDown(object sender, KeyEventArgs e)
        {
            if (e.Key == Key.Enter)
            {
                ViewModel.AcceptSearchInput();
                FocusSearch();
                e.Handled = true;
            }
            else if (e.Key == Key.Escape)
            {
                ViewModel.SearchText = string.Empty;
                FocusSearch();
                e.Handled = true;
            }
            else if (e.Key == Key.Up && ResultsList.SelectedIndex <= 0)
            {
                FocusSearch();
                e.Handled = true;
            }
        }

        private void ResultsList_MouseDoubleClick(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
        {
            var hit = ResultsList.SelectedItem as MedicineSearchHitViewModel;
            if (hit == null || !hit.CanSell || ViewModel.IsCommitted) return;

            ViewModel.AddMedicineCommand.Execute(hit);
            FocusSearch();
            e.Handled = true;
        }

        private void AddResult_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            // The command has already run through its binding; only restore the scanner focus.
            FocusSearch();
        }

        // ---- Cart quantity ----------------------------------------------------------

        private void QuantityBox_GotFocus(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            var box = sender as TextBox;
            if (box == null) return;

            var line = box.DataContext as CartLineViewModel;
            if (line != null) CartList.SelectedItem = line;

            // Select the existing quantity so staff can overtype it without clearing.
            Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action(delegate
            {
                if (box.IsKeyboardFocusWithin) box.SelectAll();
            }), DispatcherPriority.Input);
        }

        private void QuantityBox_PreviewKeyDown(object sender, KeyEventArgs e)
        {
            var box = sender as TextBox;
            if (box == null) return;
            var line = box.DataContext as CartLineViewModel;
            if (line == null) return;

            if (e.Key == Key.Enter || e.Key == Key.Escape)
            {
                line.CommitQuantityText();
                FocusSearch();
                e.Handled = true;
            }
        }

        private void QuantityBox_LostFocus(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            var box = sender as TextBox;
            if (box == null) return;
            var line = box.DataContext as CartLineViewModel;
            if (line != null) line.CommitQuantityText();
        }

        // ---- Payment amount ---------------------------------------------------------

        private void AmountBox_PreviewKeyDown(object sender, KeyEventArgs e)
        {
            var box = sender as TextBox;
            if (box == null) return;
            var tender = box.DataContext as PaymentTenderViewModel;
            if (tender == null) return;

            if (e.Key == Key.Enter)
            {
                tender.CommitAmountText();
                e.Handled = true;
            }
        }

        private void AmountBox_LostFocus(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            var box = sender as TextBox;
            if (box == null) return;
            var tender = box.DataContext as PaymentTenderViewModel;
            if (tender != null) tender.CommitAmountText();
        }

        // ---- Commands with a view-side step -----------------------------------------

        private void ClearBill_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            if (!ViewModel.HasCartItems && !ViewModel.HasTenders)
            {
                ViewModel.ClearDraft();
                FocusSearch();
                return;
            }

            var answer = MessageBox.Show(
                Window.GetWindow(this),
                BillingText.Get("BillClearConfirmMessage",
                    "All medicines and payments entered for this bill will be removed. Saved invoices are not affected."),
                BillingText.Get("BillClearConfirmTitle", "Clear this bill?"),
                MessageBoxButton.YesNo,
                MessageBoxImage.Question,
                MessageBoxResult.No);

            if (answer == MessageBoxResult.Yes)
            {
                ViewModel.ClearDraft();
                FocusSearch();
            }
        }

        private void NewBill_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            FocusSearch();
        }

        private void Print_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            // Printing is a separate integration concern. Until the integrator subscribes to
            // ViewModel.PrintRequested, show a safe, honest message instead of failing silently.
            if (!ViewModel.RaisePrintRequest())
            {
                MessageBox.Show(
                    Window.GetWindow(this),
                    BillingText.Get("BillPrintNotReady",
                        "Printing is not set up yet. The invoice is saved safely."),
                    "ORVA",
                    MessageBoxButton.OK,
                    MessageBoxImage.Information);
            }
        }

        // ---- Live paper preview window ----------------------------------------------

        private void PreviewToggle_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            if (_previewWindow == null)
            {
                OpenPreviewWindow();
            }
            else
            {
                ClosePreviewWindow();
                FocusSearch();
            }
        }

        private void OpenPreviewWindow()
        {
            if (_isUnloaded) return;

            if (_previewWindow != null)
            {
                _previewWindow.Activate();
                return;
            }

            var window = new BillingPreviewWindow(ViewModel);
            var owner = Window.GetWindow(this);
            if (owner != null)
            {
                window.Owner = owner;
                window.WindowStartupLocation = WindowStartupLocation.CenterOwner;
            }
            else
            {
                window.WindowStartupLocation = WindowStartupLocation.CenterScreen;
            }

            window.Closed += PreviewWindow_Closed;
            _previewWindow = window;
            window.Show();
            window.Activate();
        }

        private void PreviewWindow_Closed(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            var window = sender as BillingPreviewWindow;
            if (window != null) window.Closed -= PreviewWindow_Closed;
            _previewWindow = null;
        }

        private void ClosePreviewWindow()
        {
            if (_previewWindow == null) return;

            var window = _previewWindow;
            _previewWindow = null;
            window.Closed -= PreviewWindow_Closed;
            try
            {
                window.Close();
            }
            catch (InvalidOperationException)
            {
                // The window was already closing (for example, the owner window is shutting down).
            }
        }

        // ---- Focus helper -----------------------------------------------------------

        private void FocusSearch()
        {
            if (_isUnloaded) return;
            if (SearchBox == null) return;

            SearchBox.Focus();
            Keyboard.Focus(SearchBox);
            SearchBox.SelectAll();
        }
    }
}

FILE: ORVA/src/Orva.Desktop/Views/InventoryView.xaml
================================================================================

                <Button Grid.Column="6" Content="{DynamicResource OpenShopFolder}" Style="{StaticResource SecondaryButton}" VerticalAlignment="Bottom" Click="OpenFolder_Click" />
            </Grid>
        </Border>

        <Grid Grid.Row="1" Margin="0,0,0,15">
            <Grid.ColumnDefinitions><ColumnDefinition/><ColumnDefinition Width="12"/><ColumnDefinition/><ColumnDefinition Width="12"/><ColumnDefinition/></Grid.ColumnDefinitions>
            <Border Grid.Column="0" Background="#FBF7EF" BorderBrush="{StaticResource Rule}" BorderThickness="1" Padding="14" CornerRadius="5">
                <StackPanel>
                    <TextBlock Text="{DynamicResource Medicines}" Style="{StaticResource MetadataText}" />
                    <TextBlock x:Name="MedicineCountText" Text="0" FontSize="23" FontWeight="SemiBold" Foreground="{StaticResource Teal}" Margin="0,3,0,0" />
                </StackPanel>
            </Border>
            <Border Grid.Column="2" Background="#FBF7EF" BorderBrush="{StaticResource Rule}" BorderThickness="1" Padding="14" CornerRadius="5">
                <StackPanel>
                    <TextBlock Text="{DynamicResource Batches}" Style="{StaticResource MetadataText}" />
                    <TextBlock x:Name="BatchCountText" Text="0" FontSize="23" FontWeight="SemiBold" Foreground="{StaticResource Teal}" Margin="0,3,0,0" />
                </StackPanel>
            </Border>
            <Border Grid.Column="4" Background="#FBF7EF" BorderBrush="{StaticResource Rule}" BorderThickness="1" Padding="14" CornerRadius="5">
                <StackPanel>
                    <TextBlock Text="{DynamicResource PortableStockFile}" Style="{StaticResource MetadataText}" />
                    <TextBlock x:Name="SnapshotStatusText" Text="Ready to update" FontSize="13" FontWeight="SemiBold" Foreground="{StaticResource Success}" Margin="0,8,0,0" />
                </StackPanel>
            </Border>
        </Grid>

        <Border Grid.Row="2" Background="{StaticResource PaperRaised}" BorderBrush="{StaticResource Rule}" BorderThickness="1" CornerRadius="7" Padding="1">
            <DataGrid x:Name="InventoryGrid" AutoGenerateColumns="False" CanUserSortColumns="True" IsReadOnly="True" HeadersVisibility="Column" RowHeaderWidth="0" Margin="0" SelectionMode="Single">
                <DataGrid.Columns>
                    <DataGridTextColumn Header="MEDICINE" Binding="{Binding MedicineName}" Width="2.4*" MinWidth="180" />
                    <DataGridTextColumn Header="BATCH" Binding="{Binding BatchNumber}" Width="1.1*" MinWidth="90" />
                    <DataGridTextColumn Header="EXPIRY" Binding="{Binding Expiry}" Width="0.9*" MinWidth="80" />
                    <DataGridTextColumn Header="AVAILABLE" Binding="{Binding Quantity}" Width="0.9*" MinWidth="90" />
                    <DataGridTextColumn Header="UNIT" Binding="{Binding Unit}" Width="0.8*" MinWidth="70" />
                    <DataGridTextColumn Header="MRP (₹)" Binding="{Binding Mrp}" Width="1*" MinWidth="85" />
                    <DataGridTextColumn Header="STATUS" Binding="{Binding Status}" Width="1.1*" MinWidth="95" />
                </DataGrid.Columns>
            </DataGrid>
        </Border>
    </Grid>

FILE: ORVA/src/Orva.Desktop/Views/InventoryView.xaml.cs
================================================================================
using System;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using Orva.Infrastructure;

namespace Orva.Views
{
    public partial class InventoryView : UserControl
    {
        public InventoryView()
        {
            InitializeComponent();
            RefreshInventory();
        }

        private void RefreshInventory()
        {
            InventoryGrid.ItemsSource = App.Services.InventoryRepository.GetInventoryRows(SearchBox == null ? string.Empty : SearchBox.Text);
            MedicineCountText.Text = App.Services.InventoryRepository.GetMedicineCount().ToString("N0");
            BatchCountText.Text = App.Services.InventoryRepository.GetBatchCount().ToString("N0");
            try
            {
                var path = App.Services.SnapshotWriter.GetSnapshotPath();
                var dirty = App.Services.SnapshotWriter.IsDirty();
                if (dirty)
                {
                    SnapshotStatusText.Text = "Needs refresh";
                    SnapshotStatusText.Foreground = (System.Windows.Media.Brush)FindResource("Danger");
                }
                else
                {
                    SnapshotStatusText.Text = File.Exists(path) ? "Updated" : "Not created yet";
                    SnapshotStatusText.Foreground = File.Exists(path) ? (System.Windows.Media.Brush)FindResource("Success") : (System.Windows.Media.Brush)FindResource("Muted");
                }
            }
            catch
            {
                SnapshotStatusText.Text = "Not available";
            }
        }

        private void SearchBox_TextChanged(object sender, TextChangedEventArgs e) { RefreshInventory(); }

        private void AddStock_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            var dialog = new AddStockWindow();
            dialog.Owner = Window.GetWindow(this);
            if (dialog.ShowDialog() == true) RefreshInventory();
        }

        private void RefreshSnapshot_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            try
            {
                App.Services.SnapshotWriter.WriteCurrentSnapshot();
                RefreshInventory();
                MessageBox.Show("The portable stock file has been updated.", "ORVA", MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Information);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("The stock file could not be updated. Your saved inventory has not been changed. " + ex.Message,
                    "ORVA — Stock file", MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Warning);
            }
        }

        private void OpenFolder_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            try
            {
                var shop = App.Services.ShopRepository.GetProfile();
                var folder = AppPaths.GetShopFolder(shop.ShopName);
                Directory.CreateDirectory(folder);
                Process.Start(new ProcessStartInfo { FileName = folder, UseShellExecute = true });
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("The shop folder could not be opened. " + ex.Message, "ORVA", MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Warning);
            }
        }
    }
}

FILE: ORVA/src/Orva.Desktop/Views/ShopSetupWindow.xaml.cs

using System;
using System.Windows;
using Orva.Domain;
using Orva.Infrastructure.Repositories;

namespace Orva.Views
{
    public partial class ShopSetupWindow : Window
    {
        private readonly ShopRepository _repository;

        public ShopSetupWindow(ShopRepository repository)
        {
            InitializeComponent();
            _repository = repository;
            StateBox.ItemsSource = new[]
            {
                "Andhra Pradesh", "Arunachal Pradesh", "Assam", "Bihar", "Chhattisgarh", "Goa", "Gujarat",
                "Haryana", "Himachal Pradesh", "Jharkhand", "Karnataka", "Kerala", "Madhya Pradesh",
                "Maharashtra", "Manipur", "Meghalaya", "Mizoram", "Nagaland", "Odisha", "Punjab",
                "Rajasthan", "Sikkim", "Tamil Nadu", "Telangana", "Tripura", "Uttar Pradesh",
                "Uttarakhand", "West Bengal", "Delhi", "Jammu and Kashmir", "Ladakh", "Puducherry"
            };
        }

        private void Save_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            ErrorText.Text = string.Empty;
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(ShopNameBox.Text))
            {
                ErrorText.Text = "Enter the shop name to continue.";
                ShopNameBox.Focus();
                return;
            }
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(GstinBox.Text) && GstinBox.Text.Trim().Length != 15)
            {
                ErrorText.Text = "The GSTIN should contain 15 characters. Please check it before saving.";
                GstinBox.Focus();
                return;
            }

            try
            {
                _repository.SaveProfile(new ShopProfile
                {
                    ShopId = Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N"),
                    ShopName = ShopNameBox.Text.Trim(),
                    LegalName = LegalNameBox.Text.Trim(),
                    Address = AddressBox.Text.Trim(),
                    Phone = PhoneBox.Text.Trim(),
                    Email = EmailBox.Text.Trim(),
                    StateName = StateBox.Text,
                    StateCode = StateCodeBox.Text.Trim(),
                    Gstin = GstinBox.Text.Trim().ToUpperInvariant(),
                    DrugLicenceNumber = DrugLicenceBox.Text.Trim(),
                    InvoicePrefix = "INV"
                });
                DialogResult = true;
                Close();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                ErrorText.Text = "Shop details could not be saved. " + ex.Message;
            }
        }
    }
}

================================================================================
FILE: ORVA/tests/Orva.Billing.Tests/BillingServiceTests.cs
================================================================================
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Orva.Domain.Billing;
using Orva.Services.Billing;
using Xunit;

namespace Orva.Billing.Tests
{
    public sealed class BillingServiceTests
    {
        // 1. Search by partial medicine name and recognised barcode.
        [Fact]
        public void T01_SearchMedicines_FindsByPartialNameAndRecognisedBarcode()
        {
            using (var db = new BillingTestDatabase())
            {
                db.SeedDefaultShop();
                db.SeedMedicine("med-1", "Paracetamol 500mg", "strip", 1200, true);
                db.SeedMedicine("med-2", "Azithromycin 500mg", "strip", 1200, true);
                db.SeedBatch("b-1", "med-1", "P101", 202812, 2500, 5000);
                db.SeedBatch("b-2", "med-2", "A201", 202812, 9500, 3000);
                db.SeedBarcode("8901234567890", "med-2");

                var svc = db.CreateService();

                var byName = svc.SearchMedicines("paracet", 10);
                Assert.Single(byName);
                Assert.Equal("med-1", byName[0].MedicineId);
                Assert.True(byName[0].CanSell);
                Assert.Equal(5000, byName[0].AvailableQuantityMilli);

                var byBarcode = svc.SearchMedicines("8901234567890", 10);
                Assert.Single(byBarcode);
                Assert.Equal("med-2", byBarcode[0].MedicineId);
                Assert.True(byBarcode[0].CanSell);
                Assert.Equal(3000, byBarcode[0].AvailableQuantityMilli);
            }
        }

        // 2. Search bounds and empty query behaviour.
        [Fact]
        public void T02_SearchMedicines_RespectsBoundsAndEmptyQuery()
        {
            using (var db = new BillingTestDatabase())
            {
                db.SeedDefaultShop();
                db.SeedMedicine("med-1", "Alpha 10mg", "strip", 1200, true);
                db.SeedMedicine("med-2", "Beta 20mg", "strip", 1200, true);
                db.SeedMedicine("med-3", "Gamma 30mg", "strip", 1200, true);

                var svc = db.CreateService();

                Assert.Empty(svc.SearchMedicines("Alpha", 0));
                Assert.Empty(svc.SearchMedicines("Alpha", -5));

                var bounded = svc.SearchMedicines("", 2);
                Assert.Equal(2, bounded.Count);
                Assert.False(bounded[0].CanSell);
                Assert.Equal("Out of stock", bounded[0].AvailabilityMessage);
            }
        }

        // 3. One medicine/one batch successful invoice.
        [Fact]
        public void T03_FinalizeInvoice_SingleMedicineSingleBatch_Succeeds()
        {
            using (var db = new BillingTestDatabase())
            {
                db.SeedDefaultShop();
                db.SeedTaxConfig(true, "INCLUSIVE", "INTRA_STATE", "NONE");
                db.SeedMedicine("med-1", "Paracetamol 500mg", "strip", 1200, true, "30049099");
                db.SeedBatch("b-1", "med-1", "P101", 202812, 2500, 10000);

                var svc = db.CreateService();
                var draft = new InvoiceDraft();
                draft.Items.Add(new InvoiceItemDraft { MedicineId = "med-1", QuantityMilli = 2000 });
                draft.Tenders.Add(new PaymentTenderDraft { Method = InvoicePaymentMethod.Cash, AmountPaise = 5000 });

                var result = svc.FinalizeInvoice(draft);

                Assert.True(result.Succeeded);
                Assert.Equal("INV/26-27/000001", result.InvoiceNumber);
                Assert.Equal(5000, result.GrandTotalPaise);
                Assert.Equal(8000, db.QueryScalarInt64("SELECT quantity_milli FROM stock_batch WHERE batch_id = 'b-1';"));
            }
        }

        // 4. FEFO selection across two or more batches.
        [Fact]
        public void T04_FefoSelection_PicksEarliestExpiryFirst()
        {
            using (var db = new BillingTestDatabase())
            {
                db.SeedDefaultShop();
                db.SeedTaxConfig(true, "INCLUSIVE", "INTRA_STATE", "NONE");
                db.SeedMedicine("med-1", "Cetirizine 10mg", "strip", 1200, true);
                db.SeedBatch("b-late", "med-1", "LATE01", 202906, 3000, 5000);
                db.SeedBatch("b-early", "med-1", "EARLY01", 202703, 3000, 5000);

                var svc = db.CreateService();
                var draft = new InvoiceDraft();
                draft.Items.Add(new InvoiceItemDraft { MedicineId = "med-1", QuantityMilli = 2000 });
                draft.Tenders.Add(new PaymentTenderDraft { Method = InvoicePaymentMethod.Cash, AmountPaise = 6000 });

                var preview = svc.PreviewInvoice(draft);
                Assert.Single(preview.Lines[0].BatchAllocations);
                Assert.Equal("EARLY01", preview.Lines[0].BatchAllocations[0].BatchNumber);

                var finalized = svc.FinalizeInvoice(draft);
                Assert.True(finalized.Succeeded);
                Assert.Equal(3000, db.QueryScalarInt64("SELECT quantity_milli FROM stock_batch WHERE batch_id = 'b-early';"));
                Assert.Equal(5000, db.QueryScalarInt64("SELECT quantity_milli FROM stock_batch WHERE batch_id = 'b-late';"));
            }
        }

        // 5 & 6. Split one medicine line across multiple batches and respect different MRP per batch.
        [Fact]
        public void T05_T06_SplitAcrossBatches_RespectsDifferentMrpPerBatch()
        {
            using (var db = new BillingTestDatabase())
            {
                db.SeedDefaultShop();
                db.SeedTaxConfig(true, "INCLUSIVE", "INTRA_STATE", "NONE");
                db.SeedMedicine("med-1", "Pantoprazole 40mg", "strip", 1200, true);
                db.SeedBatch("b-1", "med-1", "B1", 202701, 4000, 2000); // 2 strips @ Rs 40.00 = Rs 80.00
                db.SeedBatch("b-2", "med-1", "B2", 202801, 5500, 5000); // 3 strips @ Rs 55.00 = Rs 165.00

                var svc = db.CreateService();
                var draft = new InvoiceDraft();
                draft.Items.Add(new InvoiceItemDraft { MedicineId = "med-1", QuantityMilli = 5000 });
                draft.Tenders.Add(new PaymentTenderDraft { Method = InvoicePaymentMethod.Upi, AmountPaise = 24500, ReferenceText = "UTR123" });

                var preview = svc.PreviewInvoice(draft);
                Assert.True(preview.CanFinalize);
                Assert.Single(preview.Lines);
                Assert.Equal(2, preview.Lines[0].BatchAllocations.Count);
                Assert.Equal(4000, preview.Lines[0].BatchAllocations[0].UnitPricePaise);
                Assert.Equal(8000, preview.Lines[0].BatchAllocations[0].LineTotalPaise);
                Assert.Equal(5500, preview.Lines[0].BatchAllocations[1].UnitPricePaise);
                Assert.Equal(16500, preview.Lines[0].BatchAllocations[1].LineTotalPaise);
                Assert.Equal(24500, preview.GrandTotalPaise);

                var finalized = svc.FinalizeInvoice(draft);
                Assert.True(finalized.Succeeded);
                Assert.Equal(0, db.QueryScalarInt64("SELECT quantity_milli FROM stock_batch WHERE batch_id = 'b-1';"));
                Assert.Equal(2000, db.QueryScalarInt64("SELECT quantity_milli FROM stock_batch WHERE batch_id = 'b-2';"));
                Assert.Equal(2, db.QueryScalarInt64("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM invoice_line WHERE invoice_id = @id;", p => p.AddWithValue("@id", finalized.InvoiceId)));
            }
        }

        // 7. Expired, quarantined, blocked and empty batches excluded.
        [Fact]
        public void T07_IneligibleBatches_AreExcludedFromSearchPreviewAndFinalize()
        {
            using (var db = new BillingTestDatabase())
            {
                db.SeedDefaultShop();
                db.SeedTaxConfig(true, "INCLUSIVE", "INTRA_STATE", "NONE");
                db.SeedMedicine("med-1", "Dolo 650", "strip", 1200, true);
                db.SeedBatch("b-exp", "med-1", "EXP1", 202610, 3000, 5000, "ACTIVE");     // Expired relative to 202611
                db.SeedBatch("b-quar", "med-1", "QUA1", 202812, 3000, 5000, "QUARANTINED");
                db.SeedBatch("b-blk", "med-1", "BLK1", 202812, 3000, 5000, "BLOCKED");
                db.SeedBatch("b-zero", "med-1", "ZER1", 202812, 3000, 0, "ACTIVE");
                db.SeedBatch("b-ok", "med-1", "OK1", 202611, 3000, 2000, "ACTIVE");       // Valid through end of 2026-11

                var svc = db.CreateService();
                var lookup = svc.SearchMedicines("Dolo", 10);
                Assert.Single(lookup);
                Assert.Equal(2000, lookup[0].AvailableQuantityMilli);

                var draft = new InvoiceDraft();
                draft.Items.Add(new InvoiceItemDraft { MedicineId = "med-1", QuantityMilli = 2000 });
                draft.Tenders.Add(new PaymentTenderDraft { Method = InvoicePaymentMethod.Cash, AmountPaise = 6000 });

                var result = svc.FinalizeInvoice(draft);
                Assert.True(result.Succeeded);
                Assert.Equal(0, db.QueryScalarInt64("SELECT quantity_milli FROM stock_batch WHERE batch_id = 'b-ok';"));
                Assert.Equal(5000, db.QueryScalarInt64("SELECT quantity_milli FROM stock_batch WHERE batch_id = 'b-exp';"));
            }
        }

        // 8 & 9. Exact stock boundary succeeds; one-thousandth above available fails with no writes.
        [Fact]
        public void T08_T09_ExactStockBoundarySucceeds_AndOneMilliAboveFailsWithoutWrites()
        {
            using (var db = new BillingTestDatabase())
            {
                db.SeedDefaultShop();
                db.SeedTaxConfig(true, "INCLUSIVE", "INTRA_STATE", "NONE");
                db.SeedMedicine("med-1", "Metformin 500mg", "strip", 1200, true);
                db.SeedBatch("b-1", "med-1", "M1", 202812, 2000, 3000);

                var svc = db.CreateService();

                var overDraft = new InvoiceDraft();
                overDraft.Items.Add(new InvoiceItemDraft { MedicineId = "med-1", QuantityMilli = 3001 });
                overDraft.Tenders.Add(new PaymentTenderDraft { Method = InvoicePaymentMethod.Cash, AmountPaise = 6002 });

                var overResult = svc.FinalizeInvoice(overDraft);
                Assert.False(overResult.Succeeded);
                Assert.Contains(overResult.Issues, i => i.Code == BillingIssueCodes.InsufficientStock);
                Assert.Equal(0, db.QueryScalarInt64("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM invoice;"));
                Assert.Equal(3000, db.QueryScalarInt64("SELECT quantity_milli FROM stock_batch WHERE batch_id = 'b-1';"));

                var exactDraft = new InvoiceDraft();
                exactDraft.Items.Add(new InvoiceItemDraft { MedicineId = "med-1", QuantityMilli = 3000 });
                exactDraft.Tenders.Add(new PaymentTenderDraft { Method = InvoicePaymentMethod.Cash, AmountPaise = 6000 });

                var exactResult = svc.FinalizeInvoice(exactDraft);
                Assert.True(exactResult.Succeeded);
                Assert.Equal(0, db.QueryScalarInt64("SELECT quantity_milli FROM stock_batch WHERE batch_id = 'b-1';"));
            }
        }

        // 10. Duplicate medicine lines are merged safely.
        [Fact]
        public void T10_DuplicateMedicineLines_AreMergedSafely()
        {
            using (var db = new BillingTestDatabase())
            {
                db.SeedDefaultShop();
                db.SeedTaxConfig(true, "INCLUSIVE", "INTRA_STATE", "NONE");
                db.SeedMedicine("med-1", "ORS Powder", "sachet", 500, true);
                db.SeedBatch("b-1", "med-1", "ORS1", 202812, 2000, 3000);

                var svc = db.CreateService();
                var draft = new InvoiceDraft();
                draft.Items.Add(new InvoiceItemDraft { MedicineId = "med-1", QuantityMilli = 1000 });
                draft.Items.Add(new InvoiceItemDraft { MedicineId = "med-1", QuantityMilli = 2000 });
                draft.Tenders.Add(new PaymentTenderDraft { Method = InvoicePaymentMethod.Cash, AmountPaise = 6000 });

                var preview = svc.PreviewInvoice(draft);
                Assert.Single(preview.Lines);
                Assert.Equal(3000, preview.Lines[0].QuantityMilli);
                Assert.True(preview.CanFinalize);

                var result = svc.FinalizeInvoice(draft);
                Assert.True(result.Succeeded);
                Assert.Equal(0, db.QueryScalarInt64("SELECT quantity_milli FROM stock_batch WHERE batch_id = 'b-1';"));
            }
        }

        // 11 & 12. Split tender exact match succeeds; one-paise overpayment and underpayment fail.
        [Fact]
        public void T11_T12_SplitTenderExactMatchSucceeds_OnePaiseMismatchFails()
        {
            using (var db = new BillingTestDatabase())
            {
                db.SeedDefaultShop();
                db.SeedTaxConfig(true, "INCLUSIVE", "INTRA_STATE", "NONE");
                db.SeedMedicine("med-1", "Insulin Pen", "unit", 500, true);
                db.SeedBatch("b-1", "med-1", "INS1", 202812, 50000, 5000);

                var svc = db.CreateService();

                var underDraft = new InvoiceDraft();
                underDraft.Items.Add(new InvoiceItemDraft { MedicineId = "med-1", QuantityMilli = 1000 });
                underDraft.Tenders.Add(new PaymentTenderDraft { Method = InvoicePaymentMethod.Cash, AmountPaise = 49999 });
                var underRes = svc.FinalizeInvoice(underDraft);
                Assert.False(underRes.Succeeded);
                Assert.Contains(underRes.Issues, i => i.Code == BillingIssueCodes.PaymentShort);

                var overDraft = new InvoiceDraft();
                overDraft.Items.Add(new InvoiceItemDraft { MedicineId = "med-1", QuantityMilli = 1000 });
                overDraft.Tenders.Add(new PaymentTenderDraft { Method = InvoicePaymentMethod.Cash, AmountPaise = 50001 });
                var overRes = svc.FinalizeInvoice(overDraft);
                Assert.False(overRes.Succeeded);
                Assert.Contains(overRes.Issues, i => i.Code == BillingIssueCodes.PaymentOverpaid);
                Assert.Equal(0, db.QueryScalarInt64("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM invoice;"));

                var splitDraft = new InvoiceDraft();
                splitDraft.Items.Add(new InvoiceItemDraft { MedicineId = "med-1", QuantityMilli = 1000 });
                splitDraft.Tenders.Add(new PaymentTenderDraft { Method = InvoicePaymentMethod.Cash, AmountPaise = 10000 });
                splitDraft.Tenders.Add(new PaymentTenderDraft { Method = InvoicePaymentMethod.Upi, AmountPaise = 15000, ReferenceText = "UPI01" });
                splitDraft.Tenders.Add(new PaymentTenderDraft { Method = InvoicePaymentMethod.DebitCard, AmountPaise = 15000, ReferenceText = "POS01" });
                splitDraft.Tenders.Add(new PaymentTenderDraft { Method = InvoicePaymentMethod.CreditCard, AmountPaise = 10000, ReferenceText = "POS02" });

                var okRes = svc.FinalizeInvoice(splitDraft);
                Assert.True(okRes.Succeeded);
                Assert.Equal(4, db.QueryScalarInt64("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM payment WHERE invoice_id = @id;", p => p.AddWithValue("@id", okRes.InvoiceId)));
            }
        }

        // 13 & 14. Missing tax configuration fails before writes; explicit 0% differs from unconfigured 0%.
        [Fact]
        public void T13_T14_MissingTaxConfigFails_AndExplicitZeroDiffersFromUnconfiguredZero()
        {
            using (var db = new BillingTestDatabase())
            {
                db.SeedDefaultShop();
                db.SeedMedicine("med-unconfigured", "Unconfigured Med", "strip", 0, false);
                db.SeedMedicine("med-zero-ok", "Explicit Zero Med", "strip", 0, true);
                db.SeedBatch("b-1", "med-unconfigured", "U1", 202812, 1000, 5000);
                db.SeedBatch("b-2", "med-zero-ok", "Z1", 202812, 1000, 5000);

                var svc = db.CreateService();

                // Shop tax setup missing
                var draftZero = new InvoiceDraft();
                draftZero.Items.Add(new InvoiceItemDraft { MedicineId = "med-zero-ok", QuantityMilli = 1000 });
                draftZero.Tenders.Add(new PaymentTenderDraft { Method = InvoicePaymentMethod.Cash, AmountPaise = 1000 });
                var missingShopTax = svc.FinalizeInvoice(draftZero);
                Assert.False(missingShopTax.Succeeded);
                Assert.Contains(missingShopTax.Issues, i => i.Code == BillingIssueCodes.TaxConfigMissing);

                // Configure shop tax, test unconfigured 0% medicine -> must block
                db.SeedTaxConfig(true, "INCLUSIVE", "INTRA_STATE", "NONE");
                var draftUnconfigured = new InvoiceDraft();
                draftUnconfigured.Items.Add(new InvoiceItemDraft { MedicineId = "med-unconfigured", QuantityMilli = 1000 });
                draftUnconfigured.Tenders.Add(new PaymentTenderDraft { Method = InvoicePaymentMethod.Cash, AmountPaise = 1000 });
                var unconfRes = svc.FinalizeInvoice(draftUnconfigured);
                Assert.False(unconfRes.Succeeded);
                Assert.Contains(unconfRes.Issues, i => i.Code == BillingIssueCodes.MedicineTaxNotConfigured);
                Assert.Equal(0, db.QueryScalarInt64("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM invoice;"));

                // Explicit 0% medicine -> must succeed
                var zeroRes = svc.FinalizeInvoice(draftZero);
                Assert.True(zeroRes.Succeeded);
                Assert.Equal(0, db.QueryScalarInt64("SELECT cgst_paise + sgst_paise + igst_paise FROM invoice WHERE invoice_id = @id;", p => p.AddWithValue("@id", zeroRes.InvoiceId)));
            }
        }

        // 15 & 16. Inclusive/exclusive tax arithmetic and intra-state CGST+SGST vs inter-state IGST reconciliation.
        [Fact]
        public void T15_T16_InclusiveExclusive_AndIntraInterState_Reconcile()
        {
            using (var db = new BillingTestDatabase())
            {
                db.SeedDefaultShop(stateCode: "19");
                db.SeedTaxConfig(true, "EXCLUSIVE", "INTRA_STATE", "NONE");
                db.SeedMedicine("med-1", "Test Antibiotic", "strip", 1200, true);
                db.SeedBatch("b-1", "med-1", "AB1", 202812, 10000, 10000);

                var svc = db.CreateService();

                // Exclusive Intra-State
                var intraDraft = new InvoiceDraft();
                intraDraft.Items.Add(new InvoiceItemDraft { MedicineId = "med-1", QuantityMilli = 1000 });
                var intraPreview = svc.PreviewInvoice(intraDraft);
                Assert.Equal(10000, intraPreview.TaxableValuePaise);
                Assert.Equal(600, intraPreview.CgstPaise);
                Assert.Equal(600, intraPreview.SgstPaise);
                Assert.Equal(0, intraPreview.IgstPaise);
                Assert.Equal(11200, intraPreview.GrandTotalPaise);

                // Exclusive Inter-State via out-of-state customer GSTIN (27 = Maharashtra != 19 = WB)
                var interDraft = new InvoiceDraft();
                interDraft.Customer.Gstin = "27ABCDE1234F1Z5";
                interDraft.Items.Add(new InvoiceItemDraft { MedicineId = "med-1", QuantityMilli = 1000 });
                var interPreview = svc.PreviewInvoice(interDraft);
                Assert.Equal(10000, interPreview.TaxableValuePaise);
                Assert.Equal(0, interPreview.CgstPaise);
                Assert.Equal(0, interPreview.SgstPaise);
                Assert.Equal(1200, interPreview.IgstPaise);
                Assert.Equal(11200, interPreview.GrandTotalPaise);

                // Inclusive mode
                db.SeedTaxConfig(true, "INCLUSIVE", "INTRA_STATE", "NONE");
                var inclPreview = svc.PreviewInvoice(intraDraft);
                Assert.Equal(10000, inclPreview.GrandTotalPaise);
                Assert.Equal(inclPreview.GrandTotalPaise, inclPreview.TaxableValuePaise + inclPreview.CgstPaise + inclPreview.SgstPaise + inclPreview.IgstPaise);
            }
        }

        // 17 & 18. Financial-year boundary increments and failed/cancelled attempt never reuses committed invoice numbers.
        [Fact]
        public void T17_T18_FinancialYearBoundaryAndMonotonicSequence()
        {
            using (var db = new BillingTestDatabase())
            {
                db.SeedDefaultShop(fyStartMonth: 4);
                db.SeedTaxConfig(true, "INCLUSIVE", "INTRA_STATE", "NONE");
                db.SeedMedicine("med-1", "Vitamin C", "strip", 1200, true);
                db.SeedBatch("b-1", "med-1", "VC1", 202912, 1000, 10000);

                var clockMar31 = new FakeBillingClock(new DateTime(2026, 3, 31, 10, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc), 330);
                var svcMar31 = db.CreateService(clockMar31);

                var draft = new InvoiceDraft();
                draft.Items.Add(new InvoiceItemDraft { MedicineId = "med-1", QuantityMilli = 1000 });
                draft.Tenders.Add(new PaymentTenderDraft { Method = InvoicePaymentMethod.Cash, AmountPaise = 1000 });

                var inv1 = svcMar31.FinalizeInvoice(draft);
                Assert.True(inv1.Succeeded);
                Assert.Equal("INV/25-26/000001", inv1.InvoiceNumber);

                // Mark inv1 CANCELLED in DB and verify next invoice in same FY gets 000002 (never reuses 000001).
                db.ExecuteNonQuery("UPDATE invoice SET status = 'CANCELLED', cancellation_reason = 'Test' WHERE invoice_id = @id;", p => p.AddWithValue("@id", inv1.InvoiceId));

                var inv2 = svcMar31.FinalizeInvoice(draft);
                Assert.True(inv2.Succeeded);
                Assert.Equal("INV/25-26/000002", inv2.InvoiceNumber);

                // Cross into new FY (April 1, 2026) -> starts at 26-27/000001
                var clockApr1 = new FakeBillingClock(new DateTime(2026, 4, 1, 10, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc), 330);
                var svcApr1 = db.CreateService(clockApr1);
                var inv3 = svcApr1.FinalizeInvoice(draft);
                Assert.True(inv3.Succeeded);
                Assert.Equal("INV/26-27/000001", inv3.InvoiceNumber);
            }
        }

        // 19. Injected failure after each critical insert/update rolls the whole transaction back.
        [Theory]
        [InlineData("after_sequence_increment")]
        [InlineData("after_invoice_insert")]
        [InlineData("after_invoice_line_insert")]
        [InlineData("after_batch_deduction")]
        [InlineData("after_stock_movement_insert")]
        [InlineData("after_payment_insert")]
        [InlineData("after_audit_log_insert")]
        [InlineData("after_snapshot_dirty_update")]
        public void T19_InjectedFailureAtEveryCheckpoint_RollsBackAtomically(string failAtCheckpoint)
        {
            using (var db = new BillingTestDatabase())
            {
                db.SeedDefaultShop();
                db.SeedTaxConfig(true, "INCLUSIVE", "INTRA_STATE", "NONE");
                db.SetSetting("stock_snapshot_dirty", "0");
                db.SeedMedicine("med-1", "Paracetamol 500mg", "strip", 1200, true);
                db.SeedBatch("b-1", "med-1", "P101", 202812, 2500, 10000);

                BillingWriteCheckpoint hook = name =>
                {
                    if (string.Equals(name, failAtCheckpoint, StringComparison.Ordinal))
                    {
                        throw new InvalidOperationException("Simulated crash at " + name);
                    }
                };

                var svc = db.CreateService(checkpoint: hook);
                var draft = new InvoiceDraft();
                draft.Items.Add(new InvoiceItemDraft { MedicineId = "med-1", QuantityMilli = 2000 });
                draft.Tenders.Add(new PaymentTenderDraft { Method = InvoicePaymentMethod.Cash, AmountPaise = 5000 });

                Assert.Throws<InvalidOperationException>(() => svc.FinalizeInvoice(draft));

                Assert.Equal(0, db.QueryScalarInt64("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM invoice;"));
                Assert.Equal(0, db.QueryScalarInt64("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM invoice_line;"));
                Assert.Equal(0, db.QueryScalarInt64("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM payment;"));
                Assert.Equal(0, db.QueryScalarInt64("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM stock_movement;"));
                Assert.Equal(0, db.QueryScalarInt64("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM audit_log;"));
                Assert.Equal(0, db.QueryScalarInt64("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM invoice_sequence;"));
                Assert.Equal(10000, db.QueryScalarInt64("SELECT quantity_milli FROM stock_batch WHERE batch_id = 'b-1';"));
                Assert.Equal("0", db.GetSetting("stock_snapshot_dirty"));
            }
        }

        // 20, 21, 22, 23. Timestamps, immutable snapshots after master-data edits, movement/payment/audit parity, stock_snapshot_dirty=1.
        [Fact]
        public void T20_T21_T22_T23_TimestampsSnapshotsLedgerParityAndSnapshotDirty()
        {
            using (var db = new BillingTestDatabase())
            {
                db.SeedDefaultShop();
                db.SeedTaxConfig(true, "INCLUSIVE", "INTRA_STATE", "NONE");
                db.SetSetting("stock_snapshot_dirty", "0");
                db.SeedMedicine("med-1", "Original Name 500mg", "strip", 1200, true, "30049099");
                db.SeedBatch("b-1", "med-1", "ORIG-B1", 202812, 3000, 10000);

                var fixedUtc = new DateTime(2026, 11, 18, 12, 30, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc);
                var clock = new FakeBillingClock(fixedUtc, 330);
                var svc = db.CreateService(clock);

                var draft = new InvoiceDraft();
                draft.Items.Add(new InvoiceItemDraft { MedicineId = "med-1", QuantityMilli = 2000 });
                draft.Tenders.Add(new PaymentTenderDraft { Method = InvoicePaymentMethod.Cash, AmountPaise = 6000 });

                var result = svc.FinalizeInvoice(draft);
                Assert.True(result.Succeeded);
                Assert.Equal(fixedUtc, result.IssuedAtUtc);
                Assert.Equal(330, result.IssuedLocalOffsetMinutes);
                Assert.Equal("1", db.GetSetting("stock_snapshot_dirty"));

                // Verify stock_movement, payment, and audit_log parity
                Assert.Equal(-2000, db.QueryScalarInt64("SELECT quantity_delta_milli FROM stock_movement WHERE reference_id = @id AND movement_type = 'SALE';", p => p.AddWithValue("@id", result.InvoiceId)));
                Assert.Equal(6000, db.QueryScalarInt64("SELECT SUM(amount_paise) FROM payment WHERE invoice_id = @id;", p => p.AddWithValue("@id", result.InvoiceId)));
                Assert.Equal(1, db.QueryScalarInt64("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM audit_log WHERE entity_type = 'INVOICE' AND entity_id = @id AND action_type = 'INVOICE_ISSUED';", p => p.AddWithValue("@id", result.InvoiceId)));

                // Mutate master data after sale -> invoice_line snapshot must remain immutable
                db.ExecuteNonQuery("UPDATE medicine SET display_name = 'Renamed Med', gst_rate_basis_points = 1800, hsn_code = '99999999' WHERE medicine_id = 'med-1';");
                db.ExecuteNonQuery("UPDATE stock_batch SET batch_number = 'RENAMED-B1', mrp_paise = 9999 WHERE batch_id = 'b-1';");

                Assert.Equal("Original Name 500mg", db.QueryScalarString("SELECT medicine_name_snapshot FROM invoice_line WHERE invoice_id = @id;", p => p.AddWithValue("@id", result.InvoiceId)));
                Assert.Equal("ORIG-B1", db.QueryScalarString("SELECT batch_number_snapshot FROM invoice_line WHERE invoice_id = @id;", p => p.AddWithValue("@id", result.InvoiceId)));
                Assert.Equal(3000, db.QueryScalarInt64("SELECT unit_price_paise FROM invoice_line WHERE invoice_id = @id;", p => p.AddWithValue("@id", result.InvoiceId)));
                Assert.Equal(1200, db.QueryScalarInt64("SELECT gst_rate_basis_points FROM invoice_line WHERE invoice_id = @id;", p => p.AddWithValue("@id", result.InvoiceId)));
                Assert.Equal("30049099", db.QueryScalarString("SELECT hsn_code_snapshot FROM invoice_line WHERE invoice_id = @id;", p => p.AddWithValue("@id", result.InvoiceId)));
            }
        }

        // 24. PreviewInvoice performs zero writes and returns totals identical to FinalizeInvoice.
        [Fact]
        public void T24_PreviewInvoice_PerformsZeroWrites_AndMatchesFinalizeTotals()
        {
            using (var db = new BillingTestDatabase())
            {
                db.SeedDefaultShop();
                db.SeedTaxConfig(true, "INCLUSIVE", "INTRA_STATE", "NONE");
                db.SetSetting("stock_snapshot_dirty", "0");
                db.SeedMedicine("med-1", "Atorvastatin 10mg", "strip", 1200, true);
                db.SeedBatch("b-1", "med-1", "AT1", 202705, 4500, 2000);
                db.SeedBatch("b-2", "med-1", "AT2", 202805, 5000, 5000);

                var svc = db.CreateService();
                var draft = new InvoiceDraft();
                draft.Items.Add(new InvoiceItemDraft { MedicineId = "med-1", QuantityMilli = 3000 });
                draft.Tenders.Add(new PaymentTenderDraft { Method = InvoicePaymentMethod.Cash, AmountPaise = 14000 });

                var preview = svc.PreviewInvoice(draft);
                Assert.True(preview.CanFinalize);
                Assert.Equal(14000, preview.GrandTotalPaise);

                // Verify zero writes after PreviewInvoice
                Assert.Equal(0, db.QueryScalarInt64("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM invoice;"));
                Assert.Equal(0, db.QueryScalarInt64("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM invoice_sequence;"));
                Assert.Equal(0, db.QueryScalarInt64("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM stock_movement;"));
                Assert.Equal(2000, db.QueryScalarInt64("SELECT quantity_milli FROM stock_batch WHERE batch_id = 'b-1';"));
                Assert.Equal("0", db.GetSetting("stock_snapshot_dirty"));

                var finalized = svc.FinalizeInvoice(draft);
                Assert.True(finalized.Succeeded);
                Assert.Equal(preview.GrandTotalPaise, finalized.GrandTotalPaise);
                Assert.Equal(preview.TaxableValuePaise, db.QueryScalarInt64("SELECT taxable_value_paise FROM invoice WHERE invoice_id = @id;", p => p.AddWithValue("@id", finalized.InvoiceId)));
                Assert.Equal(preview.CgstPaise, db.QueryScalarInt64("SELECT cgst_paise FROM invoice WHERE invoice_id = @id;", p => p.AddWithValue("@id", finalized.InvoiceId)));
                Assert.Equal(preview.SgstPaise, db.QueryScalarInt64("SELECT sgst_paise FROM invoice WHERE invoice_id = @id;", p => p.AddWithValue("@id", finalized.InvoiceId)));
                Assert.Equal(preview.IgstPaise, db.QueryScalarInt64("SELECT igst_paise FROM invoice WHERE invoice_id = @id;", p => p.AddWithValue("@id", finalized.InvoiceId)));
            }
        }
    }
}

================================================================================
FILE: ORVA/tests/Orva.Billing.Tests/BillingTestFixture.cs
================================================================================
using System;
using System.Data.SQLite;
using System.Globalization;
using System.IO;
using Orva.Infrastructure.Database;
using Orva.Infrastructure.Repositories;
using Orva.Services.Billing;

namespace Orva.Billing.Tests
{
    public sealed class FakeBillingClock : IBillingClock
    {
        public FakeBillingClock(DateTime utcNow, int localOffsetMinutes)
        {
            UtcNow = DateTime.SpecifyKind(utcNow, DateTimeKind.Utc);
            LocalOffsetMinutes = localOffsetMinutes;
        }

        public DateTime UtcNow { get; set; }
        public int LocalOffsetMinutes { get; set; }

        public DateTime LocalNow
        {
            get { return UtcNow.AddMinutes(LocalOffsetMinutes); }
        }

        public int CurrentExpiryYearMonth
        {
            get
            {
                var local = LocalNow;
                return local.Year * 100 + local.Month;
            }
        }
    }

    public sealed class BillingTestDatabase : IDisposable
    {
        private readonly string _tempDirectory;
        public string DatabasePath { get; private set; }
        public SqliteDatabase Database { get; private set; }

        public BillingTestDatabase()
        {
            _tempDirectory = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), "orva_billing_tests_" + Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N"));
            Directory.CreateDirectory(_tempDirectory);
            DatabasePath = Path.Combine(_tempDirectory, "orva_test.sqlite");
            Database = new SqliteDatabase(DatabasePath);

            var migrator = new DatabaseMigrator(Database);
            migrator.Migrate();
        }

        public BillingService CreateService(IBillingClock clock = null, BillingWriteCheckpoint checkpoint = null)
        {
            var effectiveClock = clock ?? new FakeBillingClock(new DateTime(2026, 11, 18, 10, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc), 330);
            return new BillingService(new BillingRepository(Database), null, effectiveClock, checkpoint);
        }

        public void SeedDefaultShop(string stateCode = "19", string invoicePrefix = "INV", int fyStartMonth = 4)
        {
            string now = "2026-11-18T10:00:00.0000000Z";
            ExecuteNonQuery(@"
INSERT INTO shop_profile(
    profile_id, shop_id, shop_name, state_name, state_code, gstin,
    invoice_prefix, financial_year_start_month, created_at_utc, updated_at_utc)
VALUES(1, 'shop-001', 'ORVA Test Pharmacy', 'West Bengal', @state, '19ABCDE1234F1Z5', @prefix, @fy_month, @now, @now);",
                p =>
                {
                    p.AddWithValue("@state", (object)stateCode ?? DBNull.Value);
                    p.AddWithValue("@prefix", invoicePrefix);
                    p.AddWithValue("@fy_month", fyStartMonth);
                    p.AddWithValue("@now", now);
                });
        }

        public void SeedTaxConfig(
            bool configured = true,
            string pricingMode = "INCLUSIVE",
            string defaultSupplyType = "INTRA_STATE",
            string roundOffMode = "NONE")
        {
            SetSetting(BillingSettingKeys.TaxConfigured, configured ? "1" : "0");
            if (pricingMode != null) SetSetting(BillingSettingKeys.TaxPricingMode, pricingMode);
            if (defaultSupplyType != null) SetSetting(BillingSettingKeys.DefaultSupplyType, defaultSupplyType);
            if (roundOffMode != null) SetSetting(BillingSettingKeys.RoundOffMode, roundOffMode);
        }

        public void SetSetting(string key, string value)
        {
            string now = "2026-11-18T10:00:00.0000000Z";
            ExecuteNonQuery(@"
INSERT INTO app_setting(setting_key, setting_value, updated_at_utc)
VALUES(@key, @value, @now)
ON CONFLICT(setting_key) DO UPDATE SET setting_value = excluded.setting_value, updated_at_utc = excluded.updated_at_utc;",
                p =>
                {
                    p.AddWithValue("@key", key);
                    p.AddWithValue("@value", value);
                    p.AddWithValue("@now", now);
                });
        }

        public string GetSetting(string key)
        {
            using (var conn = Database.OpenConnection())
            using (var cmd = new SQLiteCommand("SELECT setting_value FROM app_setting WHERE setting_key = @key;", conn))
            {
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@key", key);
                var result = cmd.ExecuteScalar();
                return result == null || result == DBNull.Value ? null : Convert.ToString(result, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
            }
        }

        public void SeedMedicine(
            string medicineId,
            string displayName,
            string stockUnitName = "strip",
            int gstRateBasisPoints = 1200,
            bool gstRateConfigured = true,
            string hsnCode = "30049099",
            long lowStockThresholdMilli = 0,
            bool isActive = true)
        {
            string now = "2026-11-18T10:00:00.0000000Z";
            ExecuteNonQuery(@"
INSERT INTO medicine(
    medicine_id, display_name, normalized_name, stock_unit_name, hsn_code,
    gst_rate_basis_points, gst_rate_configured, low_stock_threshold_milli,
    is_active, created_at_utc, updated_at_utc)
VALUES(@id, @name, @norm, @unit, @hsn, @gst, @gst_cfg, @low, @active, @now, @now);",
                p =>
                {
                    p.AddWithValue("@id", medicineId);
                    p.AddWithValue("@name", displayName);
                    p.AddWithValue("@norm", displayName.Trim().ToUpperInvariant());
                    p.AddWithValue("@unit", stockUnitName);
                    p.AddWithValue("@hsn", (object)hsnCode ?? DBNull.Value);
                    p.AddWithValue("@gst", gstRateBasisPoints);
                    p.AddWithValue("@gst_cfg", gstRateConfigured ? 1 : 0);
                    p.AddWithValue("@low", lowStockThresholdMilli);
                    p.AddWithValue("@active", isActive ? 1 : 0);
                    p.AddWithValue("@now", now);
                });
        }

        public void SeedBarcode(string barcode, string medicineId)
        {
            ExecuteNonQuery(
                "INSERT INTO barcode_mapping(barcode, medicine_id, created_at_utc) VALUES(@bc, @med, '2026-11-18T10:00:00.0000000Z');",
                p =>
                {
                    p.AddWithValue("@bc", barcode);
                    p.AddWithValue("@med", medicineId);
                });
        }

        public void SeedBatch(
            string batchId,
            string medicineId,
            string batchNumber,
            int expiryYyyyMm,
            long mrpPaise,
            long quantityMilli,
            string status = "ACTIVE",
            string stockUnitName = "strip")
        {
            string now = "2026-11-18T10:00:00.0000000Z";
            ExecuteNonQuery(@"
INSERT INTO stock_batch(
    batch_id, medicine_id, batch_number, expiry_yyyymm, stock_unit_name,
    mrp_paise, latest_purchase_price_paise, quantity_milli, batch_status,
    created_at_utc, updated_at_utc)
VALUES(@id, @med, @num, @exp, @unit, @mrp, @mrp, @qty, @status, @now, @now);",
                p =>
                {
                    p.AddWithValue("@id", batchId);
                    p.AddWithValue("@med", medicineId);
                    p.AddWithValue("@num", batchNumber);
                    p.AddWithValue("@exp", expiryYyyyMm);
                    p.AddWithValue("@unit", stockUnitName);
                    p.AddWithValue("@mrp", mrpPaise);
                    p.AddWithValue("@qty", quantityMilli);
                    p.AddWithValue("@status", status);
                    p.AddWithValue("@now", now);
                });
        }

        public long QueryScalarInt64(string sql, Action<SQLiteParameterCollection> bind = null)
        {
            using (var conn = Database.OpenConnection())
            using (var cmd = new SQLiteCommand(sql, conn))
            {
                if (bind != null) bind(cmd.Parameters);
                return Convert.ToInt64(cmd.ExecuteScalar(), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
            }
        }

        public string QueryScalarString(string sql, Action<SQLiteParameterCollection> bind = null)
        {
            using (var conn = Database.OpenConnection())
            using (var cmd = new SQLiteCommand(sql, conn))
            {
                if (bind != null) bind(cmd.Parameters);
                var val = cmd.ExecuteScalar();
                return val == null || val == DBNull.Value ? null : Convert.ToString(val, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
            }
        }

        public void ExecuteNonQuery(string sql, Action<SQLiteParameterCollection> bind = null)
        {
            using (var conn = Database.OpenConnection())
            using (var cmd = new SQLiteCommand(sql, conn))
            {
                if (bind != null) bind(cmd.Parameters);
                cmd.ExecuteNonQuery();
            }
        }

        public void Dispose()
        {
            SQLiteConnection.ClearAllPools();
            try
            {
                if (Directory.Exists(_tempDirectory))
                {
                    Directory.Delete(_tempDirectory, true);
                }
            }
            catch
            {
                // Best-effort cleanup of temp SQLite directory on Windows.
            }
        }
    }
}

================================================================================
FILE: ORVA/tests/Orva.Billing.Tests/InvoiceCalculatorTests.cs
================================================================================
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Orva.Services.Billing;
using Xunit;

namespace Orva.Billing.Tests
{
    public sealed class InvoiceCalculatorTests
    {
        [Fact]
        public void ExclusiveTax_IntraState_ReconcilesExactly()
        {
            var calc = new InvoiceCalculator();
            var medicine = new BillingMedicineRecord
            {
                MedicineId = "m1",
                DisplayName = "Paracetamol 500mg",
                StockUnitName = "strip",
                GstRateBasisPoints = 1200,
                GstRateConfigured = true
            };
            var input = new InvoiceLineCalculationInput
            {
                Medicine = medicine,
                RequestedQuantityMilli = 2000, // 2 strips
                Allocations = new List&lt;FefoBatchAllocation&gt;
                {
                    new FefoBatchAllocation
                    {
                        BatchId = "b1",
                        BatchNumber = "B1",
                        ExpiryYyyyMm = 202812,
                        QuantityMilli = 2000,
                        UnitPricePaise = 2500, // Rs 25.00
                        StockUnitName = "strip",
                        PreviousQuantityMilli = 10000
                    }
                }
            };

            var policy = new ResolvedTaxPolicy
            {
                PricingMode = TaxPricingMode.TaxExclusive,
                SupplyType = SupplyType.IntraState,
                RoundOffMode = RoundOffMode.None
            };

            var invoice = calc.Calculate(new List<InvoiceLineCalculationInput> { input }, policy);

            Assert.Equal(5000, invoice.SubtotalPaise);
            Assert.Equal(0, invoice.DiscountPaise);
            Assert.Equal(5000, invoice.TaxableValuePaise);
            Assert.Equal(300, invoice.CgstPaise);
            Assert.Equal(300, invoice.SgstPaise);
            Assert.Equal(0, invoice.IgstPaise);
            Assert.Equal(5600, invoice.GrandTotalPaise);
            Assert.Equal(invoice.GrandTotalPaise, invoice.TaxableValuePaise + invoice.CgstPaise + invoice.SgstPaise + invoice.IgstPaise + invoice.RoundOffPaise);
        }

        [Fact]
        public void InclusiveTax_InterState_ReconcilesExactly()
        {
            var calc = new InvoiceCalculator();
            var medicine = new BillingMedicineRecord
            {
                MedicineId = "m1",
                DisplayName = "Amoxicillin 500mg",
                StockUnitName = "strip",
                GstRateBasisPoints = 1200,
                GstRateConfigured = true
            };
            var input = new InvoiceLineCalculationInput
            {
                Medicine = medicine,
                RequestedQuantityMilli = 1000,
                Allocations = new List<FefoBatchAllocation>
                {
                    new FefoBatchAllocation
                    {
                        BatchId = "b1",
                        BatchNumber = "B1",
                        ExpiryYyyyMm = 202812,
                        QuantityMilli = 1000,
                        UnitPricePaise = 11200, // Rs 112.00 MRP inclusive of 12% GST
                        StockUnitName = "strip",
                        PreviousQuantityMilli = 5000
                    }
                }
            };

            var policy = new ResolvedTaxPolicy
            {
                PricingMode = TaxPricingMode.TaxInclusive,
                SupplyType = SupplyType.InterState,
                RoundOffMode = RoundOffMode.None
            };

            var invoice = calc.Calculate(new List<InvoiceLineCalculationInput> { input }, policy);

            Assert.Equal(11200, invoice.SubtotalPaise);
            Assert.Equal(10000, invoice.TaxableValuePaise);
            Assert.Equal(0, invoice.CgstPaise);
            Assert.Equal(0, invoice.SgstPaise);
            Assert.Equal(1200, invoice.IgstPaise);
            Assert.Equal(11200, invoice.GrandTotalPaise);
        }

        [Fact]
        public void OddPaiseTax_IntraState_BalancesCgstAndSgstDeterministically()
        {
            var calc = new InvoiceCalculator();
            var medicine = new BillingMedicineRecord
            {
                MedicineId = "m1",
                DisplayName = "Test Med",
                StockUnitName = "tablet",
                GstRateBasisPoints = 500, // 5%
                GstRateConfigured = true
            };

            // 5% of 100 paise = 5 paise (odd). Two such lines => 5 + 5 = 10 paise total -> CGST 5, SGST 5.
            var inputs = new List<InvoiceLineCalculationInput>
            {
                new InvoiceLineCalculationInput
                {
                    Medicine = medicine,
                    RequestedQuantityMilli = 1000,
                    Allocations = new List<FefoBatchAllocation>
                    {
                        new FefoBatchAllocation { BatchId = "b1", BatchNumber = "B1", ExpiryYyyyMm = 202812, QuantityMilli = 1000, UnitPricePaise = 100, StockUnitName = "tablet", PreviousQuantityMilli = 5000 }
                    }
                },
                new InvoiceLineCalculationInput
                {
                    Medicine = medicine,
                    RequestedQuantityMilli = 1000,
                    Allocations = new List<FefoBatchAllocation>
                    {
                        new FefoBatchAllocation { BatchId = "b2", BatchNumber = "B2", ExpiryYyyyMm = 202812, QuantityMilli = 1000, UnitPricePaise = 100, StockUnitName = "tablet", PreviousQuantityMilli = 5000 }
                    }
                }
            };

            var policy = new ResolvedTaxPolicy
            {
                PricingMode = TaxPricingMode.TaxExclusive,
                SupplyType = SupplyType.IntraState,
                RoundOffMode = RoundOffMode.None
            };

            var invoice = calc.Calculate(inputs, policy);

            Assert.Equal(5, invoice.CgstPaise);
            Assert.Equal(5, invoice.SgstPaise);
            Assert.Equal(210, invoice.GrandTotalPaise);
        }

        [Fact]
        public void FinancialYearLabel_HandlesAprilBoundaryCorrectly()
        {
            Assert.Equal("25-26", InvoiceCalculator.GetFinancialYearLabel(new DateTime(2026, 3, 31), 4));
            Assert.Equal("26-27", InvoiceCalculator.GetFinancialYearLabel(new DateTime(2026, 4, 1), 4));
            Assert.Equal("2026", InvoiceCalculator.GetFinancialYearLabel(new DateTime(2026, 1, 15), 1));
        }

        [Fact]
        public void NearestRupeeRoundOff_ReconcilesGrandTotal()
        {
            Assert.Equal(10000, InvoiceCalculator.ApplyRoundOff(10049, RoundOffMode.NearestRupee));
            Assert.Equal(10100, InvoiceCalculator.ApplyRoundOff(10050, RoundOffMode.NearestRupee));
        }
    }
}

# ORVA AI Development Assignments v2

## Agreed division of work

- **Gemini 4 Argon:** Billing backend, ledger/money/tax arithmetic, FEFO allocation, transactional SQLite persistence, invoice sequencing and difficult edge-case tests.
- **Qwen 4 Max:** Native WPF billing workspace, interactive cart/dynamic table, customer/payment interface and live on-screen invoice preview for 58mm, 80mm, A4 and A5.
- **ChatGPT:** Shared contracts, routing/integration, AppServices and MainWindow wiring, review, project registration, merge conflict resolution and final verification.

Qwen's general strength in web UI is adapted to ORVA's real platform: WPF desktop XAML and C#, not React/HTML/web code. Its “live preview” assignment is the on-screen WPF invoice paper preview.

## Files to give each model

Give **the same source ZIP** to both models:
`ORVA_Source_Package_Billing_v2.zip`

Then give Gemini:
`ORVA_GEMINI_TASK_PROMPT_v2.txt`

Give Qwen:
`ORVA_QWEN_TASK_PROMPT_v2.txt`

The source ZIP includes the frozen contract:
- `ORVA/src/Orva.Desktop/Domain/Billing/BillingContracts.cs`
- `ORVA/docs/BILLING_UI_BACKEND_CONTRACT_v1.md`

## Strict ownership boundary

Gemini must not edit WPF views, MainWindow or AppServices. Qwen must not edit the schema, repositories, billing backend or AppServices/MainWindow. Neither may modify `BillingContracts.cs`. ChatGPT will integrate both outputs.

## Deferred work

Qwen's earlier portable backup/restore assignment is deferred. It should not be run concurrently with the billing UI task. We will assign it after the billing backend and UI merge, using the updated database schema.

## Important

The source is an early permanent WPF foundation. Do not claim a Windows build, test, live preview or hardware validation unless it was actually performed. The actual owner/staff usability constraint is mandatory for every contribution.

================================================================================
FILE: ORVA_CHATGPT_INTEGRATION_PLAN.md
================================================================================
# ORVA — ChatGPT Integration Ownership

ChatGPT is the architecture/integration owner, not a competing implementation branch.

## Responsibilities retained by ChatGPT

1. Maintain product requirements and the single authoritative `AI_COLLABORATION_CONTEXT.md`.
2. Freeze and maintain the public backend/UI contract in `BillingContracts.cs` and its documentation.
3. Keep Gemini and Qwen file ownership disjoint.
4. Review their ZIPs against the real current source tree and reject broad or unrelated rewrites.
5. Integrate `BillingService` from Gemini into `AppServices` and connect Qwen's `BillingView` to `MainWindow`.
6. Resolve project-file, resource and namespace registration after both branches return.
7. Integrate portable stock import and full backup/restore after billing/UI contract is stable.
8. Integrate invoice printing, barcode scanner input, subscription registry and activation workflow in separate controlled tasks.
9. Run static/schema checks available in the current environment; arrange Windows build/test verification and report limitations honestly.
10. Preserve the core requirement: ORVA must suit the real owner and staff described. Every interaction must minimise typing, decisions, technical concepts and customer waiting.

## File ownership

| Area | Owner |
|---|---|
| Billing DTO/interface contract | ChatGPT (frozen boundary) |
| Billing service, calculator, FEFO, persistence, tests | Gemini |
| New Billing WPF view, view-model, live paper preview | Qwen |
| `AppServices.cs`, `MainWindow.xaml`, `MainWindow.xaml.cs` integration | ChatGPT |
| Database migrations integration | ChatGPT after Gemini review |
| Printer integration | Later ChatGPT-controlled task |
| Backup/restore and stock import | Deferred separate Qwen task after billing merge |
| Subscription/GitHub licensing | Deferred separate task |

## Merge protocol

- Both models receive the same source ZIP and exact `BillingContracts.cs`.
- They must not modify each other's owned files.
- Each returns changed files only, preserving paths under `ORVA/`, plus an implementation report.
- ChatGPT compares each changed file against the baseline and contract before merging.
- Never accept a claim of successful Windows compilation unless the model supplies actual build/test evidence.
